yamhā sattamyatthai hiṃ hauti|

yahiṃ|

283. tā hañ‍ca ka॰ 253; rū॰ 273; nī॰ 501|

tamhā sattamyatthai hiṃ hauti hañ‍ca|

tahiṃ, tahaṃ| dutiyatthaipi dissati ‘‘tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintau gacchatī’’ti|

284. kiṃssa kukañ‍ca ka॰ 251, 227-8-9; rū॰ 226, 270-1-2; nī॰ 500, 456-7, 460|

kiṃmhā sattamyatthai hiṃ, taṃ hauti| kiṃssa kuttaṃ kattañ‍ca hauti|

kuhiṃ gacchati, kuhaṃ gacchati| kahaṃ aikaputtaka kahaṃ aikaputtaka saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.63| kuhiñ‍ci, kuhiñ‍cananti dvai ci, cana-nipātantā sijjhanti|

iti sāmañ‍ñasattamyantarāsi|

kālasattamyantaṃ  vuc‍catai|

285. sabbaikañ‍ñayataihi kālaidā ka॰ 257; rū॰ 276; nī॰ 505|

sabba, aika, añ‍ña, ya, tasaddaihi kālai dā hauti|

sabbasmiṃ kālai sabbadā, aikasmiṃ kālai aikadā, añ‍ñasmiṃ kālai añ‍ñadā, yasmiṃ kālai yadā, tasmiṃ kālai tadā|

286. kadākudāsadāadhunaidāni ka॰ 257-8-9; rū॰ 276-8-9; nī॰ 505-6-7|

aitaipi sattamyatthai kālai dā, dhunā, dānipac‍cayantā sijjhanti|

kiṃsmiṃ kālai kadā, kudā, sabbasmiṃ kālai sadā, imasmiṃ kālai adhunā, idāni|

287. aj‍jasaj‍juparaj‍jaitarahikarahā ka॰ 259; rū॰ 279, 423; nī॰ 507|

aitaipi kālai j‍ja, j‍ju, rahi, raha pac‍cayantā sijjhanti|

imasmiṃ kālai aj‍ja, imasmiṃ divasaityatthau|

samānai kālai saj‍ju-‘samānai’ti vij‍jamānai| na hi pāpaṃ kataṃ kammaṃ, saj‍ju khīraṃva muc‍cati dha॰ pa॰ 71, saj‍jukaṃ pāhaisi – tattha ‘saj‍jū’ti tasmiṃ divasai|

aparasmiṃ kālai aparaj‍ju, punadivasaiti atthau|

imasmiṃ kālai aitarahi, kiṃsmiṃ kālai karaha| kutauci, k‍vaci, katthaci, kuhiñ‍ci, kadāci, karahacisaddā pana ci-nipātantā haunti, tathā yatau kutauci, yattha katthaci, yadā kadācīti| kiñ‍canaṃ, kuhiñ‍canaṃ, kudācananti cana-nipātantāti|

vibhattipac‍cayantarāsi niṭṭhitau|

abyayapadāni

upasaggapadarāsi

atha  abyayapadāni dīpiyantai|

chabbidhāni abyayapadāni upasaggapadaṃ, nipātapadaṃ, vibhattipac‍cayantapadaṃ, abyayībhāvasamāsapadaṃ, abyayataddhitapadaṃ, tvādipac‍cayantapadanti| byayau vuc‍cati vikārau, nānāliṅgavibhattivacanaihi natthi rūpabyayau aitaisanti abyayā, asaṅkhyāti ca vuc‍canti|

tattha vibhattipac‍cayantapadatau puna vibhattuppatti nāma natthi| abyayībhāvasamāsamhi vibhattīnaṃ vidhi samāsakaṇḍai vakkhati, tasmā tāni dvai ṭhapaitvā saisāni cattāri idha vuc‍cantai|

288. asaṅkhyaihi sabbāsaṃ caṃ॰ 2.1.38; pā॰ 2.4.82|

asaṅkhyaihi padaihi yathārahaṃ sabbāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupau hauti, kaihici padaihi paṭhamāya laupau, kaihici padaihi dutiyāya laupau…pai॰… kaihici sattamiyā, kaihici dvin‍naṃ, kaihici tissan‍naṃ…pai॰… kaihici sattan‍nanti vuttaṃ hauti|

tattha āvusau, bhau, bhantaiic‍cādīhi āmantananipātaihi atthi, natthi, sak‍kā, labbhā, siyā, siyuṃ, sādhu, tuṇhīic‍cādīhi ca paṭhamāya laupau|

ciraṃ, cirassaṃ, nic‍caṃ, satataṃ, abhiṇhaṃ, abhikkhaṇaṃ, muhuttaṃ ic‍cādīhi ac‍cantasaṃyaugalakkhaṇai dutiyāya|

yathā, tathā, sabbathā, sabbasau, musā, micchāic‍cādīhi tatiyāya|

kātuṃ, kātavai ic‍cādīhi catutthiyā|

samantā, samantatau, dīghasau, aurasauic‍cādīhi pañ‍camiyā|

purai, purā, pacchā, uddhaṃ, upari, adhau, haiṭṭhā, antarā, antau, rahau, āvi, hiyyau, suvaiic‍cādīhi sattamiyā laupau|

namausaddamhā ‘‘namau tai buddha vīra’tthū’’ti aittha paṭhamāya| ‘‘namau karauhi nāgassā’’ti aittha dutiyāya|

sayaṃsaddamhā ‘‘kusūlau sayamaiva bhij‍jatai’’ti aittha paṭhamāya| ‘‘sayaṃ kataṃ sukhadukkha’’nti dī॰ ni॰ 3.191, 193 aittha tatiyāya, ic‍cādinā yathārahavibhāgau vaiditabbau|

iti, aivaṃsaddaihi payaugānurūpaṃ sattan‍naṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupaṃ icchanti|

upasaggaihipi atthānurūpaṃ taṃtaṃvibhattilaupau|

rūpasiddhiyaṃ pana ‘‘taihi paṭhamaikavacanamaiva bhavatī’’ti rū॰ 131 (piṭṭhai) vuttaṃ|

tattha ‘‘abhik‍kamati, abhidhammau’’ ic‍cādīsu dhātuliṅgāni upaic‍ca taisaṃ atthaṃ nānāppakāraṃ karauntā saj‍janti saṅkharauntīti upasaggā| tai hi k‍vaci tadatthaṃ visiṭṭhaṃ karaunti ‘‘jānāti, pajānāti, sañ‍jānāti, avajānāti, abhijānāti, parijānāti, susīlau, dussīlau, suvaṇṇau, dubbaṇṇau, surājā, durājā’’ ic‍cādīsu|

k‍vaci tadatthaṃ nānāppakāraṃ katvā vibhaj‍janti ‘‘gacchati, āgacchati, uggacchati, augacchati’’ic‍cādīsu|

k‍vaci tadatthaṃ bādhaitvā tappaṭiviruddhai vā tadañ‍ñasmiṃ vā atthai tāni yaujainti|

tattha  tappaṭiviruddhai –

jaiti, parājaiti, aumuñ‍cati, paṭimuñ‍cati, gilati, uggilati, nimmuj‍jati, ummuj‍jati, dhammau, uddhammauic‍cādi|

tadañ‍ñasmiṃ –

dadāti, ādadāti, dadhāti, vidhaiti, pidhaiti, nidhaiti, sandhiyati, saddahati, abhidhātiic‍cādi|

k‍vaci pana padasaubhaṇaṃ katvā tadatthaṃ anuvattanti, ‘‘vij‍jati, saṃvij‍jati, labhati, paṭilabhati’’ ic‍cādi|

tai vīsati haunti-pa, ā, u, au, du, ni, vi, su, saṃ, ati, adhi, anu, apa, api, abhi, ava, upa, pati, parā, pari|

kac‍cāyanai pana ausaddau avakāriyamattanti taṃ aggahaitvā nīsaddaṃ gaṇhāti, idha pana nīsaddau nissa dīghamattanti taṃ aggahaitvā ausaddaṃ gaṇhāti|

tattha pa –

pakāratthai-pañ‍ñā| ādikammai-vippakataṃ| padhānai-paṇītaṃ| issariyai-pabhū| antaubhāvai-pakkhittaṃ, passāsau| viyaugai-pavāsau| tapparai-pācariyau| tadanubandhai-puttau, paputtau, nattā, panattā| bhusatthai-pavaḍḍhau| sambhavai-pabhavati| tittiyaṃ-pahutaṃ an‍naṃ| anāvilai-pasan‍nau| patthanāyaṃ-paṇidhānaṃ|

ā –

abhimukhai-āgacchati| uddhaṃkammai-ārauhati| mariyādāyaṃ-āpabbatā khaittaṃ| abhividhimhi-ābrahmalaukā kittisaddau| pattiyaṃ-āpan‍nau| icchāyaṃ-ākaṅkhā| parissajanai-āliṅgati| ādikammai-ārambhau| gahaṇai-ādīyati| nivāsai-āvasathau| samīpai-āsan‍naṃ| avhānai-āmantanaṃ|

u –

uggatai-uggacchati| uddhaṃkammai-uṭṭhāti| padhānai-uttarau| viyaugai-upavāsau| sambhavai-ubbhūtau| atthalābhai-rūpassa uppādau| sattiyaṃ-ussahati gantuṃ| sarūpakhyānai-uddaisau|

au –

antaubhāvai-aucarakau, auraudhau| adhaukammai-aukkhittau| niggahai-auvādau| antarai, daisai ca-aukāsau| pātubhāvai-aupapātikau| yaisu atthaisu avasaddau vattati, taisupi ausaddau vattati|

du –

asaubhaṇai-duggandhau| abhāvai-dubbhikkhaṃ, dussīlau, duppañ‍ñau| kucchitai-duk‍kaṭaṃ| asamiddhiyaṃ-dusassaṃ| kicchai-duk‍karaṃ| virūpai-dubbaṇṇau, dummukhau|

ni –

nissaisai-nirutti| niggatai-niyyānaṃ| nīharaṇai-niddhāraṇaṃ| antaupavaisanai-nikhātau| abhāvai-nimmakkhikaṃ| nisaidhai-nivāraiti| nikkhantai-nibbānaṃ| pātubhāvai-nimmitaṃ| avadhāraṇai-vinicchayau| vibhaj‍janai-niddaisau| upamāyaṃ-nidassanaṃ| upadhāraṇai-nisāmaiti| avasānai-niṭṭhitaṃ| chaikai-nipuṇau|

vi –

visaisai-vipassati| vividhai-vicittaṃ| viruddhai-vivādau| vigatai-vimalau| viyaugai-vippayuttau| virūpai-vippaṭisārau|

su –

saubhaṇai-suggati| sundarai-sumanau| sammāsaddatthai-sugatau| samiddhiyaṃ-subhikkhaṃ| sukhatthai-sukarau|

saṃ –

samaudhānai-sandhi| sammā, samatthaisu-samādhi, sampayuttau| samantabhāvai-saṃkiṇṇau| saṅgatai-samāgamau, saṅkhaipai-samāsau| bhusatthai-sārattau| sahatthai-saṃvāsau, sambhaugau| appatthai-samagghau| pabhavai-sambhavau| abhimukhai-sammukhaṃ| saṅgahai-saṅgayhati| pidahanai-saṃvutau| punappunakammai-sandhāvati, saṃsarati| samiddhiyaṃ-sampan‍nau|

ati –

atik‍kamai-atiraucati, ac‍cayau, atītau| atik‍kantai-ac‍cantaṃ| atissayai-atikusalau| bhusatthai-atikaudhau| antaukammai-mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā atiharitvā ṭhapaiti|

adhi –

adhikai-adhisīlaṃ| issarai-adhipati, adhibrahmadattai pañ‍cālā| uparibhāvai-adhisaiti| paribhavanai-adhibhūtau| ajjhāyanai-ajjhaiti, byākaraṇamadhītai| adhiṭṭhānai-navakammaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, cīvaraṃ adhiṭṭhāti, iddhivikubbanaṃ adhiṭṭhāti| nicchayai-adhimuc‍cati| pāpuṇanai-bhaugakkhandhaṃ adhigacchati, amataṃ adhigacchati|

anu –

anugatai-anvaiti| anuppacchin‍nai-anusayau| pacchāsaddatthai-anurathaṃ| punappunabhāvai-anvaḍḍhamāsaṃ, anusaṃvaccharaṃ| yaugyabhāvai-anurūpaṃ|kaniṭṭhabhāvai-anubuddhau, anuthairau| saisaṃ kārakakaṇḍai vakkhati|

apa –

apagatai-apaiti, apāyau| garahai-apagabbhau, apasaddau| vaj‍janai-apasālāya āyanti| pūjāyaṃ-vuḍḍha-mapacāyanti| padussanai-aparajjhati|

api –

sambhāvanai-apipabbataṃ bhindaiyya, mairumpi vinivijjhaiyya| apaikkhāyaṃ-ayampi dhammau aniyatau| samuc‍cayai-itipi arahaṃ, chavimpi dahati, cammampi dahati, maṃsampi dahati| garahāyaṃ-api amhākaṃ paṇḍitaka| pucchāyaṃ-api bhantai bhikkhaṃ labhittha, api nu tumhai sautukāmāttha|

abhi –

abhimukhai-abhik‍kantau| visiṭṭhai-abhiñ‍ñā| adhikai-abhidhammau| uddhaṃkammai-abhirūhati| kulai-abhijātau| sāruppai-abhirūpau| vandanai-abhivādaiti| saisaṃ kārakakaṇḍai vakkhati|

ava –

adhaubhāgai-avakkhittau| viyaugai-avakaukilaṃ vanaṃ| paribhavai-avajānāti| jānanai-avagacchati| suddhiyaṃ-vaudāyati, vaudānaṃ| nicchayai-avadhāraṇaṃ| daisai-avakāsau| thaiyyai-avahārau|

upa –

upagamai-upanisīdati| samīpai-upacārau, upanagaraṃ| upapattiyaṃ-saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| sadisai-upamāṇaṃ, upamaiyyaṃ| adhikai-upakhāriyaṃ dauṇau| uparibhāvai – upasampan‍nau, upacayau| anasanai-upavāsau| dausakkhānai-paraṃ upavadati| sañ‍ñāyaṃ-upadhā, upasaggau| pubbakammai-upak‍kamau, upahārau| pūjāyaṃ-buddhaṃ upaṭṭhāti| gayhākārai-pac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ| bhusatthai-upādānaṃ, upāyāsau, upanissayau|

pati –

patigatai-pac‍cakkhaṃ| paṭilaumai-paṭisautaṃ| paṭiyaugimhi-paṭipuggalau| nisaidhai-paṭisaidhau| nivattai-paṭik‍kamati| sadisai-paṭirūpakaṃ| paṭikammai-raugassa paṭikārau| ādānai-paṭiggaṇhāti| paṭibaudhai-paṭivaidhau| paṭic‍cai-pac‍cayau| saisaṃ kārakakaṇḍai vakkhati|

parā –

parihāniyaṃ-parābhavau| parājayai-parājitau| gatiyaṃ-parāyanaṃ| vik‍kamai-parak‍kamau| āmasanai-parāmasanaṃ|

pari –

samantabhāvai-parivutau, parikkhittau, parikkhārau| paricchaidai-pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, parijānāti| vaj‍janai-pariharati| parihārau| āliṅganai-parissajati| nivāsanai-vatthaṃ paridahati| pūjāyaṃ-pāricariyā| bhaujanai-parivisati| abhibhavai-paribhavati| dausakkhānai-paribhāsati| saisaṃ kārakakaṇḍai vakkhati|

nīsaddau pana nīharaṇa, nīvaraṇādīsu vattati, nīharaṇaṃ, nīvaraṇaṃic‍cādi|

iti upasaggapadarāsi|

nipātapadarāsi

nic‍caṃ  aikarūpaina vākyapathai patantīti nipātā| padānaṃ ādi, majjhā’vasānaisu nipatantīti nipātātipi vadanti|

asatvavācakā cādisaddā nipātā nāma| tai pana vibhattiyuttā, ayuttā cāti duvidhā haunti| tattha vibhattiyuttā pubbai dassitā aiva| cādayau ayuttā nāma| tai pana anaikasatappabhaidā haunti| nighaṇṭusatthaisu gahaitabbāti|

abyayataddhitapac‍cayapadarāsi

abyayataddhitapac‍cayantā nāma yathā, tathā, aikadhā, aikajjhaṃ, sabbasau, kathaṃ, itthaṃ ic‍cādayau| taihi tatiyālaupau|

tvādipac‍cayantapadarāsi

tvādipac‍cayantā nāma katvā, katvāna, kātuna, kātuṃ, kātavai, dakkhitāyai, haituyai, ādāya, upādāya, vicaiyya, vinaiyya, sak‍kac‍ca, āhac‍ca, upasampaj‍ja, samaic‍ca, avaic‍ca, paṭic‍ca, atic‍ca, āgamma, ārabbhaic‍cādayau| taisu tvā, tvānantaihi paṭhamālaupau| tuṃ, tavai, tāyai, tuyaipac‍cayantaihi catutthīlaupauti|

dhātavau pac‍cayā caiva, upasagganipātakā|

anaikatthāva tai paṭi-sambhidā ñāṇagaucarā|

iti niruttidīpaniyā nāma mauggal‍lānadīpaniyā

nāmakaṇḍau niṭṭhitau|

3. kārakakaṇḍa

paṭhamāvibhattirāsi

atha nāmavibhattīnaṃ atthabhaidā vuc‍cantai|

kasmiṃ atthai paṭhamā?

289. paṭhamatthamattai caṃ॰ 2.1.93; pā॰ 2.3.46|

nāmassa abhidhaiyyamattai paṭhamāvibhatti hauti|

rukkhau, mālā, dhanaṃ|

aittha ca mattasaddaina kattu, kammādikai vibhatyatthai nivattaiti| tasmā atthamattanti liṅgatthauyaiva vuc‍cati|

tattha anuc‍cāritai sati suṇantassa aviditau atthau līnatthau nāma| taṃ līnamatthaṃ gamaiti baudhaitīti liṅgaṃ, uc‍cāritapadaṃ|

taṃ pana pakatiliṅgaṃ, nipphan‍naliṅganti duvidhaṃ| tattha vibhattirahitaṃ pakatiliṅgaṃ idhādhippaitaṃ liṅga, vibhattīnaṃ visuṃ visuṃ vibhāgaṭṭhānattā|

līnaṃ aṅganti liṅgaṃ| tattha ‘līna’nti apākaṭaṃ| ‘aṅga’nti avayavau| liṅgaṃ, nāmaṃ, pāṭipadikanti atthatau aikaṃ|

liṅgassa atthau paramatthau, pañ‍ñattiatthauti duvidhau| tathā visaisanatthau, visaisyatthauti|

tattha visaisanatthau nāma sakatthau, tassa tassa saddassa paṭiniyatau pāṭipuggalikatthauti vuttaṃ hauti| sauyaiva tasmiṃ tasmiṃ atthai ādimhi sadduppattiyā cirakālañ‍ca saddapavattiyā nibaddhakāraṇattā nimittatthauti ca vuc‍cati| sau suti, jāti, guṇa, dabba, kriyā, nāma, sambandhavasaina sattavidhau hauti|

visaisyatthau nāma sāmañ‍ñatthau, bahunimittānaṃ sādhāraṇatthauti vuttaṃ hauti, sauyaiva taṃtaṃnimittayaugā naimittakatthauti ca vuc‍cati, sau jāti, guṇa, dabba, kriyā, nāmavasaina pañ‍cavidhau| gau, suk‍kau, daṇḍī, pācakau, tissauti|

tattha gausaddau yadā jātimattaṃ vadati gau jātīti, tadā suti visaisanaṃ| yadā dabbaṃ vadati gau gacchatīti, tadā suti ca jāti ca visaisanaṃ|

suk‍kasaddau yadā guṇamattaṃ vadati suk‍kau guṇauti, tadā suti visaisanaṃ| yadā guṇavisaisaṃ vadati gaussa suk‍kauti, tadā suti ca guṇajāti ca visaisanaṃ| yadā guṇavantaṃ dabbaṃ vadati suk‍kau gauti, tadā suti ca guṇajāti ca guṇavisaisau ca sambandhau ca visaisanaṃ|

daṇḍīsaddau yadā jātimattaṃ vadati daṇḍī jātīti, tadā suti ca dabbañ‍ca visaisanaṃ| yadā dabbavantaṃ dabbaṃ vadati daṇḍī purisauti, tadā suti ca dabbañ‍ca jāti ca sambandhau ca visaisanaṃ|

pācakasaddau yadā jātimattaṃ vadati pācakau jātīti, tadā suti ca kriyā ca visaisanaṃ| yadā kriyānipphādakaṃ dabbaṃ vadati pācakau purisauti, tadā suti ca kriyā ca jāti ca kriyākārakasambandhau ca visaisanaṃ|

tissasaddau yadā nāmamattaṃ vadati tissau nāmanti, tadā suti visaisanaṃ| yadā nāmavantaṃ dabbaṃ vadati, tissau bhikkhūti, tadā suti ca nāmajāti ca sambandhau ca visaisanaṃ|

sabbattha yaṃ yaṃ vadatīti vuttaṃ, taṃ taṃ visaisyanti ca dabbanti ca vaiditabbaṃ| aittha ca suti nāma saddasabhāvā aiva hauti, saddapakkhikā  aiva| sabbau saddau paṭhamaṃ sattābhidhāyakauti ca ñāsai vuttaṃ| tasmā sabbattha sutiṭṭhānai sattā aiva yuttā vattunti| sattāti ca tassa tassa atthassa vauhāramattainapi laukai vij‍jamānatā vuc‍cati, taṃ taṃ saddaṃ suṇantassa ca ñāṇaṃ taṃtadatthassa atthitāmattaṃ sabbapaṭhamaṃ jānāti, tatau paraṃ jātisaddai jātiṃ jānāti| guṇasaddai guṇanti aivamādi sabbaṃ vattabbaṃ| liṅga, saṅkhyā, parimāṇānipi visaisanatthai saṅgayhanti|

tattha liṅgaṃ nāma yai itthi, purisānaṃ liṅga, nimitta, kuttā’kappā nāma abhidhammai vuttā, yai ca napuṃsakānaṃ liṅga, nimitta, kuttā’kappā nāma avuttasiddhā, yai ca saddaisu caiva atthaisu ca visadā’visadākāra, majjhimākārā sandissanti, sabbamaitaṃ liṅgaṃ nāma|

aivaṃ visaisana, visaisyavasaina duvidhau atthau pubbai vuttassa saddaliṅgassa atthau nāma, sau saliṅgau, sasaṅkhyau, saparimāṇau cāti tividhau hauti|

tattha saliṅgau yathā? sañ‍ñā, phassau, cittaṃ| kañ‍ñā, purisau, kulaṃ| mālā, rukkhau, dhananti|

sasaṅkhyau yathā? aikau, dvai, tayau, bahū ic‍cādi|

saparimāṇau yathā? vidatthi, hatthau, dauṇau, āḷhakaṃ ic‍cādi|

api ca suddhau, saṃsaṭṭhauti duvidhau liṅgatthau| tattha kammādisaṃsaggarahitau suddhau nāma| sau saliṅgau, sasaṅkhyau, saparimāṇau, upasaggatthau, nipātatthau, pāṭi-padikatthauti chabbidhau| atthi, sak‍kā, labbhāic‍cādi idha pāṭipadikaṃ nāma| tuna, tvāna, tvā, tavai, tuṃ, khattuṃpac‍cayantāpi nipātaisu gayhanti|

saṃsaṭṭhau  vuttasaṃsaṭṭhau, avuttasaṃsaṭṭhauti duvidhau| tattha vuttasaṃsaṭṭhau catubbidhau samāsaina vuttasaṃsaṭṭhau, taddhitaina, ākhyātaina, kitaināti| tattha samāsaina vuttau chakārakasambandhavasaina sattavidhau, bhāvaina saddhiṃ aṭṭhavidhau vā, tathā taddhitaina vuttau| ākhyātaina vuttau kattu, kamma, bhāvavasaina tividhau| kitaina vuttau chakāraka, bhāvavasaina sattavidhau| sabbau suddhau caiva vuttasaṃsaṭṭhau ca paṭhamāya visayau|

avuttasaṃsaṭṭhaupi kattusaṃsaṭṭhau, kammasaṃsaṭṭhautiādinā anaikavidhau| sau dutiyādīnaṃ aiva visayauti| aittha ca vibhattiyā vinā kaivalau saddau payaugaṃ nārahatīti katvā payaugārahatthamaiva chabbidhai suddhai catubbidhai ca vuttasaṃsaṭṭhai paṭhamā payuj‍jati, na atthajautanatthaṃ|

kainaci vācakaina avuttāni pana kammādīni vibhattīhi vinā viditāni na hauntītikatvā atthajautanatthampi kammādīsu dutiyādayau payuj‍janti| tasmā atthamattaiti idha daisantarāvacchaidakai visayamattai bhummaṃ| kammai dutiyāic‍cādīsu pana nipphādaitabbai payaujanai bhummanti aivaṃ dvin‍naṃ bhummānaṃ nānattaṃ vaiditabbanti|

290. āmantanai ka॰ 285; rū॰ 70; nī॰ 578; caṃ॰ 2.1.94; pā॰ 2.3.47; āmantaṇai (bahūsu)|

pagaiva siddhassa vatthunau nāmaina vā nipātaina vā attanau abhimukhīkaraṇaṃ āmantanaṃ nāma| adhikāmantanai atthamattai paṭhamā hauti| aittha ca āmantanapadaṃ nāma kriyāpaikkhaṃ na hauti, tasmā kārakasañ‍ñaṃ na labhati|

taṃ pana duvidhaṃ sādarā’nādaravasaina| aihi samma, aihi jaiti|

tathā  sajīva, nij‍jīvavasaina, bhau purisa, vadaihi bhau saṅkha, vadaihi bhau saṅkha dī॰ ni॰ 2.426| ummuj‍ja bhau puthusilai, ummuj‍ja bhau puthusilaiti saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.358|

tathā pac‍cakkhā’pac‍cakkhavasaina, bhau purisa, kahaṃ aikaputtaka, kahaṃ aikaputtakāti saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.63; dha॰ pa॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.2|

tathā niyamā’niyamavasaina, bhau purisa, acchariyaṃ vata bhau abbhutaṃ vata bhau dī॰ ni॰ 2.192| yatra hi nāma sañ‍ñī samānautiādi dī॰ ni॰ 2.192| idaṃ āmantanaṃ nāma pagaiva siddhai aiva hauti, na vidhātabbai, na hi pagaiva rājabhāvaṃ vā bhikkhubhāvaṃ vā appattaṃ janaṃ ‘‘bhau rājā’’ti vā ‘‘bhau bhikkhū’’ti vā āmantaintīti|

paṭhamāvibhattirāsi niṭṭhitau|

dutiyāvibhattirāsi

kasmiṃ atthai dutiyā?

291. kammai dutiyā ka॰ 297; rū॰ 284; nī॰ 580; caṃ॰ 2.1.43; pā॰ 1.4.49-51|

kammatthai dutiyā hauti| kariyataiti kammaṃ, taṃ nibbattikammaṃ, vikatikammaṃ, pattikammanti tividhaṃ hauti|

tattha nibbattikammaṃ yathā? iddhimā hatthivaṇṇaṃ māpaiti, rājā nagaraṃ māpaiti, mātā puttaṃ vijāyati, bījaṃ rukkhaṃ janaiti, kammaṃ vipākaṃ janaiti, āhārau balaṃ janaiti, janau puñ‍ñaṃ karauti, pāpaṃ karauti, buddhau dhammaṃ daisaisi, vinayaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi, bhikkhu jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti ic‍cādi|

vikatikammaṃ  yathā? gaihaṃ karauti, rathaṃ karauti, ghaṭaṃ karauti, paṭaṃ vāyati, audanaṃ pacati, bhattaṃ pacati, kaṭṭhaṃ aṅgāraṃ karauti, suvaṇṇaṃ kaṭakaṃ karauti, gaihaṃ jhāpaiti, rukkhaṃ chindati, pākāraṃ bhindati, vihayau lunāti, pāṇaṃ hanati, bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jati ic‍cādi|

pattikammaṃ yathā? gāmaṃ gacchati, gaihaṃ pavisati, rukkhaṃ ārauhati, nadiṃ tarati, ādic‍caṃ passati, dhammaṃ suṇāti, buddhaṃ vandati payirupāsati ic‍cādi|

pakatikammaṃ, vikatikammanti duvidhaṃ| suvaṇṇaṃ kaṭakaṃ karauti, kaṭṭhaṃ aṅgāraṃ karauti, purisaṃ ṭhitaṃ passati, purisaṃ gacchantaṃ passati, bhikkhuṃ passati sataṃ, sampajānaṃ, abhik‍kamantaṃ, paṭik‍kamantaṃ, ālaukaintaṃ, vilaukaintaṃ, samiñ‍jaintaṃ, pasāraintaṃ|

aittha ‘purisaṃ, bhikkhu’nti pakatikammaṃ, ‘ṭhitaṃ, sataṃ’ic‍cādīni vikatikammāni|

dhātukammaṃ, kāritakammanti duvidhaṃ| gāmaṃ gacchati, purisaṃ gāmaṃ gamaiti|

dhātukammañ‍ca dvikammikadhātūnaṃ duvidhaṃ padhānakammaṃ, appadhānakammanti| ajapālau ajaṃ gāmaṃ naiti, purisau bhāraṃ gāmaṃ vahati, harati, gāmaṃ sākhaṃ kaḍḍhati, gāviṃ khīraṃ dauhati, brāhmaṇaṃ kambalaṃ yācati, brāhmaṇaṃ bhattaṃ bhikkhati, gāviyau vajaṃ avarundhati, bhagavantaṃ pañhaṃ pucchati, rukkhaṃ phalāni aucināti, sissaṃ dhammaṃ bravīti, bhagavā bhikkhū aitadavauca udā॰ 23 (thaukaṃ visadisaṃ), sissaṃ dhammaṃ anusāsati ic‍cādi|

aittha ca ‘ajaṃ, khīraṃ’ ic‍cādi padhānakammaṃ nāma kattārā pariggahaituṃ iṭṭhatarattā| ‘gāmaṃ, gāviṃ’ic‍cādi appadhānakammaṃ nāma tathā aniṭṭhatarattā|

tattha  padhānakammaṃ kathinakammaṃ nāma, kammabhāvai thirakammanti vuttaṃ hauti| appadhānakammaṃ akathinakammaṃ nāma, athirakammanti vuttaṃ hauti| tañhi kadāci sampadānaṃ hauti, kadāci apādānaṃ, kadāci sāmi, kadāci aukāsau| yathā – sau maṃ dakāya naiti, gāvitau khīraṃ dauhati, gāviyā khīraṃ dauhahi, gāviyaṃ khīraṃ dauhati ic‍cādi|

kammai dutiyāti vattatai|

292. gatibaudhāhārasaddatthā kammaka bhaj‍jādīnaṃ payauj‍jai ka॰ 300; rū॰ 286; nī॰ 587; caṃ॰ 2.1.44; pā॰ 1.4.52|

nic‍cavidhisuttamidaṃ| gamanatthānaṃ baudhanatthānaṃ āhāratthānaṃ saddatthānaṃ akammakānaṃ bhaj‍jādīnañ‍ca dhātūnaṃ payauj‍jai kammani dutiyā hauti| aittha ca payauj‍jakammaṃ nāma kāritakammaṃ vuc‍cati|

purisau purisaṃ gāmaṃ gamayati, sāmikau ajapālaṃ ajaṃ gāmaṃ nayāpaiti, ācariyau sissaṃ dhammaṃ baudhaiti, purisau purisaṃ bhattaṃ bhaujaiti, ācariyau sissaṃ dhammaṃ pāṭhaiti, purisau purisaṃ sayāpaiti, acchāpaiti, uṭṭhāpaiti, purisau purisaṃ dhañ‍ñaṃ bhaj‍jāpaiti, kauṭṭāpaiti, uddharāpaiti|

aitaisamīti kiṃ? purisau purisaina audanaṃ pācaiti|

aittha ca gamanatthādīnaṃ payauj‍jai tatiyāpi rūpasiddhiyaṃ 141 piṭṭhai saddanītiyañ‍ca sutta-148 piṭṭhai vuttā| saddanītiyaṃ tatiyāpayaugaipi kammatthamaiva icchati| ñāsādīsu katvatthaṃ icchanti|

yadā  pana paṭhamaṃ payaujakaṃ añ‍ñau dutiyau payaujaiti, tadā paṭhamau payauj‍jau nāma| tasmiṃ tatiyāaivāti vuttiyaṃ vuttaṃ| attanā vippakataṃ kuṭiṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti pārā॰ 363|

293. harādīnaṃ vā ka॰ 300; rū॰ 286; nī॰ 587; caṃ॰ 2.1.45; pā॰ 1.4.53|

harādīnaṃ payauj‍jai kammani vikappaina dutiyā hauti|

sāmikau purisaṃ bhāraṃ hāraiti purisaina vā, purisaṃ āhāraṃ ajjhauhāraiti purisaina vā, purisaṃ kammaṃ kāraiti purisaina vā, rājā purisaṃ attānaṃ dassaiti purisaina vā, purisaṃ buddhaṃ vandāpaiti purisaina vā|

194. na khādādīnaṃ ka॰ 300; rū॰ 286; nī॰ 587; caṃ॰ 2.1.47; pā॰ 1.4.57|

khādādīnaṃ payauj‍jai kammani na dutiyā hauti|

sāmikau purisaina khaj‍jaṃ khādāpaiti, ada-bhakkhanai, bhattaṃ ādaiti, sāmikau dāsaina purisaṃ avhāpaiti, saddāyāpaiti, kandayati, nādayati| aittha ca ‘saddāyāpaitī’ti saddaṃ kārāpaiti, nāmadhātu caisā| kanda, nadāpi saddatthāyaiva|

295. vahissāniyantukai ka॰ 300; rū॰ 286; nī॰ 587; caṃ॰ 2.1.48; pā॰ 1.4.52|

vahissāti dhātuniddaisau i-kārau, niyāmaiti payaujaitīti niyantā, natthi niyantā aitassāti aniyantukau| yassa añ‍ñaina payaujakaina kic‍caṃ natthi, sayamaiva ñatvā vahati, sau aniyantukau nāma, vahadhātussa tādisai aniyantukai payauj‍jai kammani dutiyā na hauti|

sāmikau  dāsaina bhāraṃ vāhaiti|

aniyantukaiti kiṃ? balībaddai bhāraṃ vāhaiti|

296. bhakkhissāhiṃsāyaṃ ka॰ 300; rū॰ 286; nī॰ 587; caṃ॰ 2.1.49; pā॰ 1.4.5|

bhakkhituṃ icchantassa bhakkhāpanaṃ hiṃsā nāma na hauti, anicchantassa bhakkhāpanaṃ hiṃsā nāma, bhakkhadhātussa payauj‍jai kammani ahiṃsāvisayai dutiyā na hauti|

sāmikau purisaina maudakai bhakkhāpaiti|

ahiṃsāyanti kiṃ? balībaddai sassaṃ bhakkhāpaiti| aittha ‘sassa’nti thūlataraṃ sassanti vadanti|

pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘sabbaisaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvāna apa॰ thaira 1.1.438, tausainti sabbapāṇinaṃ apa॰ thaira 1.1.300| thairassa pattau dutiyassa gāhaitabbau’’ ic‍cādinā pārā॰ 615 payauj‍jai chaṭṭhīpi dissati|

297. jhādīhi yuttā ka॰ 299; rū॰ 288; nī॰ 582, 586; caṃ॰ 2.1.50; pā॰ 2.3.2|

dhīic‍cādīhi nipātaupasaggaihi yuttā liṅgamhā dutiyā hauti|

dhī brāhmaṇassa hantāraṃ dha॰ pa॰ 389, dhīratthu’maṃ pūtikāyaṃ jā॰ 1.3.129, dhīratthu taṃ dhanalābhaṃ jā॰ 1.4.36, dhīratthu bahukai kāmai| tatiyāpi dissati, dhīratthu jīvitaina mai jā॰ 2.17.135| antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca nāḷandaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 1.1, abhitau gāmaṃ vasati, paritaugāmaṃ vasati, nadiṃ nairañ‍jaraṃ pati su॰ ni॰ 427, aitaisu chaṭṭhyatthai dutiyā|

tathā  paṭibhāti maṃ bhagavā udā॰ 45; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.217, apissu maṃ tissau upamāyau paṭibhaṃsu ma॰ ni॰ 1.374, paṭibhātu taṃ bhikkhu dhammau bhāsituṃ mahāva॰ 258| paṭibhantu taṃ cunda baujjhaṅgā saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.79 – ‘ma’nti mama, ‘ta’nti tava, sampadānatthai dutiyā| ‘ma’nti mamañāṇai, ‘ta’nti tavañāṇaitipi vaṇṇaisuṃ| na upāyamantaraina atthassa siddhi, natthi samādānamantaraina sikkhāpaṭilābhau, naividha na huraṃ na ubhayamantaraina, aisaivantau dukkhassa ma॰ ni॰ 3.393; udā॰ 74| tattha ‘antarainā’ti nipātapadamaitaṃ, vaj‍jaitvātyatthau| pubbaina gāmaṃ, dakkhiṇaina gāmaṃ, uttaraina gāmaṃ, gāmassa pubbaiti atthau|

upasaggapubbānaṃ akammakadhātūnaṃ payaugai ādhārai dutiyā, pathaviṃ adhisaissati, gāmaṃ adhitiṭṭhati, rukkhaṃ ajjhāvasati, mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā abhinisīdaiyya vā abhinipaj‍jaiyya vā pāci॰ 130, gāmaṃ upavasati, gāmaṃ anuvasati, pabbataṃ adhivasati, gharaṃ āvasati, agāraṃ ajjhāvasati dī॰ ni॰ 1.258; pārā॰ 519, upausathaṃ upavasati, kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jati, rūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jati, arūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jati, sak‍kassa sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati, nipan‍naṃ vā upanipaj‍jaiyya dī॰ ni॰ 3.282, nisin‍naṃ vā upanisīdaiyya dī॰ ni॰ 3.282, ṭhitaṃ vā upatiṭṭhaiyya dī॰ ni॰ 3.282 ic‍cādi|

tappāna, cāraipi dutiyā, nadiṃ pivati, samuddaṃ pivati, gāmaṃ carati, arañ‍ñaṃ carati, nadiyaṃ, gāmaiti atthau|

kāla, disāsupi ādhārai aiva dutiyā, taṃ khaṇaṃ, taṃ muhuttaṃ, taṃ kālaṃ, aikamantaṃ khu॰ pā॰ 5.1, aikaṃ samayaṃ khu॰ pā॰ 5.1; dī॰ ni॰ 1.1, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pārā॰ 16, sāyanhasamayaṃ, taṃ divasaṃ, imaṃ rattiṃ dī॰ ni॰ 3.285, dutiyampi, tatiyampi, catutthaṃ vā pañ‍camaṃ vā appaiti, tatau pubbaṃ, tatau paraṃ, purimaṃ disaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 2.336, dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 2.336, pacchimaṃ disaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 2.336, uttaraṃ disaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 2.336, imā dasa  disāyau, katamaṃ disaṃ tiṭṭhati nāgarājā jā॰ 1.16.104, imāsu disāsu katamāya disāya tiṭṭhati chaddantanāgarājāti atthauic‍cādi|

298. lakkhaṇitthambhūtavicchāsvabhinā ka॰ 299; rū॰ 288; nī॰ 582, 586; caṃ॰ 2.1.54; pā॰ 1.4.90, 91; 2.3.8|

lakkhaṇādīsu atthaisu pavattaina abhinā yuttā liṅgamhā dutiyā hauti|

lakkhīyati lakkhitabbaṃ anaināti lakkhaṇaṃ| ayaṃ pakārau itthaṃ, īdisau visaisauti atthau| itthaṃ bhūtau pattauti itthambhūtau| bhin‍nai atthai byāpituṃ icchā vicchā|

tattha lakkhaṇai –

rukkhamabhi vij‍jautatai vij‍ju, rukkhaṃ abhi byāpaitvā vij‍jautataiti atthau, vij‍jaubhāsaina byāpitau rukkhau vij‍juppādassa lakkhaṇaṃ sañ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti|

itthambhūtai –

sādhu daivadattau mātaramabhi, mātaraṃ abhi visiṭṭhaṃ katvā sādhūti atthau, daivadattau sak‍kac‍caṃ mātupaṭṭhānai aggapurisauti vuttaṃ hauti| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau pārā॰ 1| aittha ca ‘abbhuggatau’ti abhi visiṭṭhaṃ katvā uggatauti atthau, ayaṃ kittisaddau bhautau gautamassa sakalalaukaggabhāvaṃ pakāsaitvā uggatauti vuttaṃ hauti, kittisaddasambandhai pana tassa khau pana bhautau gautamassāti atthau|

vicchāyaṃ –

rukkhaṃ  rukkhaṃ abhi vij‍jautatai candau, byāpaitvā vij‍jautataityatthau|

aittha ca lakkhaṇādiatthā abhisaddaina jautanīyā piṇḍatthā aiva, na vacanīyatthā, byāpanādiatthā aiva vacanīyatthāti|

299. patiparīhi bhāgai ca ka॰ 299; rū॰ 288; nī॰ 582, 586; caṃ॰ 2.1.55; pā॰ 1.4.90|

lakkhaṇi’tthambhūta, vicchāsu ca bhāgai ca pavattaihi pati, parīhi yuttā liṅgamhā dutiyā hauti|

lakkhaṇai –

rukkhaṃ pati vij‍jautatai vij‍ju, rukkhaṃ pari vij‍jautatai vij‍ju| tattha ‘patī’ti paṭic‍ca, ‘parī’ti pharitvā|

itthambhūtai –

sādhu daivadattau mātaraṃ pati, sādhu daivadattau mātaraṃ pari|

vicchāyaṃ –

rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ pati vij‍jautatai candau, rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ pari vij‍jautatai candau|

bhāgai –

taṃ dīyatu, yadaittha maṃ pati siyā, taṃ dīyatu, yadaittha maṃ pari siyā| tattha ‘patī’ti paṭic‍ca, ‘parī’ti paric‍ca, uddissāti atthau, ‘ṭhapita’nti pāṭhasaisau| aittha maṃ uddissa yaṃ  vatthu ṭhapitaṃ siyā, taṃ mai dīyatūtyatthau, aitaisu bahūsu bhāgaisu yau mama bhāgau, sau mayhaṃ dīyatūti vuttaṃ hautīti|

300. anunā ka॰ 299; rū॰ 288; nī॰ 582, 586; caṃ॰ 2.1.56; pā॰ 1.4.84, 90|

lakkhaṇi’tthambhūta, vicchāsu ca bhāgai ca pavattaina anunā yuttā liṅgamhā dutiyā hauti|

lakkhaṇai –

rukkhaṃ anu vij‍jautatai vij‍ju, rukkhaṃ anu pharitvāti atthau| caturāsītisahassāni, sambuddhamanupabbajuṃ bu॰ vaṃ॰ 21.5, ‘sambuddha’nti baudhisattaṃ, anu gantvā pabbajiṃsūti atthau, vipassibaudhisattai pabbajitai sati tānipi caturāsītikulaputtasahassāni pabbajiṃsūti vuttaṃ hauti| sac‍cakriyamanu vuṭṭhi pāvassi, ‘anū’ti anvāya, paṭic‍cāti atthau, sac‍cakriyāya sati sac‍cakriyahaitu daivau pāvassīti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘haitu ca lakkhaṇaṃ bhavatī’’ti vuttiyaṃ vuttaṃ| sac‍cakriyāya sahaivātipi yuj‍jati| ‘‘saha sac‍cai katai mayha’’nti cariyā॰ 3.82 hi vuttaṃ|

itthambhūtai –

sādhu daivadattau mātaramanu| tattha ‘anū’ti anvāya paṭic‍ca|

vicchāyaṃ –

rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ anu vij‍jautatai candau| tattha ‘anū’ti anu pharitvā|

bhāgai –

yadaittha  maṃ anu siyā, taṃ dīyatu| tattha ‘anū’ti anvāya| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

301. sahatthai ka॰ 299; rū॰ 288; nī॰ 582, 586; caṃ॰ 2.1.57; pā॰ 1.4.85|

sahatthai anunā yuttā liṅgamhā dutiyā hauti|

pabbataṃ anu tiṭṭhati pabbatamanusainā tiṭṭhati (mauggal‍lānavuttiyaṃ)| nadiṃ anvāvasitā bārāṇasī| ‘anū’ti anugantvā, nadiyā saha ābaddhā tiṭṭhatīti vuttaṃ hauti|

302. hīnai ka॰ 299; rū॰ 288; nī॰ 582, 586; caṃ॰ 2.1.58; pā॰ 1.4.86|

hīnai pavattaina anunā yuttā liṅgamhā dutiyā hauti|

anu sāriputtaṃ pañ‍ñavantau, anugatā pacchatau gatāti atthau, sabbai pañ‍ñavantau sāriputtatau hīnāti vuttaṃ hauti|

303. upaina ka॰ 299; rū॰ 288; nī॰ 582, 586; caṃ॰ 2.1.59; pā॰ 1.4.87|

hīnai upaina yuttā liṅgamhā dutiyā hauti|

upa sāriputtaṃ pañ‍ñavantau, upaic‍ca gatā samīpai gatāti atthau, hīnātvaiva vuttaṃ hauti|

aittha ca abhiic‍cādayau kammappavacanīyāti saddasatthaisu vuttā| tattha pakāraina vuc‍catīti pavacanīyaṃ, pakārau ca lakkhaṇi’tthambhūta, vicchādikau piṇḍatthau vuc‍cati, kammanti byāpanādikriyā, kammaṃ pavacanīyaṃ yaihi tai kammappavacanīyā|

tattha  byāpanādikriyāvisaisavācīhi upasaggaihi sambandhaisati kammatthai dutiyā hauti, asambandhai pana ādhāra, sāmyādiatthaisu hauti, lakkhaṇādayau pana sāmatthiyasiddhā piṇḍatthā aivāti|

304. kāladdhānamac‍cantasaṃyaugai ka॰ 298; rū॰ 287; nī॰ 581; pā॰ 2.3.5

kālassa vā addhunau vā dabba, guṇa, kriyāhi ac‍cantaṃ nirantaraṃ saṃyaugai kāla’ddhānavācīhi liṅgaihi paraṃ dutiyā hauti|

kālai –

sattāhaṃ gavapānaṃ, māsaṃ maṃsaudanaṃ, saradaṃ ramaṇīyā nadī, sabbakālaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ nandanaṃ, māsaṃ sajjhāyati, vassasataṃ jīvati, tayau māsai abhidhammaṃ daisaiti|

addhānai –

yaujanaṃ vanarāji, yaujanaṃ dīghau pabbatau, kausaṃ sajjhāyati|

ac‍cantasaṃyaugaiti kiṃ? māsai māsai bhuñ‍jati, yaujanai yaujanai vihārau|

aittha ca kriyāvisaisanampi kattārā sādhaitabbattā kammagatikaṃ hauti, tasmā tampi ‘kammai dutiyā’ti aittha kammasaddaina gayhati|

sukhaṃ saiti, dukkhaṃ saiti, sīghaṃ gacchati, khippaṃ gacchati, dandhaṃ gacchati, muduṃ pacati, garuṃ aissati, lahuṃ aissati, san‍nidhikārakaṃ bhuñ‍jati, samparivattakaṃ autāpaiti, kāyappacālakaṃ gacchati pāci॰ 590, hatthappacālakaṃ  gacchati, sīsappacālakaṃ gacchati pāci॰ 594-595, surusurukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jati pāci॰ 627, avagaṇḍakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jati pāci॰ 622, piṇḍukkhaipakaṃ bhuñ‍jati pāci॰ 620, hatthaniddhunakaṃ bhuñ‍jati pāci॰ 623, hatthanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jati pāci॰ 628, candimasūriyā samaṃ pariyāyanti, visamaṃ pariyāyanti ic‍cādi|

dutiyāvibhattirāsi niṭṭhitau|

tatiyāvibhattirāsi

kasmiṃ atthai tatiyā?

305. kattukaraṇaisu tatiyā ka॰ 286, 288; rū॰ 291, 293; nī॰ 591, 594; caṃ॰ 2.1.62-3; pā॰ 2.3.18|

kattari karaṇai ca tatiyā hauti| kattāti ca kārakauti ca atthatau aikaṃ ‘‘karautīti kattā, karautīti kārakau’’ti, tasmā ‘‘kattukārakau’’ti vuttai dvin‍naṃ pariyāyasaddānaṃ vasaina ayamaiva kriyaṃ aikantaṃ karauti, sāmī hutvā karauti, attappadhānau hutvā karautīti viñ‍ñāyati, tatau kriyā nāma kattunau aiva byāpārau, na añ‍ñaisanti ca, añ‍ñai pana kriyāsādhanai kattunau upakārakattā kārakā nāmāti ca, tathā anupakārakattā akārakā nāmāti ca viñ‍ñāyantīti|

tattha kattā tividhau sayaṃkattā, payaujakakattā, kammakattāti|

tattha dhātvatthaṃ sayaṃ karauntau sayaṃkattā nāma, purisau kammaṃ karauti|

paraṃ  niyaujaintau payaujakakattā nāma, purisau dāsaṃ kammaṃ kāraiti|

kammakattā nāma payauj‍jakakattāpi vuc‍cati, purisau dāsaina kammaṃ kāraiti dāsassa vā, yau ca añ‍ñaina kataṃ payaugaṃ paṭic‍ca kammabhūtaupi sukarattā vā kammabhāvaina avattukāmatāya vā ajānanatāya vā vañ‍caitukāmatāya vā kattubhāvaina vauharīyati, sau kammakattā nāma, kusūlau sayamaiva bhij‍jati, ghaṭau sayamaiva bhij‍jati| apica sukarau vā hautu duk‍karau vā, yau kammarūpakriyāpadai paṭhamantau kattā, sau kammakattāti vuc‍cati| saddarūpaina kammañ‍ca taṃ attharūpaina kattā cāti kammakattā, kusūlau bhij‍jati, ghaṭau bhij‍jati, pac‍cati muninau bhattaṃ, thaukaṃ thaukaṃ gharai gharai ic‍cādi|

aittha ca saddatthau duvidhau paramatthau, pañ‍ñattatthauti| tattha paramatthau aikantaina vij‍jamānauyaiva| pañ‍ñattatthau pana kauci vij‍jamānauti sammatau| yathā? rājaputtau, gauvisāṇaṃ, campakapupphanti| kauci avij‍jamānauti sammatau| yathā? vañjhāputtau, sasavisāṇaṃ, udumbarapupphanti| saddau ca nāma vatticchāpaṭibaddhavuttī hauti, vattamānau ca saddau atthaṃ na dīpaitīti natthi, saṅkaitai sati suṇantassa atthavisayaṃ buddhiṃ na janaitīti natthīti adhippāyau| iti avij‍jamānasammataupi atthau saddabuddhīnaṃ visayabhāvaina vij‍jamānau aiva hauti| itarathā ‘vañjhāputtau’ti padaṃ suṇantassa tadatthavisayaṃ cittaṃ nāma na pavattaiyyāti, saddabuddhīnañ‍ca visayabhāvaina vij‍jamānau nāma atthau saddanānāttai buddhinānāttai ca sati nānā hauti, visuṃ visuṃ vij‍jamānau nāma hautīti adhippāyau| aivaṃ saddabuddhivisayabhāvaina vij‍jamānañ‍ca nānābhūtañ‍ca atthaṃ paṭic‍ca kārakanānāttaṃ kriyākārakanānāttañ‍ca hauti, na pana sabhāvatau vij‍jamānamaiva nānābhūtamaiva ca atthanti niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ| tasmā ‘‘saṃyaugau jāyatai’’ ic‍cādīsu saddabuddhīnaṃ nānāttasiddhaina atthanānāttaina dvin‍naṃ saddānaṃ dvin‍naṃ atthānañ‍ca kriyākārakatāsiddhi vaiditabbāti|

kayiratai anaināti karaṇaṃ, kriyāsādhanai kattunau sahakārīkāraṇanti vuttaṃ hauti| taṃ duvidhaṃ ajjhattikakaraṇaṃ, bāhirakaraṇanti|

tattha kattunau aṅgabhūtaṃ karaṇaṃ ajjhattikaṃ nāma, purisau cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, manasā dhammaṃ vijānāti, hatthaina kammaṃ karauti, pādaina maggaṃ gacchati, rukkhau phalabhāraina auṇamati|

kattunau bahibhūtaṃ bāhiraṃ nāma, purisau yānaina gacchati, pharasunā parasunā (sak‍kataganthaisu) chindati, rukkhau vātaina auṇamati|

306. sahatthaina ka॰ 287; rū॰ 296; nī॰ 592; caṃ॰ 2.1.65; pā॰ 2.3.19|

sahasaddassa atthau yassa sauti sahatthau, sahatthaina saddaina yuttā liṅgamhā tatiyā hauti| sahasaddassa atthau nāma samavāyatthau|

sau tividhau dabbasamavāyau, guṇasamavāyau, kriyāsamavāyauti| puttaina saha dhanavā pitā, puttaina saha thūlau pitā, puttaina saha āgatau pitā| saha, saddhiṃ, samaṃ, nānā, vināic‍cādikau sahatthasaddau nāma|

nisīdi bhagavā saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina mahāva॰ 59, sahassaina samaṃ mitā saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.32, piyaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau dī॰ ni॰ 2.183, 207, saṅghau saha vā gaggaina vinā vā gaggaina upausathaṃ karaiyya mahāva॰ 167|

307. lakkhaṇai rū॰ 147 piṭṭhai; nī॰ 598; caṃ॰ 2.1.66; pā॰ 2.3.21|

lakkhaṇaṃ  vuc‍cati itthambhūtalakkhaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ tatiyā hauti|

assupuṇṇaihi naittaihi, pitaraṃ sau udikkhati jā॰ 2.22.2123| brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati a॰ ni॰ 3.67, asambhin‍naina vilaipanaina rājānamadakkhi, tidaṇḍakaina paribbājakamadakkhi, ūnapañ‍cabandhanaina pattaina añ‍ñaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti pārā॰ 612 (thaukaṃ visadisaṃ), bhikkhu pāsādikaina abhik‍kantaina paṭik‍kantaina ālaukitaina vilaukitaina samiñ‍jitaina pasāritaina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi, sā kāḷī dāsī bhin‍naina sīsaina lauhitaina galantaina pativissakānaṃ ujjhāpaisi ma॰ ni॰ 1.226, ukkhittakāya antaragharai gacchanti pāci॰ 584, pal‍latthikāya antaragharai nisīdanti pāci॰ 599|

aṅgavikāraupi idha saṅgayhati ka॰ 291; rū॰ 299; nī॰ 603, akkhinā kāṇaṃ passati, ‘akkhī’ti idaṃ ‘kāṇa’nti padai visaisanaṃ, vikalaina cakkhuaṅgaina sau kāṇau nāma hauti| hatthaina kuṇiṃ passati, pādaina khañ‍jaṃ passati|

308. haitumhi ka॰ 289; rū॰ 297; nī॰ 601|

hinauti pavattati phalaṃ aitaināti haitu, tasmiṃ tatiyā hauti|

an‍naina vasati, vij‍jāya sādhu, kammunā vattati laukau, kammunā vattati pajā ma॰ ni॰ 2.460; su॰ ni॰ 659 (vattatī pajā)| kammunā vasalau hauti, kammunā hauti brāhmaṇau su॰ ni॰ 136| kainaṭṭhaina dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathā, kaina nimittaina, kaina vaṇṇaina  saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.234 kaina pac‍cayaina, kaina haitunā jā॰ 2.22.2097, kaina kāraṇaina jā॰ aṭṭha॰ 4.15 mātaṅgajātakavaṇṇanā ic‍cādi|

aittha ca karaṇaṃ tividhaṃ kriyāsādhakakaraṇaṃ, visaisanakaraṇaṃ, nānāttakaraṇanti|

tattha kriyāsādhakaṃ pubbai vuttamaiva|

visaisanakaraṇaṃ yathā? ādic‍cau nāma gauttaina, sākiyau nāma jātiyā su॰ ni॰ 425| gauttaina gautamau nāthau apa॰ thaira 1.1.253 (visadisaṃ), sāriputtauti nāmaina apa॰ thaira 1.1.251, vissutau pañ‍ñavā ca sau, jātiyā khattiyau buddhau dī॰ ni॰ 2.92, jātiyā sattavassikau mi॰ pa॰ 6.4.8, sippaina naḷakārau sau, aikūnatiṃsau vayasā dī॰ ni॰ 2.214, vij‍jāya sādhu, tapasā uttamau, suvaṇṇaina abhirūpau, pakatiyā abhirūpau, pakatiyā bhaddakau, yaibhuyyaina mattikā pāci॰ 86, dhammaina samaina raj‍jaṃ kāraiti, samaina dhāvati, visamaina dhāvati, sukhaina sukhitau haumi, pāmauj‍jaina pamuditau bu॰ vaṃ॰ 2.78| dvidauṇaina dhañ‍ñaṃ kiṇāti, sahassaina assai vik‍kiṇāti, attanāva attānaṃ samman‍nati pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikathā (samman‍ni) ic‍cādi|

nānāttakaraṇaṃ yathā? kiṃ mai aikaina tiṇṇaina, purisaina thāmadassinā bu॰ vaṃ॰ 2.56| kiṃ tai jaṭāhi dummaidha, kiṃ tai ajinasāṭiyā dha॰ pa॰ 394| alaṃ tai idha vāsaina pārā॰ 436, alaṃ mai buddhaina pārā॰ 52, kin‍numaibuddhaina pārā॰ 52, na mamatthau buddhaina pārā॰ 52, maṇinā mai atthau pārā॰ 344, vadaiyyātha bhantai yainatthau, pānīyaina pāṇiyaina (mū॰) atthau, mūlaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi atthau mahāva॰ 263, saiyyaina atthikau, mahagghaina  atthikau, māsaina pubbau, pitarā sadisau, mātarā samau, kahāpaṇaina ūnau, dhanaina vikalau, asinā kalahau, vācāya kalahau, ācāraina nipuṇau, vācāya nipuṇau, guḷaina missakau, tilaina missakau, vācāya sakhilau ic‍cādi|

tathā kammā’vadhi, ādhāra’c‍cantasaṃyauga, kriyāpavaggāpi nānāttakaraṇai saṅgayhanti|

kammai tāva –

tilaihi khaittai vappati, tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaiti, sunakhaihi khādāpainti ic‍cādi|

avadhimhi –

sumuttā mayaṃ taina mahāsamaṇaina cūḷava॰ 437, muttaumhi kāsirājaina, cakkhu suñ‍ñaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.85, auttappati kāyaduc‍caritaina a॰ ni॰ 5.2, hirīyati kāyaduc‍caritaina a॰ ni॰ 5.2, jigucchati sakaina kāyaina, pathabyā aikaraj‍jaina, saggassa gamanaina vā| sabbalaukādhipac‍caina, sautāpattiphalaṃ varaṃ dha॰ pa॰ 178 ic‍cādi|

ādhārai –

taina khaṇaina taina layaina taina muhuttaina mahāva॰ 17, taina samayaina pārā॰ 1, kālaina dhammassavanaṃ khu॰ pā॰ 5.9| sau vau mamac‍cayaina satthā dī॰ ni॰ 2.216, tiṇṇaṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina dī॰ ni॰ 2.168; udā॰ 51, pubbaina gāmaṃ, dakkhiṇaina gāmaṃ, puratthimaina dhataraṭṭhau, dakkhiṇaina virūḷhakau pacchimaina virūpakkhau dī॰ ni॰ 2.336, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami khu॰ pā॰ 5.1 ic‍cādi|

ac‍cantasaṃyaugai –

māsaina bhuñ‍jati, yaujanaina dhāvati ic‍cādi|

kriyāpavaggau  nāma kriyāya sīghataraṃ niṭṭhāpanaṃ, tasmiṃ jautaitabbai tatiyā, aikāhainaiva bārāṇasiṃ pāpuṇi, tīhi māsaihi abhidhammaṃ daisaisi, navahi māsaihi vihāraṃ niṭṭhāpaisi, gamanamattaina labhati, auṭṭhapahaṭamattaina paguṇaṃ akāsi|

tatiyāvibhattirāsi niṭṭhitau|

catutthīvibhattirāsi

kasmiṃ atthai catutthī?

309. sampadānai catutthī ka॰ 293; rū॰ 301; nī॰ 605; catutthī sampadānai (bahūsu), caṃ॰ 2.1.73; pā॰ 2.3.13|

sampadānai catutthī hauti| sammā padīyatai assāti sampadānaṃ, sampaṭicchakanti vuttaṃ hauti|

taṃ vatthusampaṭicchakaṃ, kriyāsampaṭicchakanti duvidhaṃ| bhikkhussa cīvaraṃ daiti, buddhassa silāghatai|

puna anirākaraṇaṃ, anumati, ārādhananti tividhaṃ hauti| tattha na nirākarauti na nivāraitīti anirākaraṇaṃ, diyyamānaṃ na paṭikkhipatīti atthau| asati hi paṭikkhipanai sampaṭicchanaṃ nāma hautīti| kāyacittaihi sampaṭicchanākāraṃ dassaitvā paṭiggaṇhantaṃ sampadānaṃ anumati nāma| vividhaihi āyācanavacanaihi parassa cittaṃ ārādhaitvā sampaṭicchantaṃ ārādhanaṃ nāma| baudhirukkhassa jalaṃ daiti, bhikkhussa an‍naṃ daiti, yācakassa an‍naṃ daiti|

kriyāsampaṭicchakaṃ nānākriyāvasaina bahuvidhaṃ|

tattha  raucanakriyāyaugai –

tañ‍ca amhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca ma॰ ni॰ 1.179; ma॰ ni॰ 2.435, pabbaj‍jā mama ruc‍cati jā॰ 2.22.43, kassa sāduṃ na ruc‍cati, na mai ruc‍cati bhaddantai, ulūkassābhisaicanaṃ jā॰ 1.3.60| gamanaṃ mayhaṃ ruc‍cati, māyasmantānampi saṅghabhaidau ruc‍cittha pārā॰ 418, yassāyasmatau na khamati, khamati saṅghassa pārā॰ 438, bhattaṃ mayhaṃ chādaiti, bhattamassa nacchādaiti cūḷava॰ 282, taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ lūkhāni bhaujanāni nacchādainti mahāva॰ 261 (thaukaṃ visadisaṃ)| tattha ‘chādaitī’ti icchaṃ uppādaitīti atthau|

dhāraṇappayaugai –

chattaggāhau rañ‍ñau chattaṃ dhāraiti, sampatijātassa baudhisattassa daivā chattaṃ dhārayiṃsu|

buddhassa silāghatai, thaumaitīti atthau, tuyhaṃ hanutai, tuṇhibhāvaina vañ‍caitīti atthau, bhikkhunī bhikkhussa bhuñ‍jamānassa pānīyaina vā vidhūpanaina vā upaṭṭhāti pāci॰ 816 (visadisaṃ)| dutiyāpi hauti, rañ‍ñau upaṭṭhāti, rājānaṃ upaṭṭhāti, ahaṃ bhautiṃ upaṭṭhissaṃ jā॰ 2.22.1934, ahaṃ taṃ upaṭṭhissāmi, mātāpituupaṭṭhānaṃ khu॰ pā॰ 5.6, tuyhaṃ sapatai, sapassu mai vaipacitti saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.253, sapathampi tai samma ahaṃ karaumi jā॰ 2.21.407, tava mayi saddahanatthaṃ sac‍caṃ karaumīti atthau, rañ‍ñau sataṃ dhāraiti, idha kulaputtau na kassaci kiñ‍ci dhāraiti a॰ ni॰ 4.62, tassa rañ‍ñau mayaṃ nāgaṃ dhārayāma| tattha ‘rañ‍ñau sataṃ dhāraitī’ti sataṃ balidhanaṃ vā daṇḍadhanaṃ vā nidaitīti nidhaitīti, nidhaima (kaici) atthau, ‘‘iṇaṃ katvā gaṇhātī’’ti ca vadanti| ‘dhārayāmā’ti puna nidaima nidhaitīti, nidhaima (kaici), tuyhaṃ saddahati, mayhaṃ saddahati, saddahāsi siṅgālassa, surāpītassa brāhmaṇa jā॰ 1.1.113|

daivāpi  tai pihayanti tādinau dha॰ pa॰ 94 (tassa pihayanti), daivāpi taisaṃ pihayanti, sambuddhānaṃ satīmataṃ dha॰ pa॰ 181, ‘pihayantī’ti punappunaṃ daṭṭhuṃ patthaintīti atthau| dutiyāpi hauti, sacai maṃ pihayasi, dhanaṃ pihaiti, hirañ‍ñaṃ pihaiti, suvaṇṇaṃ pihaiti| tatiyāpi dissati, rūpaina pihaiti, saddaina pihaiti ic‍cādi|

tassa kujjha mahāvīra jā॰ 1.4.49, mā mai kujjha rathaisabha jā॰ 2.22.1696 (kujjhi), yadihaṃ tassa kuppaiyyaṃ, mātu kuppati, pitu kuppati, yau appaduṭṭhassa narassa dussati dha॰ pa॰ 125; su॰ ni॰ 667; jā॰ 1.5.94, duhayati disānaṃ maighau, pūraiti vināsaiti vāti atthau, akālai vassantau hi vināsaiti nāma, yau mittānaṃ na dubbhati jā॰ 2.22.19, aduṭṭhassa tuvaṃ dubbhi jā॰ 1.16.295, mittānaṃ na dubbhaiyya, titthiyā issanti samaṇānaṃ, ussūyanti duj‍janā guṇavantānaṃ, pativissakānaṃ ujjhāpaisi ma॰ ni॰ 1.226 (ujjhāpaisi), mā tumhai tassa ujjhāyittha udā॰ 26 (visadisaṃ), mahārājānaṃ ujjhāpaitabbaṃ viravitabbaṃ vik‍kanditabbaṃ, kyāhaṃ ayyānaṃ aparajjhāmi pārā॰ 383 ayyai vā, rañ‍ñau aparajjhati rājānaṃ vā, ārādhau mai rājā hauti|

pati, āpubbassa su-dhātussa anu, patipubbassa ca gī-dhātussa yaugai sampadānai catutthī| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti, ‘‘bhaddantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ a॰ ni॰ 1.1| aittha ca pubbavākyai āmantanakriyāya kattā bhagavā, sau paravākyai pac‍cāsuyaugai sampadānaṃ hauti, ‘pac‍cassausu’nti bhaddantaiti paṭivacanaṃ adaṃsūti atthau| bhikkhū buddhassa āsuṇanti, rājā bimbisārau pilindavacchatthairassa ārāmikaṃ paṭissutvā mahāva॰ 270, amac‍cau rañ‍ñau bimbisārassa paṭissutvā mahāva॰ 270, sampaṭicchitvāti atthau, bhikkhu janaṃ dhammaṃ sāvaiti, janau tassa bhikkhunau anugiṇāti paṭigiṇāti, sādhukāraṃ daitīti atthau|

āraucanatthayaugai –

āraucayāmi vau bhikkhavai ma॰ ni॰ 1.416, paṭivaidayāmi vau bhikkhavai ma॰ ni॰ 1.416, āmantayāmi vau bhikkhavai dī॰ ni॰ 2.218 (visadisaṃ), dhammaṃ vau daisaissāmi ma॰ ni॰ 3.105, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti, yathā nau bhagavā byākaraiyya, niruttiṃ tai pavakkhāmi, ahaṃ tai ācikkhissāmi, ahaṃ tai kittayissāmi, bhikkhūnaṃ aitadavauca|

310. tadatthai ka॰ 277; rū॰ 303; nī॰ 554|

tassā tassā kriyāya atthauti tadatthau, tadatthai sampadānai catutthī hauti|

311. sassāya catutthiyā ka॰ 109; rū॰ 304; nī॰ 279-80|

akārantatau catutthībhūtassa sassa āyau hauti vā|

vinayau saṃvaratthāya, saṃvarau avippaṭisāratthāya, avippaṭisārau pāmuj‍jatthāya, pāmuj‍jaṃ pītatthāya, pīti passaddhatthāya, passaddhi sukhatthāya, sukhaṃ samādhatthāya, samādhi yathābhūtañāṇadassanatthāya, yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ nibbidatthāya, nibbidā virāgatthāya, virāgau vimuttatthāya, vimutti vimuttiñāṇadassanatthāya, vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ anupādāparinibbānatthāya pari॰ 366, atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ ma॰ ni॰ 1.50, alaṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāya pārā॰ 38, alaṃ sammauhāya, pākāya vajati, yuddhāya gacchati, gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ pāci॰ 902|

tumatthaupi  tadatthai saṅgayhati, alaṃ mittai sukhāpaituṃ, amittānaṃ dukhāya ca jā॰ 2.17.13| laukānukampāya buddhau laukai uppaj‍jati, alaṃ phāsuvihārāya, abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati mahāva॰ 13|

alamatthayaugai –

alaṃ mal‍lau mal‍lassa, arahati mal‍lau mal‍lassa, alaṃ tai idha vāsaina pārā॰ 436, alaṃ tai hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaina, kiṃ mai aikaina tiṇṇaina bu॰ vaṃ॰ 2.56, kiṃ tai jaṭāhi dummaidha, kiṃ tai ajinasāṭiyā dha॰ pa॰ 394|

mañ‍ñanāpayaugai anādarai apāṇismimaiva catutthī, kaṭṭhassa tuvaṃ mañ‍ñai, kaliṅgarassa kaḷiṅgarassa, kaḷaṅgarassa (ka॰) tuvaṃ mañ‍ñai, jīvitaṃ tiṇāyapi na mañ‍ñati|

anādaraiti kiṃ? suvaṇṇaṃ taṃ mañ‍ñai|

apāṇisminti kiṃ? gadrabhaṃ tuvaṃ mañ‍ñai|

gatyatthānaṃ nayanatthānañ‍ca dhātūnaṃ kammani catutthī, appau saggāya gacchati dha॰ pa॰ 174, yau maṃ dakāya naiti jā॰ 1.6.97, nirayāyupakaḍḍhati dha॰ pa॰ 311, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya cūḷava॰ 111|

āsīsanakriyāyaugai –

āyu bhavatau hautu, bhaddaṃ tai hautu, bhaddamatthu tai jā॰ 1.8.15; jā॰ 2.17.1, kusalaṃ tai hautu, anāmayaṃ tai hautu, sukhaṃ tai hautu, atthaṃ tai hautu, hitaṃ tai hautu, kalyāṇaṃ tai hautu, svāgataṃ tai hautu, sautthi tai hautu sautthi gabbhassa ma॰ ni॰ 2.351, maṅgalaṃ tai hautu|

sammutiyaugai  kammatthai [chaṭṭhī], itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau rūpiyachaḍḍakassa sammuti pārā॰ 590, pattagāhāpakassa sammutiic‍cādi pārā॰ 614|

āvikaraṇādiyaugai –

tuyhañ‍cassa āvi karaumi, tassa mai sak‍kau pāturahausi, tassa pahiṇaiyya, bhikkhūnaṃ dūtaṃ pāhaisi, kappati bhikkhūnaṃ āyaugau, vaṭṭati bhikkhūnaṃ āyaugau, patthaudanau dvin‍naṃ tiṇṇaṃ nappahauti, aikassa pahauti, aikassa pariyattau, upamaṃ tai karissāmi ma॰ ni॰ 1.258; jā॰ 2.19.24, añ‍jaliṃ tai paggaṇhāmi jā॰ 2.22.327, tathāgatassa phāsu hauti, āvikatā hissa phāsu mahāva॰ 134, laukassa atthau, laukassa hitaṃ, maṇinā mai atthau pārā॰ 344, na mamatthau buddhaina pārā॰ 52, namatthu buddhānaṃ namatthu baudhiyā jā॰ 1.2.17, vipassissa ca namatthu dī॰ ni॰ 3.277, namau karauhi nāgassa ma॰ ni॰ 1.249, namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama apa॰ thaira 1.2.129| sautthi pajānaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 1.274, suvatthi pajānaṃ ic‍cādi|

catutthīvibhattirāsi niṭṭhitau|

pañ‍camīvibhattirāsi

kasmiṃ atthai pañ‍camī?

312. pañ‍camyāvadhismiṃ ka॰ 295; rū॰ 307; nī॰ 607; caṃ॰ 2.1.81; pā॰ 2.3.28; 1.4.24|

avadhiyati vavatthiyati padatthau aitasmāti avadhi, tasmiṃ pañ‍camī hauti, avadhīti ca apādānaṃ vuc‍cati|

apanaitvā itau añ‍ñaṃ ādadāti gaṇhātīti apādānaṃ| taṃ tividhaṃ niddiṭṭhavisayaṃ, uppāṭavisayaṃ, anumaiyyavisayanti|

tattha  yasmiṃ apādānavisayabhūtau kriyāvisaisau sarūpatau niddiṭṭhau hauti, taṃ niddiṭṭhavisayaṃ| yathā? gāmā apainti munayau, nagarā niggatau rājā|

yasmiṃ pana sau pāṭhasaisaṃ katvā ajjhāharitabbau hauti, taṃ uppāṭavisayaṃ| yathā? valāhakā vij‍jautatai vij‍ju, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitauti mahāva॰ 30| aittha hi ‘nikkhamitvā’ti padaṃ ajjhāharitabbaṃ|

yasmiṃ pana sau niddiṭṭhau ca na hauti, ajjhāharituñ‍ca na sak‍kā, atha khau atthatau anumānavasaina sau viñ‍ñaiyyau hauti, taṃ anumaiyyavisayaṃ| yathā? māthurā pāṭaliputtakaihi abhirūpatarā, sīlamaiva sutā saiyyau jā॰ 1.5.65, mayā bhiyyau na vij‍jati, aggauhamasmi laukassa, jaiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa, saiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa ic‍cādi ma॰ ni॰ 3.207| kriyaṃ vinā kārakaṃ nāma na sijjhatītikatvā uk‍kaṃsanakriyā aittha anumaitabbā hauti| aivaṃ kriyāpadarahitaisu dūrayaugādīsupi avinābhāvikriyānumānaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

puna calā’calavasaina duvidhaṃ|

calaṃ yathā? purisau dhāvatā assā patati, dvai maiṇḍā yujjhitvā añ‍ñamañ‍ñatau apasak‍kanti| aittha ca yadi calaṃ siyā, kathaṃ avadhi nāma bhavaiyya| ac‍cutilakkhaṇau hi avadhīti? vuc‍catai-dvai maiṇḍā sakasakakriyāya calanti, itarītarakriyāya avadhī hauntīti natthi aittha avadhilakkhaṇaviraudhauti|

acalaṃ yathā? gāmā apainti munayau, nagarā niggatau rājā|

puna kāyasaṃsaggapubbakaṃ, cittasaṃsaggapubbakanti duvidhaṃ hauti, gāmā apainti munayau, caurā bhayaṃ jāyatai| aittha ca ‘‘kiṃva  dūrau itau gāmau, itau sā dakkhiṇā disā dī॰ ni॰ 3.279| itau aikanavutikappai’’ti dī॰ ni॰ 2.4 ādīsu vadantassa cittasaṃsaggapubbakampi vaiditabbaṃ| ‘‘na mātā puttatau bhāyati, na ca puttau mātitau bhāyati, bhayā bhītau na bhāsasī’’ti jā॰ 2.21.138 pāḷi| atthi tai itau bhayaṃ ma॰ ni॰ 2.350, natthi tai itau bhayaṃ, yatau khaimaṃ tatau bhayaṃ jā॰ 1.9.58, caurā bhāyati, caurā bhītau| chaṭṭhī ca, caurassa bhāyati, caurassa bhītau| dutiyā ca, ‘‘kathaṃ paralaukaṃ na bhāyaiyya, aivaṃ paralaukaṃ na bhāyaiyya, bhāyasi maṃ samaṇa su॰ ni॰ sūcilaumasutta, nāhaṃ taṃ bhāyāmi su॰ ni॰ sūcilaumasutta, bhāyitabbaṃ na bhāyati, nāhaṃ bhāyāmi bhauginaṃ jā॰ 2.22.835, na maṃ migā uttasantī’’ti jā॰ 2.22.307 pāḷipadāni dissanti| tattha ‘‘bhaugina’nti nāgaṃ, caurā tasati uttasati caurassa vā, sabbai tasanti daṇḍassa dha॰ pa॰ 129, pāpatau auttappati jigucchati harāyati pāpaina vā|

yatau kiñ‍ci sippaṃ vā vij‍jaṃ vā dhammaṃ vā gaṇhāti, tasmiṃ akkhātari pañ‍camī, upajjhāyā adhītai, upajjhāyā sippaṃ gaṇhāti, dvāsīti buddhatau gaṇhiṃ, dvai sahassāni bhikkhutau| caturāsītisahassāni, yaimai dhammā pavattinau thairagā॰ 1027|

yatau suṇāti, tasmiṃ pañ‍camī, chaṭṭhī ca, itau sutvā, imassa sutvā vā, yamhā dhammaṃ vijānaiyya, sammāsambuddhadaisitaṃ dha॰ pa॰ 392|

yatau labhati, tasmiṃ pañ‍camī, saṅghatau labhati, gaṇatau labhati|

yatau parājayati, yatau pabhavati, yatau jāyati, tasmiṃ pañ‍camī, buddhasmā parājayanti añ‍ñatitthiyā, pāḷiyaṃ pana parājiyaugai apādānaṃ pāṭhasaisavasaina labbhati, tasmiṃ khau  pana saṅgāmai daivā jiniṃsu, asurā parājiniṃsu| aittha daivaihi parājiniṃsūti pāṭhasaisau| ‘‘mayaṃ jitāmhā ambakāya| himavantā pabhavanti pañ‍ca mahānadiyau a॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 3.8.19, ayaṃ bhāgīrathī gaṅgā, himavantā pabhāvitā’’ti pāḷi apa॰ thaira 1.1.255, caurā bhayaṃ jāyatai, kāmatau jāyatai bhayaṃ dha॰ pa॰ 215, jātaṃ saraṇatau bhayaṃ jā॰ 1.1.36; 1.2.13; 1.9.56, 57, 59, yaṃkiñ‍ci bhayaṃ vā vairaṃ vā upaddavau vā upasaggau vā jāyati, sabbaṃ taṃ bālatau jāyati, nau paṇḍitatau, kāmatau jāyatī saukau dha॰ pa॰ 214, ubhatau sujātau puttau dī॰ ni॰ 1.311, urasmā jātau, urai jātau vā, cīvaraṃ uppaj‍jaiyya saṅghatau vā gaṇatau vā ñātimittatau vā pārā॰ 500 (thaukaṃ visadisaṃ)|

añ‍ñatthānaṃ yaugai pañ‍camī, tatau añ‍ñaṃ, tatau paraṃ mahāva॰ 346, tatau aparaina samayaina pārā॰ 195|

upasaggānaṃ yaugai pana –

313. apaparīhi vaj‍janai ka॰ 272; rū॰ 309; nī॰ 558, 568; caṃ॰ 2.1.82; pā॰ 1.4.88; 2.3.10|

vaj‍janai pavattaihi apa, parīhi yaugai pañ‍camī hauti|

apapabbatā vassati daivau, paripabbatā vassati daivau, apasālāya āyanti vāṇijā, parisālāya āyanti vāṇijā, pabbataṃ sālaṃ vaj‍jaitvāti atthau| kac‍cāyanai pana ‘‘uparipabbatā daivau vassatī’’ti pāṭhau paurāṇapāṭhau, paripabbatāti yuttau| upariyaugai pana sattamīyaiva dissati – ‘‘tasmiṃ uparipabbatai ma॰ ni॰ 3.216; jā॰ 1.8.16, uparipāsādai dī॰ ni॰ 2.408, uparivaihāsai, uparivaihāsakuṭiyā’’ti, pāci॰ 130 tattha pabbatassa upari uparipabbatanti atthau|

314. paṭinidhipaṭidānaisu patinā ka॰ 272; rū॰ 309; nī॰ 558, 568; caṃ॰ 2.1.83; pā॰ 2.3.11; 1.4.9.2|

paṭinidhi  nāma paṭibimbaṭṭhapanaṃ, paṭidānaṃ nāma paṭibhaṇḍadānaṃ taisu pavattaina patinā yaugai pañ‍camī hauti|

buddhasmā pati sāriputtau dhammaṃ daisaiti, tailasmā pati ghataṃ daiti|

315. ritai dutiyā ca ka॰ 272; rū॰ 309; nī॰ 558, 568; caṃ॰ 2.1.84; pā॰ 2.3.29|

ritaisaddaina yaugai pañ‍camī hauti dutiyā ca|

ritai saddhammā, ritai saddhammaṃ|

316. vināñ‍ñatraihi tatiyā ca ka॰ 272; rū॰ 309; nī॰ 558, 568; caṃ॰ 2.1.85; pā॰ 2.3.32; ‘vināñ‍ñatra tatiyāca’ (bahūsu)|

vaj‍janai pavattaihi vinā, añ‍ñatrasaddaihi yaugai pañ‍camī, dutiyā, tatiyā ca haunti|

vinā saddhammā, vinā saddhammaṃ, vinā saddhammaina, añ‍ñatra saddhammā, añ‍ñatra saddhammaṃ, añ‍ñatra saddhammaina|

317. puthunānāhi ca ka॰ 272; rū॰ 309; nī॰ 558, 568; caṃ॰ 2.1.86; pā॰ 2.3.32; ‘puthanānāhi ca’ (bahūsu)|

vaj‍janai pavattaihi puthu, nānāsaddaihi ca yaugai pañ‍camī, tatiyā ca haunti|

puthagaiva janasmā, puthagaiva janaina, nānā saddhammā, nānā saddhammaina, piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau dī॰ ni॰ 2.183; cūḷava॰ 437| ‘‘tai bhikkhū nānākulā pabbajitā’’ti aittha pana nānāppakāratthau nānāsaddau, na vaj‍janatthau, aittha ca vaj‍janatthau nāma viyaugatthau asammissatthau|

mariyādā’bhividhīsu  pavattaihi āsadda, yāvasaddaihi yaugaipi pañ‍camī, dutiyā ca|

tattha yassa avadhinau sambandhinī kriyā, taṃ bahikatvā pavattati, sau mariyādau| yathā? āpabbatā khaittaṃ tiṭṭhati āpabbataṃ vā, yāvapabbatā khaittaṃ tiṭṭhati yāvapabbataṃ vā|

yassa sambandhinī kriyā, taṃ antaukatvā byāpaitvā pavattati, sau abhividhi| yathā? ābhavaggā bhagavatau kittisaddau abbhuggatau ābhavaggaṃ vā, bhavatau ābhavaggaṃ dhammatau āgautrabhuṃ savantīti āsavā, yāvabhavaggā yāvabhavaggaṃ vā, tāvadaiva yāvabrahmalaukā saddau abbhuggatau|

ārabbhai, sahatthai ca pañ‍camī, yatauhaṃ bhagini ariyāya jātiyā jātau ma॰ ni॰ 2.351, yatau paṭṭhāyāti atthau| yatau sarāmi attānaṃ, yatau pattausmi viñ‍ñutaṃ jā॰ 2.22.307| yatau paṭṭhāya, yatau pabhuti|

sahatthai –

saha sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇappaṭilābhā, saha parinibbānā dī॰ ni॰ 2.220, saha dassanuppādā|

‘‘uppādā vā tathāgatānaṃ anuppādā vā tathāgatāna’’nti saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.20, aittha bhāvalakkhaṇai pañ‍camī|

‘‘sahatthā dānaṃ daiti, sahatthā paṭiggaṇhātī’’ti aittha karaṇai|

‘‘aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 1.250, tadaggai khau vāsaiṭṭha’’ic‍cādīsu dī॰ ni॰ 3.130, ārabbhai sattamī|

‘‘yatvādhikaraṇaṃ  dī॰ ni॰ 1.213, yataunidānaṃ su॰ ni॰ 275, tataunidānaṃ’’ ic‍cādīsu ma॰ ni॰ 1.238 vākyai icchitai sati haitvatthai pañ‍camī, samāsai icchitai sati atthamattai pañ‍camī|

dvin‍naṃ kārakānaṃ kriyānañ‍ca majjhai pavattakāladdhānavācīhi pañ‍camī, luddakau pakkhasmā migaṃ vijjhati, kausā kuñ‍jaraṃ vijjhati| aittha ca luddakau sakiṃ migaṃ vijjhitvā pakkhabbhantaramhi na vijjhi, pakkhai paripuṇṇai puna vijjhati, pakkhasaddau dvin‍naṃ vijjhanavārānaṃ majjhai kālavācī hauti, dvaipi vijjhanakriyā kārakaihi sahaiva sijjhantīti kārakānañ‍ca majjhaiti vuc‍cati| vuttiyaṃ pana ‘‘aj‍ja bhutvā daivadattau dvihai bhuñ‍jissati, dvihā bhuñ‍jissati, atraṭṭhau’yamissāsau kausai lakkhaṃ vijjhati, kausā lakkhaṃ vijjhatī’’ti mauga॰ 79 aivaṃ sattamīvasaina paripuṇṇavākyampi vuttaṃ| pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘anāpatti chabbassā karauti pārā॰ 564, atiraikachabbassā karautī’’ti pārā॰ 564, ‘‘chabbassānī’’tipi pāṭhau|

rakkhanatthānaṃ yaugai –

yañ‍ca vatthuṃ guttaṃ icchiyatai, yatau ca guttaṃ icchiyatai, tattha pañ‍camī, yavaihi gāvau rakkhati vāraiti, taṇḍulā kākai rakkhati vāraiti, taṃ maṃ puñ‍ñā nivāraisi, pāpā cittaṃ nivārayai dha॰ pa॰ 116, na naṃ jāti nivāraiti, duggatyā garahāya vā su॰ ni॰ 141 (na nai)| rājatau vā cauratau vā ārakkhaṃ gaṇhantu|

antaradhānatthayaugai –

yassa adassanaṃ icchiyati, tasmiṃ pañ‍camī, upajjhāyā antaradhāyati sissau, nilīyatīti atthau| pāḷiyaṃ pana yassa adassanaṃ icchiyati, tasmiṃ chaṭṭhī aiva- ‘‘antaradhāyissāmi samaṇassa  gautamassa, antaradhāyissāmi samaṇassa gautamassā’’ti| ‘‘na sakkhi mai antaradhāyitu’’nti pāḷi, ‘antaradhāyissāmī’ti antaritai acakkhuvisayai ṭhānai attānaṃ ṭhapaissāmītyatthau, nilīyissāmīti vuttaṃ hauti|

yasmiṃ ṭhānai antaradhāyati, tasmiṃ sattamī aiva dissati, atikhippaṃ laukai cakkhu antaradhāyissati dī॰ ni॰ 2.224 (visadisaṃ), jaitavanai antaradhāyitvā, brahmalaukai antaradhāyitvā, maddakucchismiṃ antaradhāyitvā, tatthaivantaradhāyī saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.1 ic‍cādi| ‘‘bhagavatau puratau antaradhāyitvā’’ti aitthapi tausaddau sattamyatthai aiva| ‘‘sak‍kau nimissa rañ‍ñau sammukhai antarahitau’’ti pāḷi| ‘dhajatavanai antaradhāyitvā’ti jaitavanai añ‍ñaisaṃ adassanaṃ katvā, añ‍ñaisaṃ acakkhuvisayaṃ katvāti atthau| ‘‘andhakārau antaradhāyati, ālaukau antaradhāyati, saddhammau antaradhāyati, sāsanaṃ antaradhāyati’’ ic‍cādīsu pana chaṭṭhī, sattamiyau yathāsambhavaṃ vaiditabbā|

dūratthayaugai –

kiṃva dūrau itau gāmau, kac‍ci ārā pamādamhā su॰ ni॰ 156, athau ārā pamādamhā su॰ ni॰ 157, gāmatau avidūrai, ārakā tai maughapurisā imasmā dhammavinayā, ārakā taihi bhagavā, kilaisaihi ārakāti arahaṃ, ārā sau āsavakkhayā dha॰ pa॰ 253| dutiyā ca tatiyā ca chaṭṭhī ca, ārakā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ iminā dhammavinayaina vā, ārakā mandabuddhīnaṃ visuddhi ṭī॰ 1.130|

dūratthai –

dūratauva namassanti, addasā khau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 1.409, kin‍nu tiṭṭhatha ārakā, tasmā tiṭṭhāma ārakā| dutiyā ca tatiyā ca, dūraṃ gāmaṃ āgatau, dūraina gāmaina āgatau, dūrā gāmā āgatau ic‍caivatthau, dūraṃ gāmaina vā|

antikatthayaugai –

antikaṃ gāmā, āsan‍naṃ gāmā, samīpaṃ gāmā| dutiyā ca tatiyā ca chaṭṭhī ca, antikaṃ gāmaṃ, antikaṃ gāmaina, antikaṃ gāmassa|

kāladdhānaṃ parimāṇavacanai –

itau mathurāya catūsu yaujanaisu saṅkassaṃ, rājagahatau pañ‍cacattālīsayaujanai sāvatthi, itau aikanavutikappai dī॰ ni॰ 2.4, itau aikatiṃsai kappai dī॰ ni॰ 2.4, itau sattamai divasai, itau tiṇṇaṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina parinibbāyissāmi dī॰ ni॰ 2.168; udā॰ 51 ic‍cādi|

pamāṇatthai –

āyāmatau ca vitthāratau ca yaujanaṃ, parikkhaipatau navayaujanasataparimāṇau majjhimadaisau parikkhaipaina vā, dīghasau navavidatthiyau pāci॰ 548, yaujanaṃ āyāmaina yaujanaṃ vitthāraina yaujanaṃ ubbaidhaina sāsaparāsi saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.129 ic‍cādi|

tvālaupaipi pañ‍camī| aittha ca tvālaupau nāma paripuṇṇavākyai laddhabbassa tvāntapadassa aparipuṇṇavākyai natthi bhāvau, yañ‍ca padaṃ tvāntapadai sati kammaṃ vā hauti adhikaraṇaṃ vā| taṃ tvāntapadai asati padantarai avadhi hauti, tasmiṃ pañ‍camī, pāsādā vā pāsādaṃ saṅkamaiyya saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.132, hatthikkhandhā vā hatthikkhandhaṃ saṅkamaiyya saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.132 ic‍cādi| aittha ca paṭhamaṃ aikaṃ pāsādaṃ abhirūhitvā puna añ‍ñaṃ pāsādaṃ saṅkamaiyyāti vā paṭhamaṃ aikasmiṃ pāsādai nisīditvā puna añ‍ñaṃ pāsādaṃ saṅkamaiyyāti vā aivaṃ paripuṇṇavākyaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| ‘‘andhakārā vā andhakāraṃ gacchaiyya, tamā vā  tamaṃ gacchaiyyā’’ti saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.132 pāḷi| tathā raṭṭhā raṭṭhaṃ vicarati, gāmā gāmaṃ vicarati, vanā vanaṃ vicarati, vihāratau vihāraṃ gacchati, parivaiṇatau parivaiṇaṃ gacchati, bhavatau bhavaṃ gacchati, kulatau kulaṃ gacchati ic‍cādi| tathā vinayā vinayaṃ pucchati, abhidhammā abhidhammaṃ pucchati, vinayā vinayaṃ kathaiti, abhidhammā abhidhammaṃ kathaiti| aitthapi paṭhamaṃ aikaṃ vinayavacanaṃ pucchitvā vā aikasmiṃ vinayavacanai ṭhatvā vā puna añ‍ñaṃ vinayavacanaṃ pucchatīti paripuṇṇavākyaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| vuttiyaṃ pana ‘‘pāsādaṃ āruyha paikkhati, pāsādā paikkhati, āsanai pavisitvā paikkhati, āsanā paikkhatī’’ti vuttaṃ|

disatthayaugai disatthai ca pañ‍camī, itau sā purimā disā dī॰ ni॰ 3.278, itau sā dakkhiṇā disā dī॰ ni॰ 3.278, avīcitau upari, uddhaṃ pādatalā, adhau kaisamatthakā dī॰ ni॰ 2.377; ma॰ ni॰ 1.110|

disatthai –

purimatau gāmassa, dakkhiṇatau gāmassa, uparitau pabbatassa, haiṭṭhatau pāsādassa, puratthimatau, dakkhiṇatau, yatau khaimaṃ, tatau bhayaṃ jā॰ 1.9.58, yatau yatau sammasati, khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ dha॰ pa॰ 374 ic‍cādi|

pubbādiyaugaipi pañ‍camī, pubbaiva mai sambaudhā a॰ ni॰ 3.104, itau pubbai, tatau pubbai, itau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā mahāva॰ 259, tatau purai, tatau pacchā, tatau uttari pārā॰ 499 ic‍cādi|

vibhattatthai ca pañ‍camī chaṭṭhī ca| vibhatti nāma pagaiva visuṃbhūtassa atthassa kainaci adhikaina vā hīnaina vā bhāgaina tadañ‍ñatau puthak‍karaṇaṃ, māthurā pāṭaliputtakaihi abhirūpatarā, yatau paṇītatarau vā visiṭṭhatarau vā natthi, attadantau tatau varaṃ dha॰ pa॰ 322, chan‍navutīnaṃ pāsaṇḍānaṃ pavaraṃ yadidaṃ sugatavinayau, sadaivakassa laukassa, satthā laukai anuttarau, aggauhamasmi laukassa ma॰ ni॰ 3.207, jaiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa ma॰ ni॰ 3.207, saiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa ma॰ ni॰ 3.207, pañ‍ñavantā nāma sāriputtatau hīnā sāriputtassa vā, tatau adhikaṃ vā ūnaṃ vā na vaṭṭati ic‍cādi|

viramaṇatthayaugai –

āratī viratī pāpā khu॰ pā॰ 5.8, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇi khu॰ pā॰ 2.1 ic‍cādi|

suddhatthayaugai –

laubhanīyaihi dhammaihi suddhau ic‍cādi|

maucanatthayaugai pañ‍camī tatiyā ca, sau parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, parimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.29, maukkhanti mārabandhanā dha॰ pa॰ 37, na tai muc‍canti mac‍cunā ic‍cādi, sabbattha avadhiatthau vaiditabbau|

haitvatthai –

318. pañ‍camīṇai vā ka॰ 296; rū॰ 314; nī॰ 608; caṃ॰ 2.1.69; pā॰ 2.3.24|

319. guṇai caṃ॰ 2.1.70; pā॰ 2.3.25|

ajjhattabhūtau haitu guṇau nāma, aguṇaupi idha guṇautvaiva vuc‍cati, tasmiṃ pañ‍camī hauti vā|

jaḷattā bandhau narau jaḷattaina vā, attanau bālattāyaiva bandhauti atthau, pañ‍ñāya bandhanā muttau, vācāya marati, vācāya muc‍cati, vācāya piyau hauti, vācāya daissau, issariyā janaṃ rakkhati rājā issariyaina vā, sīlatau naṃ pasaṃsanti a॰ ni॰ 4.6 sīlaina vā, hutvā abhāvatau anic‍cā, udayabbayapīḷanatau dukkhā, avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau udā॰ 2, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau udā॰ 2, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ anañ‍ñā appaṭivaidhā dīghamaddhānaṃ saṃsaranti dī॰ ni॰ 2.186 (visadisaṃ) ic‍cādi|

pañhā, kathanaisupi pañ‍camī, kutau bhavaṃ, ahaṃ pāṭaliputtatau ic‍cādi|

thaukatthaipi asatvavacanai pañ‍camī, satvaṃ vuc‍cati dabbaṃ, thaukā muc‍cati thaukaina vā, muc‍canamattaṃ hautīti vuttaṃ hauti ‘‘nadiṃ tarantau manaṃ vuḷhau’’ti mahāva॰ 148 aittha viya| appamattakā muc‍cati appamattakaina vā, kicchā muc‍cati kicchaina vā, kicchā laddhau piyau puttau jā॰ 2.22.353, kicchā muttā’mha dukkhasmā, yāma dāni mahausadha jā॰ 2.22.700|

asatvavacanaiti  kiṃ? pac‍cati muninau bhattaṃ, thaukaṃ thaukaṃ gharai gharaiti thairagā॰ 248 (kulai kulai)|

‘‘anupubbaina maidhāvī, thaukaṃ thaukaṃ khaṇai khaṇai dha॰ pa॰ 239’’ ic‍cādīsu kriyāvisaisanai dutiyā|

akattaripi pañ‍camī, tassa kammassa katattā upacitattā ussan‍nattā vipulattā tathāgatau suppatiṭṭhitapādau hauti dī॰ ni॰ 3.201| aittha ca ‘akattarī’ti akārakai ñāpakahaitumhīti vadanti| ñāsai pana ‘‘akattarīti haitvatthai saṅgaṇhāti| yattha hi kattubuddhi sañ‍jāyatai, sauva kattā na hautīti vattuṃ sak‍kā’’ti vuttaṃ| aitaina kattusadisau janakahaitu akattā nāmāti dīpaiti, kammassa katattāic‍cādi ca janakahaitu aivāti|

bhiyyatthayaugai –

yaudha sītañ‍ca uṇhañ‍ca, tiṇā bhiyyau na mañ‍ñati dī॰ ni॰ 3.253, sukhā bhiyyau saumanassaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 2.287, khantyā bhiyyau na vij‍jati saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.250, mayā bhiyyau na vij‍jati, sautukāmāttha tumhai bhikkhavai bhiyyausaumattāya pubbainivāsakathaṃ, attamanau tvaṃ hauhi paraṃ viya mattāya, ahampi attamanau haumi paraṃ viya mattāya|

pañ‍camīvibhattirāsi niṭṭhitau|

chaṭṭhīvibhattirāsi

kasmiṃ atthai chaṭṭhī?

320. chaṭṭhī sambandhai ka॰ 301; rū॰ 315; nī॰ 609; caṃ॰ 2.1.95; pā॰ 2.3.50|

dvin‍naṃ sambandhīnaṃ kainaci pakāraina āyattabhāvau sambandhau nāma, sambandhai jautaitabbai visaisanasambandhimhi chaṭṭhī hauti|

tattha  kriyākārakasañ‍jātau assaidambhāvahaitukau sambandhau nāmāti vuttaṃ| tattha dvai sambandhinau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ taṃtaṃkriyaṃ karaunti, taṃ disvā ‘‘imai añ‍ñamañ‍ñasambandhinau’’ti jānantassa dvin‍naṃ kārakānaṃ dvin‍naṃ kriyānañ‍ca saṃyaugaṃ nissāya sambandhaupi viditau hauti, aivaṃ sambandhau kriyākārakasañ‍jātau, ‘imassa aya’nti aivaṃ pavattabuddhiyā haitubhūtattā assaidambhāvahaitukau ca|

tattha sambandhau tividhau sāmisambandhau, nānāttasambandhau, kriyākārakasambandhauti|

tattha ‘sāmī’ti yassa kassaci visaisanasambandhinau nāmaṃ, tasmā visaisyapadatthassa taṃtaṃvisaisanabhāvaina sambandhau sāmisambandhau nāma|

sau visaisyapadatthabhaidaina anaikavidhau|

tattha tassa mātā, tassa pitāic‍cādi janakasambandhau nāma|

tassā puttau, tassā dhītā ic‍cādi jañ‍ñasambandhau nāma|

tassa bhātā, tassa bhaginī ic‍cādi kulasambandhau nāma|

sak‍kau daivānamindau saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.248 ic‍cādi sāmisambandhau nāma|

pahūtaṃ mai dhanaṃ sak‍ka jā॰ 1.15.72, bhikkhussa pattacīvaraṃ ic‍cādi saṃsambandhau nāma|

ambavanassa avidūrai, nibbānassaiva santikai dha॰ pa॰ 32 ic‍cādi samīpasambandhau nāma|

suvaṇṇassa rāsi, bhikkhūnaṃ samūhau ic‍cādi samūhasambandhau nāma|

manussassaiva tai sīsaṃ jā॰ 1.4.81, rukkhassa sākhā ic‍cādi avayavasambandhau nāma|

suvaṇṇassa  bhājanaṃ, alābussa kaṭāhaṃ, bhaṭṭhadhañ‍ñānaṃ sattu ic‍cādi vikārasambandhau nāma|

yavassa aṅkurau, maighassa saddau, pupphānaṃ gandhau, phalānaṃ rasau, aggissa dhūmau ic‍cādi kāriyasambandhau nāma|

khandhānaṃ jāti, khandhānaṃ jarā, khandhānaṃ bhaidau saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.1 ic‍cādi avatthāsambandhau nāma|

suvaṇṇassa vaṇṇau, vaṇṇau na khīyyaitha tathāgatassa dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.304, buddhassa kittisaddau, sippikānaṃ sataṃ natthi jā॰ 1.1.113, tilānaṃ muṭṭhi ic‍cādi guṇasambandhau nāma|

pādassa ukkhipanaṃ, hatthassa samiñ‍janaṃ, dhātūnaṃ gamanaṃ ṭhānaṃ ic‍cādi kriyāsambandhau nāma|

cātumahārājikānaṃ ṭhānaṃ ic‍cādi ṭhānasambandhau nāma| aivamādinā nayaina sāmisambandhau anaikasahassappabhaidau, sau ca kriyāsambandhābhāvā kārakau nāma na hauti| yadi aivaṃ ‘‘pādassa ukkhipanaṃ’’ ic‍cādi kriyāsambandhau nāmāti idaṃ na yuj‍jatīti? vuc‍catai – kriyāsambandhābhāvāti idaṃ sādhakabhāvaina sambandhābhāvaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ, siddhāya pana kriyāya sambandhaṃ sandhāya kriyāsambandhau nāma vuttauti|

nānāttasambandhai pana nānāatthaisu chaṭṭhī hauti| tattha ṇī, āvīpac‍cayānaṃ kammai nic‍caṃ chaṭṭhī, jhānassa lābhī, cīvarassa lābhī, dhanassa lābhī, ādīnavassa dassāvī, atthi rūpānaṃ dassāvī, atthi samavisamassa dassāvī, atthi tārakarūpānaṃ dassāvī, atthi candimasūriyānaṃ dassāvī|

tu, aka, ana, ṇapac‍cayānaṃ yaugai k‍vaci kammatthai chaṭṭhī|

tupac‍cayai tāva –

tassa  bhavanti vattārau ma॰ ni॰ 2.173, sahasā kammassa kattārau, amatassa dātā ma॰ ni॰ 1.203, bhin‍nānaṃ sandhātā dī॰ ni॰ 1.9, 164, sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā dī॰ ni॰ 1.9, 164 ic‍cādi|

k‍vacīti kiṃ? gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā a॰ ni॰ 7.37, gādhaṃ kattā nauvasitā a॰ ni॰ 4.107, kālaina dhammīkathaṃ bhāsitā, sarasi tvaṃ aivarūpaṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā, paraisaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ anumaudaitā, bujjhitā sac‍cāni mahāni॰ 192 ic‍cādi|

akapac‍cayai –

kammassa kārakau natthi, vipākassa ca vaidakau visuddhi 2.689, avisaṃvādakau laukassa dī॰ ni॰ 1.9 ic‍cādi|

k‍vacīti kiṃ? mahatiṃ mahiṃ anusāsakau, janaṃ ahaiṭhakau, kaṭaṃ kārakau, pasavau ghātakau ic‍cādi|

anapac‍cayai –

pāpassa akaraṇaṃ sukhaṃ dha॰ pa॰ 61, bhārassa ukkhipanaṃ, hatthassa gahaṇaṃ, hatthassa parāmasanaṃ, añ‍ñatarassa aṅgassa parāmasanaṃ pārā॰ 270 ic‍cādi|

k‍vacīti kiṃ? bhagavantaṃ dassanāya udā॰ 23 ic‍cādi|

ṇapac‍cayai –

acchariyau arajakaina vatthānaṃ rāgau, agaupālakaina gāvīnaṃ dauhau, appapuñ‍ñaina lābhānaṃ lābhau, hatthassa gāhau, pattassa paṭiggāhau ic‍cādi|

tvāpac‍cayaipi  k‍vaci kammani chaṭṭhī, alaj‍jīnaṃ nissāya, āyasmatau nissāya vacchāmi, catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau dha॰ sa॰ 596-599 (upādāya) ic‍cādi|

kattari ta, tavantu, tāvī, māna’ntānaṃ yaugai pana kammani dutiyā aiva, sukhakāmau vihāraṃ katau, gāmaṃ gatau, audanaṃ bhuttavā bhuttāvī, kammaṃ kurumānau, kammaṃ karauntau ic‍cādi|

k‍vaci chaṭṭhīpi dissati, dhammassa guttau maidhāvī dha॰ pa॰ 257 ic‍cādi|

sara, isu, cinta, isa, dayadhātūnaṃ kammani chaṭṭhī vā, mātussa sarati, mātaraṃ sarati, pitussa sarati, pitaraṃ sarati, na raj‍jassa sarissasi jā॰ 2.22.1721, na taisaṃ kauci sarati, sattānaṃ kammapac‍cayā khu॰ pā॰ 7.2, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati cūḷava॰ 157, puttassa icchati puttaṃ vā, mātussa cintaiti mātaraṃ vā, thairassa ajjhaisati thairaṃ vā, tailassa dayati tailaṃ vā, rakkhatīti atthau|

karadhātussa abhisaṅkharaṇatthavācinau kammai chaṭṭhī, udakassa paṭikurutai udakaṃ vā, kaṇḍassa paṭikurutai kaṇḍaṃ vā ic‍cādi|

tapac‍cayai pūjanatthādidhātūnaṃ kattari chaṭṭhī vā, rañ‍ñau sammatau rañ‍ñā vā, gāmassa pūjitau gāmaina vā, rañ‍ñau sak‍katau rañ‍ñā vā, rañ‍ñau apacitau rañ‍ñā vā, rañ‍ñau mānitau dī॰ ni॰ 1.303 rañ‍ñā vā, tathā suppaṭividdhā buddhānaṃ dhammadhātu, amataṃ taisaṃ paribhuttaṃ, yaisaṃ kāyagatāsati paribhuttā a॰ ni॰ 1.603, amataṃ taisaṃ viraddhaṃ, yaisaṃ kāyagatāsati viraddhā a॰ ni॰ 1.603|

tipac‍cayaipi  k‍vaci kattari chaṭṭhī vā, saubhaṇā kac‍cāyanassa pakati kac‍cāyanaina vā, saubhaṇā buddhaghausassa pakati buddhaghausaina vā ic‍cādi|

pūjanatthānaṃ pūraṇatthānañ‍ca karaṇai chaṭṭhī, pupphassa buddhaṃ pūjaiti pupphaina vā, ghatassa aggiṃ juhauti ghataina vā, pattaṃ udakassa pūraitvā, pūraṃ nānāppakārassa asucinau dī॰ ni॰ 2.377, bālau pūrati pāpassa dha॰ pa॰ 121, dhīrau pūrati puñ‍ñassa dha॰ pa॰ 121 pūrati dhañ‍ñānaṃ vā muggānaṃ vā māsānaṃ vā ic‍cādi| tatiyā vā, khaimā nāma paukkharaṇī, puṇṇā haṃsaihi tiṭṭhati|

tabba, rujādiyaugai pana sampadānai catutthī aiva, yakkhasaināpatīnaṃ ujjhāpaitabbaṃ vik‍kanditabbaṃ viravitabbaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 3.283 (visadisaṃ), daivadattassa ruj‍jati, rajakassa vatthaṃ dadāti ic‍cādi|

bhayatthādīnaṃ apādānai bahulaṃ chaṭṭhī, kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ tassa sukhassa bhāyāmi, sabbai tasanti daṇḍassa, sabbai bhāyanti mac‍cunau dha॰ pa॰ 129, bhītau catun‍naṃ āsīvisānaṃ saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.238, mā bhikkhavai puñ‍ñānaṃ bhāyittha udā॰ 22, saṅkhātuṃ naupi sak‍kaumi, musāvādassa auttapaṃ saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.184 ic‍cādi| tattha ‘auttapa’nti auttappantau| tathā aggauhamasmi laukassa, jaiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa ma॰ ni॰ 3.207 ic‍cādi ca|

kusala, kauvida, pasādatthānaṃ ādhārai chaṭṭhī, kusalā nac‍cagītassa jā॰ 2.22.94, kusalau tvaṃ rathassa aṅgapac‍caṅgānaṃ ma॰ ni॰ 2.87, amac‍cai tāta jānāhi, dhīrai atthassa kauvidai jā॰ 1.17.13, narā dhammassa kauvidā jā॰ 1.1.37, maggāmaggassa kauvidā, ‘‘kaici iddhīsu kauvidā’’tipi atthi, santi yakkhā buddhassa pasan‍nā dī॰ ni॰ 3.276 (visadisaṃ), dhammassa pasan‍nā, saṅghassa pasan‍nā, buddhai pasan‍nā, dhammai pasan‍nā, saṅghai pasan‍nā vā| tathā  caitaupariyañāṇassa, vasī haumi mahāmuni| jhānassa vasimhi ic‍cādi|

321. chaṭṭhī haitvatthaihi rū॰ 163 piṭṭhai; nī॰ 650; caṃ॰ 2.1.71; pā॰ 2.3.26|
