taṃ kissa haitu ma॰ ni॰ 1.2; caṃ॰ 2.1.96; pā॰ 2.3.72, aṅgavarassa haitu, udarassa haitu, udarassa kāraṇā pārā॰ 228 ic‍cādi|

322. tulyatthaina vā tatiyā nī॰ 638|

tulyatthaina yaugai chaṭṭhī hauti tatiyā vā|

tulyau pitu pitarā vā, sadisau pitu pitarā vā| iti nānāttasambandhau|

kriyākārakasambandhau nāma kārakānaṃ kriyāya saha sādhaka, sādhyabhāvaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñāpaikkhatā avinābhāvitā vuc‍cati, na hi kriyaṃ vinā kārakaṃ nāma sijjhati, na ca kārakaṃ vinā kriyā nāma sijjhatīti, sā pana chaṭṭhīvisayau na hautīti|

chaṭṭhīvibhattirāsi niṭṭhitau|

sattamīvibhattirāsi

kasmiṃ atthai sattamī?

323. sattamyādhārai ka॰ 302; rū॰ 313; nī॰ 630; caṃ॰ 2.1.88; pā॰ 1.3.45|

ādhārau, aukāsau, adhikaraṇanti atthatau aikaṃ, ādhāratthai sattamī hauti| kattukammaṭṭhaṃ kriyaṃ bhusau dhāraitīti ādhārau|

kaṭai  nisīdati purisau, thāliyaṃ audanaṃ pacati| tattha kaṭau kattubhūtai purisai ṭhitaṃ nisīdanakriyaṃ dhāraiti, thālī kammabhūtai taṇḍulai ṭhitaṃ pacanakriyaṃ dhāraiti|

sau catubbidhau byāpikādhārau, aupasilaisikādhārau, sāmīpikādhārau, vaisayikādhārauti|

tattha yasmiṃ ādhaiyyavatthu sakalai vā aikadaisai vā byāpaitvā tiṭṭhati, sau byāpikau| yathā? tilaisu tailaṃ tiṭṭhati, ucchūsu rasau tiṭṭhati, jalaisu khīraṃ tiṭṭhati, dadhimhi sappi tiṭṭhatīti|

yasmiṃ ādhaiyyavatthu al‍līyitvā vā tiṭṭhati, adhiṭṭhitamattaṃ hutvā vā tiṭṭhati, sau aupasilaisikau| yathā? ukkhaliyaṃ ācāmau tiṭṭhati, ghaṭaisu udakaṃ tiṭṭhati, āsanai nisīdati bhikkhu, pariyaṅkai rājā saiti|

yau pana atthau ādhaiyyassa avatthubhūtaupi tadāyattavuttidīpanatthaṃ ādhārabhāvaina vauhariyati, sau sāmīpikau nāma| yathā? gaṅgāyaṃ ghausau tiṭṭhati, sāvatthiyaṃ viharati bhagavāti a॰ ni॰ 1.1|

yau ca atthau attanā vinā ādhaiyyassa añ‍ñatthattaṃ kriyaṃ sampādaituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyattā ādhārabhāvaina vauhariyati, yau ca ādhaiyyassa anañ‍ñābhimukhabhāvadīpanatthaṃ ādhārabhāvaina vauhariyati, sau vaisayikau nāma| yathā? ākāsai sakuṇā pakkhanti, bhūmīsu manussā caranti, udakai macchā caranti, bhagavantaṃ pādaisu vandati, pādaisu patitvā raudati, pāpasmiṃ ramatī manau dha॰ pa॰ 116, pasan‍nau buddhasāsanaiti dha॰ pa॰ 368|

324. nimittai ka॰ 310; rū॰ 324; nī॰ 641; caṃ॰ 2.1.89; pā॰ 2.3.36|

niminanti sañ‍jānanti aitaināti nimittaṃ, naimittakasahabhāvinau sañ‍ñāṇakāraṇassaitaṃ nāmaṃ, tasmiṃ nimittai sattamī hauti|

dīpi cammaisu hañ‍ñatai, kuñ‍jarau dantaisu hañ‍ñatai, musāvādai pācittiyaṃ pāci॰ 2, aumasavādai pācittiyaṃ pāci॰ 14 ic‍cādi|

325. yambhāvau bhāvalakkhaṇaṃ ka॰ 313; rū॰ 327; nī॰ 644; caṃ॰ 2.1.90; pā॰ 2.3.37; ‘yabbhā vau’ (bahūsu)|

yādisau bhāvau yambhāvau, lakkhiyati aitaināti lakkhaṇaṃ, bhāvantarassa lakkhaṇaṃ bhāvalakkhaṇaṃ, yambhāvau bhāvantarassa lakkhaṇaṃ hauti, tasmiṃ bhāvai gamyamānai sattamī hauti, chaṭṭhīpi dissati|

acirapak‍kantassa sāriputtassa brāhmaṇau kālamakāsi ma॰ ni॰ 2.452 (visadisaṃ), appamattassa tai viharatau itthāgāraupi tai appamattau viharissati saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.129 (visadisaṃ) ic‍cādi|

imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.21, acirapak‍kantai bhagavati brāhmaṇau kālamakāsi, sabbai maggā vivaj‍janti, gacchantai laukanāyakai ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.22| gāvīsu duyhamānāsu gatau, gāvīsu duddhāsu āgatau ic‍cādi|

k‍vaci  paṭhamāpi bahulaṃ dissati, gacchantau sau bhāradvājau, addasā ac‍cutaṃ isiṃ jā॰ 2.22.2007 (addassa)| yāyamānau mahārājā, addā sīdantarai nagai jā॰ 2.22.566 ic‍cādi|

pubbaṇhasamayai gatau, sāyanhasamayai āgatau ic‍cādi vaisayikādhārau aiva|

tathā akālai vassatī tassa, kālai tassa na vassati jā॰ 1.2.88; 1.8.48| itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau apa॰ thaira 2.54.28 ic‍cādi|

326. chaṭṭhī cānādarai ka॰ 305; rū॰ 323; nī॰ 633; caṃ॰ 2.1.91; pā॰ 2.3.38|

‘anādarau’ti dvin‍naṃ lakkhaṇa, lakkhitabbakriyānaṃ aikappahāraina pavattiyā adhivacanaṃ, anādarabhūtai bhāvalakkhaṇai gamyamānai sattamī chaṭṭhī ca hauti|

mac‍cu gacchati ādāya, paikkhamānai mahājanai| ākauṭayantau sau naiti, sivirājassa paikkhatau jā॰ 2.22.2122 (tainaiti)| akāmakānaṃ mātāpitūnaṃ rudantānaṃ pabbaji, anagāriyupaitassa, vippamuttassa tai satau| samaṇassa na taṃ sādhu, yadañ‍ñamanusaucati jā॰ 1.7.107 (yaṃ paitamanusaucasi)|

327. yatau niddhāraṇaṃ ka॰ 304; rū॰ 322; nī॰ 632; caṃ॰ 2.1.92; pā॰ 2.3.41|

jāti, guṇa, kriyā, nāmaihi samudāyatau aikadaisassa puthak‍karaṇaṃ niddhāraṇaṃ, yatau taṃ niddhāraṇaṃ jāyati, tasmiṃ samudāyai chaṭṭhī, sattamiyau haunti|

jātiyaṃ  tāva –

manussānaṃ khattiyau sūratamau, manussaisu khattiyau sūratamau|

guṇai –

kaṇhā gāvīnaṃ sampan‍nakhīratamā, kaṇhāgāvīsu sampan‍nakhīratamā|

kriyāyaṃ –

addhikānaṃ dhāvantau sīghatamau, addhikaisu dhāvantau sīghatamau|

nāmai –

āyasmā ānandau arahataṃ añ‍ñatarau, arahantaisu añ‍ñatarau ic‍cādi|

idha nānāttasattamī vuc‍catai|

kammatthai sattamī, bhikkhūsu abhivādainti pārā॰ 517, puttaṃ muddhani cumbitvā, purisaṃ nānābāhāsu gahaitvā saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.63 ic‍cādi|

atha vā ‘muddhani, bāhāsū’ti ādhārai aiva bhummaṃ| yathā? rukkhaṃ mūlai chindati, rukkhaṃ khandhai chindati, purisaṃ sīsai paharati, bhagavantaṃ pādaisu vandati|

karaṇai ca sattamī, hatthaisu piṇḍāya caranti mahāva॰ 119, pattaisu piṇḍāya caranti, pathaisu gacchanti, saupi maṃ anusāsaiyya, sampaṭicchāmi matthakai mi॰ pa॰ 6.4.8|

sampadānai ca sattamī, saṅghai din‍nai mahapphalaṃ, saṅghai gautamī dadaiyyāsi, saṅghai din‍nai ahañ‍caiva pūjitau bhavissāmi  ma॰ ni॰ 3.376, vicaiyya dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, yattha din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ pai॰ va॰ 329| aitaisu pana visayasattamīpi yuj‍jati|

apādānai ca sattamī, gadalīsu gajai rakkhantiic‍cādi|

sāmissarādiyaugai pana chaṭṭhī sattamī ca hauti, gun‍naṃ sāmi, gausu sāmi, gun‍naṃ issarau, gausu issarau, gun‍naṃ adhipati, gausu adhipati, gun‍naṃ dāyādau, gausu dāyādau, gun‍naṃ sakkhi, gausu sakkhi, gun‍naṃ patibhū, gausu patibhū, gun‍naṃ pasutau, gausu pasutau, āyuttau kaṭakaraṇassa, āyuttau kaṭakaraṇaiti, aitaisu pana sambandhai chaṭṭhī, visayādhārai sattamī| ñāṇasmiṃ pasan‍nau, ñāṇasmiṃ ussuk‍kauti visayādhārai sattamī| ñāṇaina pasan‍nau, ñāṇaina ussuk‍kauti karaṇai tatiyā|

328. sattamyādhikyai ka॰ 314; rū॰ 328; nī॰ 645; caṃ॰ 2.1.60; pā॰ 2.3.9; 1.4.87|

adhikabhāvatthai upaina yuttā liṅgamhā sattamī hauti|

upa khāriyaṃ dauṇau, upa nikkhai kahāpaṇaṃ, atiraikadauṇā khārī, atiraikakahāpaṇaṃ nikkhanti vuttaṃ hauti|

329. sāmittaidhinā caṃ॰ 2.1.61; pā॰ 2.3.9; 1.4.97|

sāmibhāvatthai adhinā yuttā liṅgamhā sattamī hauti|

adhi brahmadattai pañ‍cālā, adhi pañ‍cālaisu brahmadattau, adhi daivaisu buddhau| tattha ‘adhi brahmadattai pañ‍cālā’ti brahmadattissarā pañ‍cālaraṭṭhavāsinauti vadanti, ‘pañ‍cālā’ti vā janapadanāmattā bahuvacanaṃ, kadāci pañ‍cālarājā brahmadattai kāsirañ‍ñai issarau, kadāci brahmadattau pañ‍cālarañ‍ñai issarauti atthau|

330. sabbāditau  sabbā caṃ॰ 2.1.72; pā॰ 2.3.27|

haitvatthaihi yaugai sabbādīhi sabbanāmaihi haitvatthai sabbā vibhattiyau haunti|

kiṃ kāraṇaṃ, kaina kāraṇaina jā॰ aṭṭha॰ 4.15 mātaṅgajātakavaṇṇanā, kiṃ nimittaṃ, kaina nimittaina, kiṃ payaujanaṃ, kaina payaujanaina, kainaṭṭhaina dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathā, kaina vaṇṇaina saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.234, kimatthaṃ, kutau nidānaṃ pārā॰ 42, kissa haitu pārā॰ 39, kasmiṃ nidānai, aitasmiṃ nidānai pārā॰ 42, aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai pārā॰ 42 ic‍cādi|

sattamīvibhattirāsi niṭṭhitau|

iti niruttidīpaniyā nāma mauggal‍lānadīpaniyā

kārakakaṇḍau niṭṭhitau|

4. samāsakaṇḍa

atha  yuttatthānaṃ syādyantapadānaṃ aikatthībhāvau vuc‍catai| aikatthībhāvauti ca idha samāsau vuc‍cati| sau ca samāsau chabbidhau abyayībhāvau, tappurisau, kammadhārayau, digu, bahubbīhi, dvandauti|

abyayībhāvasamāsa

tattha abyayībhāvau paṭhamaṃ vuc‍catai| byayau vuc‍cati vikārau, natthi byayau aitassāti abyayau, abyayau hutvā bhavatīti abyayībhāvau, nānāliṅga, vibhatti, vacanaisu rūpavikārarahitau hutvā bhavatīti atthau, sabbaliṅga,-vibhatti, vacanaisupi yaibhuyyaina aikarūpaina pavattatīti vuttaṃ hauti|

abyayanti vā upasagganipātānaṃ aiva nāmaṃ, ayaṃ pana pakati abyayaṃ na hauti, asaṅkhyaihi saha aikatthatāvasaina abyayaṃ hauti, iti anabyayampi abyayaṃ bhavatīti abyayībhāvau|

331. syādisyādinaikatthaṃ ka॰ 316; rū॰ 331; nī॰ 675; caṃ॰ 2.2.1; pā॰ 2.1.4|

adhikārasuttamidaṃ| syādi vuc‍cati syādyantapadaṃ, ‘syādinā’ti syādyantapadaina, aikau atthau yassa taṃ aikatthaṃ, syādipadaṃ syādipadaina saha aikatthaṃ hautīti atthau|

aittha ca ‘syādī’ti vacanaina upasagga, nipātaihi saddhiṃ sabbāni nāmikapadāni nāmapaṭirūpakāni ca saṅgaṇhāti, tyādyantapadāni nivattaiti|

tattha  nāmapaṭirūpakāni nāma ‘yaivāvanakadhammā’ ic‍cādīni| tathā sañ‍ñāsaddabhāvaṃ pattāni ‘‘atthipac‍cayau, natthipac‍cayau, atthikhīrā brāhmaṇī, añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñau, makkhaligausālau’’ ic‍cādīsu ‘atthi’ ic‍cādīni|

‘aikattha’nti aitaina dvandasamāsaipi padānaṃ aikakattu, aikakammādibhāvaina aikatthībhāvau vuttau hautīti|

332. asaṅkhyaṃ vibhattisampattisamīpasākalyābhāvayathāpacchāyugapadatthai ka॰ 319; rū॰ 330; nī॰ 696; caṃ॰ 2.2.2; pā॰ 2.1.6|

‘asaṅkhya’nti upasaggapadaṃ nipātapadañ‍ca vuc‍cati| taṃ dvayampi hi aikatta, bahuttasaṅkhyaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpavikārarahitattā ‘asaṅkhya’nti vuc‍cati| vibhatyatthai, sampatyatthai, samīpatthai, sākalyatthai, abhāvatthai, yathātthai, pacchātthai, yugapadatthai pavattaṃ asaṅkhyaṃ nāma syādipadaṃ añ‍ñaina syādipadaina saha aikatthaṃ hauti| ayañ‍ca samāsau anvatthavasaina ‘asaṅkhyau’ti ca ‘abyayībhāvau’ti ca vuc‍cati|

vibhatyatthai tāva –

adhitthi| aittha ca adhitau si, tassa ‘asaṅkhyaihi sabbāsa’nti suttaina laupau, itthitau su, ‘adhi itthīsū’ti vākyaṃ, tassa ca atthaṃ kathaintaina nic‍casamāsattā añ‍ñapadaina viggahau kātabbau ‘‘itthīsu pavattā kathā’’ti vā ‘‘itthīsu pavattau vacanapathau’’ti vā ‘‘itthīsu pavattaṃ vacana’’nti vā, tatau purimasuttaina aikatthasañ‍ñā, iminā suttaina asaṅkhyaikatthasañ‍ñā ca kariyatai, aikatthasañ‍ñāya pana katāya vākyatthāya payuttānaṃ vibhattīnaṃ atthau aikatthapadaina vuttau hauti, tadā vibhattiyau vuttatthā nāma|

idāni  vuttatthānaṃ appayaugārahattā laupavidhānamāha|

333. aikatthatāyaṃ ka॰ 316; rū॰ 331; nī॰ 675; caṃ॰ 2.1.39; pā॰ 2.4.71; 1.2.45, 46|

aikau atthau yaisaṃ tāni aikatthāni, ‘atthau’ti caittha padantarai kattu, kammādibhāvaina vidhaiyyau padhānatthau aiva vaiditabbau| tathā hi ‘rājaputtau’ti aittha puttasaddatthau aiva tathāvidhaiyyau hauti, na rājasaddatthau, sabbañ‍ca vacanavākyaṃ nāma vidhaiyyatthaihi aiva sijjhati, nau añ‍ñathā, yasmā ca ‘rājaputtau’ti aitaṃ puttasaddatthassaiva nāmaṃ hauti, na rājasaddatthassa, tasmā aikau padhānabhūtau puttasaddatthau aiva taisaṃ dvin‍naṃ saddānaṃ atthau nāma hauti, na rājasaddatthauti, aikatthānaṃ bhāvau aikatthatā, aikatthībhāvauti vuttaṃ hauti| sau tividhau samāsau, taddhitau, dhātupac‍cayantau cāti| tissaṃ tividhāyaṃ aikatthatāyaṃ sabbāsaṃ vuttatthānaṃ syādivibhattīnaṃ laupau hautīti iminā sussa laupau| bahulādhikārattā pana aluttasamāsaupi dissati|

334. taṃ napuṃsakaṃ ka॰ 320; rū॰ 335; nī॰ 698; caṃ॰ 2.2.15; pā॰ 2.4.18|

taṃ asaṅkhyaṃ nāma aikatthaṃ napuṃsakaṃ hautīti iminā adhitthīsaddassa napuṃsakabhāvaṃ katvā tatau syādyuppatti|

335. syādīsu rassau ka॰ 342; rū॰ 337; nī॰ 734; caṃ॰ 2.2.84; pā॰ 1.2.47|

napuṃsakassa aikatthassa rassau hauti syādīsu vibhattīsūti iminā īkārassa rassau|

336. pubbasmāmāditau ka॰ 343; rū॰ 338; nī॰ 375; caṃ॰ 2.1.40; pā॰ 1.1.41|

pubbaamādi  nāma pubbapadatthapadhānabhūtau asaṅkhyasamāsau vuc‍cati, tatau parāsaṃ sabbāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupau hauti, ādisaddaina caittha paṭhamāvibhattipi gayhati| atha vā amādi vuc‍cati tappurisau, tatau pubbaṃ nāma asaṅkhyasamāsau, iti amāditau pubbabhūtā asaṅkhyaikatthā parāsaṃ sabbāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupau hautīti iminā adhitthisaddatau sabbavibhattīnaṃ laupau|

adhitthi tiṭṭhati, itthīsu pavattā kathā tiṭṭhatīti atthau| adhitthi tiṭṭhanti, itthīsu pavattā kathāyau tiṭṭhantīti atthau| aisa nayau saisavibhattīsu saisavacanaisu saisaliṅgaisu ca| aivaṃ sabbaliṅgaisu sabbavibhattīsu sabbavacanaisu ca aikainaiva rūpaina tiṭṭhati, tasmā ayaṃ samāsau rūpavikārarahitattā ‘abyayībhāvau’ti vuc‍cati|

aittha ca vibhatyatthau nāma ‘‘adhitthi, bahigāmaṃ, uparigaṅga’’ mic‍cādīsu sampatyādīhi visaisatthaihi rahitau kaivalau vibhattīnaṃ atthau vuc‍cati| viggahai pana ‘‘kathā, pavattā’’ ic‍cādīni samāsasāmatthiyaina viditāni atthapadāni nāma, adhisaddassa atthapadānītipi vadanti| aivaṃ adhikumāri, adhivadhu, adhijambuic‍cādi|

attani pavattau dhammau, pavattā vādhammāti atthai vibhattīnaṃ laupai katai adhiattasaddassa napuṃsakabhāvaṃ katvā tatau syādyuppatti, ‘pubbasmāmāditau’ti syādīnaṃ laupai sampattai –

337. nātaumapañ‍camiyā ka॰ 341; rū॰ 336; nī॰ 733; caṃ॰ 2.1.41; pā॰ 2.4.83; mu॰ 4.3.374|

akārantamhā  asaṅkhyaikatthā paraṃ sabbāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupau na hauti, pañ‍camīvaj‍jitānaṃ vibhattīnaṃ aṃ hauti|

ajjhattaṃ dhammau jāyati, ajjhattaṃ dhammā jāyanti, ajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ passati, ajjhattaṃ dhammai passanti|

apañ‍camiyāti kiṃ? ajjhattā apaiti, ajjhattaihi apaiti|

338. vā tatiyāsattamīnaṃ ka॰ 341; rū॰ 336; nī॰ 733; caṃ॰ 2.1.42; pā॰ 2.4.84; mu॰ 4.3.375|

akārantamhā asaṅkhyaikatthā paraṃ tatiyā, sattamīnaṃ vikappaina aṃ hauti|

ajjhattaṃ dhammaina vattati ajjhattaina vā, ajjhattaṃ dhammaihi vattati ajjhattaihi vā, ajjhattaṃ dhammassa daiti, ajjhattaṃ dhammānaṃ daiti, ajjhattā dhammā apaiti, ajjhattaihi dhammaihi apaiti, ajjhattaṃ dhammassa santakaṃ, ajjhattaṃ dhammānaṃ santakaṃ, ajjhattaṃ dhammai tiṭṭhati ajjhattai vā, ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu tiṭṭhati ajjhattaisu vā| aittha ca ‘ajjhattaṃ dhammau’ti ajjhattabhūtau dhammau, ‘ajjhattaṃ dhammā’ti ajjhattabhūtā dhammā ic‍cādinā atthau vaiditabbau| attānaṃ adhikic‍ca pavattau pavattāti vā vuttaipi attassa ādhārabhāvau sijjhatiyaiva| aivaṃ adhicittaṃ, attani visuṃ visuṃ pavattaṃ pavattāni vā pac‍cattaṃ| aittha ca ‘‘ajjhattaṃ abhinivisitvā ajjhattaṃ vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā abhinivisitvā ajjhattaṃ vuṭṭhātī’’ti dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ 350; saṃ॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.2.32 pāṭhau atthi, tasmā pañ‍camiyā aṃbhāvavaj‍janaṃ appakattāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ‘‘ajjhattā dhammā, bahiddhā  dhammā’’ti dha॰ sa॰ tikamātikā 20 pāṭhau atthi, tasmā paṭhamādīnampi vikappau labbhatīti|

sampattiatthai –

sampan‍naṃ brahmaṃ sabrahmaṃ, ‘brahma’nti vaidau vuc‍cati| aittha ca ‘akālai sakatthassā’ti suttaina sahasaddassa sādaisau, bhikkhānaṃ samiddhi subhikkhaṃ, ‘syādīsu rassau’ti suttaina katanapuṃsakassa rassattaṃ|

samīpai –

nagarassa samīpaṃ upanagaraṃ, kumbhassa samīpaṃ upakumbhaṃ, maṇikāya samīpaṃ upamaṇikaṃ, vadhuyā samīpaṃ upavadhu, gun‍naṃ samīpaṃ upagu, ‘gaussū’ti suttaina aussa uttaṃ|

sākalyai –

tiṇaina saha sakalaṃ satiṇaṃ, tiṇaina saddhiṃ sakalaṃ vatthuṃ ajjhauharatīti atthau| sahasaddassa sādaisau|

abhāvai –

makkhikānaṃ abhāvau nimmakkhikaṃ, darathānaṃ abhāvau niddarathaṃ, bhikkhānaṃ abhāvau dubbhikkhaṃ, abhāvatthaupi dusaddau atthi| yathā? dussīlau duppañ‍ñauti| aittha ca ‘sampan‍naṃ brahma’ntiādinā saddabyākaraṇaisu atthavacanaṃ saddatthavibhāvanamattaṃ| suttantaisu pana imaisaṃ padānaṃ yuttaṃ abhidhaiyyatthaṃ ñatvā tadanurūpaṃ atthavacanampi vaiditabbaṃ|

yathāsaddatthai –

rūpassa sabhāvassa yaugyaṃ anurūpaṃ, attānaṃ attānaṃ paṭic‍ca pavattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ, aḍḍhamāsaṃ aḍḍhamāsaṃ anugataṃ anvaḍḍhamāsaṃ, gharaṃ gharaṃ anugataṃ anugharaṃ, vassaṃ vassaṃ anugataṃ anuvassaṃ, jaiṭṭhānaṃ  anupubbaṃ anujaiṭṭhaṃ, sattiyā anurūpaṃ yathāsatti, balassa anurūpaṃ yathābalaṃ, kamassa anurūpaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ| aivaṃ yathāsaṅkhyaṃ, yathālābhaṃ| sautassa paṭilaumaṃ paṭisautaṃ, paṭivātaṃ, paṭisaddaṃ|

pacchāpadatthai –

rathassa pacchā anurathaṃ|

yugabhūtau padatthau yugapadatthau, sahabhāvīatthadvayassaitaṃ nāmaṃ| tattha asaniphalaina saha pavattaṃ cak‍kaṃ sacak‍kaṃ, gadāvudhaina yugaḷapavattaṃ vāsudaivassa cak‍kāvudhantipi vadanti yugapadatthai sacak‍kaṃ nidhaihi, (mauggal‍lānavuttiyaṃ)| cak‍kaina yugapata dhaihi sacak‍kaṃ, (mugdhabaudhavuttiyaṃ)| hai bisaṇu! cak‍kaina saha yugapadaikakālai gadaṃ dhāraya| (mugdhabaudhaṭīkāyaṃ 226 piṭṭhai), sahassa sattaṃ|

339. yathā natulyai ka॰ 319; rū॰ 330; nī॰ 696; caṃ॰ 2.2.3; pā॰ 2.1.7|

tulyatau añ‍ñasmiṃ atthai pavattau yathāsaddau syādinā saha aikatthau hauti mauggal‍lānai añ‍ñathāvutti dassitā|

yathāsatti, yathābalaṃ, yathāk‍kamaṃ, yai yai vuḍḍhā yathāvuḍḍhaṃ, vuḍḍhānaṃ paṭipāṭi vā yathāvuḍḍhaṃ|

natulyaiti kiṃ? yathā daivadattau, tathā yañ‍ñadattau|

340. yāvāvadhāraṇai ka॰ 319; rū॰ 330; nī॰ 696; caṃ॰ 2.2.4; pā॰ 2.1.8|

avadhāraṇaṃ vuc‍cati paricchindanaṃ, avadhāraṇai pavattau yāvasaddau syādinā saha aikatthau bhavati|

yattakaṃ  atthau vattatīti yāvadatthaṃ, dāgamau| yattakaṃ jīvau vattatīti yāvajīvaṃ, yattakaṃ āyu vattatīti yāvatāyukaṃ, takāra, kakārā āgamā|

341. parāpābahitiraupuraipacchā vā pañ‍camyā ka॰ 319; rū॰ 330; nī॰ 696; caṃ॰ 2.2.7; pā॰ 2.1.12, 13; ‘payyapā…’ (bahūsu)|

pari, apa, āic‍cādayau saddā pañ‍camyantaina syādinā saha aikatthā bhavanti vā|

pabbatatau pari samantā vassīti daivau paripabbataṃ paripabbatā vā, pabbataṃ vaj‍jaitvā vassīti atthau| pabbatatau bahiddhā apapabbataṃ apapabbatā vā, pāṭaliputtatau bahiddhā vassīti daivau āpāṭaliputtaṃ āpāṭaliputtā vā, ākumāraihi kac‍cāyanassa yasau vattatīti ākumāraṃ ākumārā vā, ābhavaggā bhagavatau yasau vattatīti ābhavaggaṃ ābhavaggā vā, āpāṇakauṭiyā saraṇagamanaṃ vattatīti āpāṇakauṭikaṃ, kāgamau| gāmatau bahi bahigāmaṃ bahigāmā vā, aivaṃ bahinagaraṃ, bahilaiṇaṃ, pabbatatau tirau tiraupabbataṃ tiraupabbatā vā, aivaṃ tiraupākāraṃ, tiraukuṭṭaṃ| aittha ca ‘tirau’ti parabhāgau vuc‍cati| bhattamhā purai puraibhattaṃ puraibhattā vā, aruṇamhā purai purāruṇaṃ purāruṇā vā, bhattassa pacchā pacchābhattaṃ pacchābhattā vā|

342. samīpāyāmaisvanu ka॰ 319; rū॰ 330; nī॰ 696; caṃ॰ 2.2.9; pā॰ 2.1.15, 16|

samīpai āyāmai ca pavattau anusaddau syādinā saha aikatthau bhavati vā|

vanassa samīpaṃ anuvanaṃ, asani anuvanaṃ gatā, gaṅgaṃ anuyātā anugaṅgaṃ, bārāṇasī|

343. aurau  pari paṭi pārai majjhai haiṭṭhuddhādhauntau vā chaṭṭhiyā ka॰ 319; rū॰ 330; nī॰ 696; caṃ॰ 2.2.11; pā॰ 2.1.18; ‘auraipari…’ (bahūsu)|

aurādayau saddā chaṭṭhīyantaina syādinā saha aikatthā bhavanti vā|

aittha ca aurai, pārai, majjhaisaddaisu ‘tadaminādīnī’ti suttaina aikārau, gaṅgāya auraṃ auraigaṅgaṃ, sikharassa upari uparisikharaṃ| aivaṃ uparipāsādaṃ, uparimañ‍caṃ, uparipabbataṃ, sautassa paṭilaumaṃ paṭisautaṃ| aivaṃ paṭivātaṃ, yamunāya pāraṃ pāraiyamunaṃ, gaṅgāya majjhaṃ majjhaigaṅgaṃ, pāsādassa haiṭṭhā haiṭṭhāpāsādaṃ, haiṭṭhāmañ‍caṃ, gaṅgāya uddhaṃ uddhaṃgaṅgaṃ, gaṅgāya adhau adhaugaṅgaṃ, pāsādassa antau antaupāsādaṃ| aivaṃ antaugāmaṃ, antaunagaraṃ, antauvassaṃ|

vāti kiṃ? gaṅgāauraṃ, majjhaisamuddasmiṃ ic‍cādi|

344. tiṭṭhagvādīni ka॰ 319; rū॰ 330; nī॰ 697; caṃ॰ 2.2.10; pā॰ 2.1.17|

tiṭṭhaguic‍cādīni asaṅkhyaikatthai sijjhanti|

tiṭṭhanti gāvau yasmiṃ kālai tiṭṭhagu, vahanti gāvauyasmiṃ kālai vahagu, ‘gaussū’ti suttaina aussa uttaṃ| āyatiṃ yavau yasmiṃ kālaiti āyatiyavau, khalai yavau yasmiṃ kālaiti khalaiyavaṃ| pubbapadai vibhattialaupau| lunā yavā yasmiṃ kālaiti lunayavaṃ, aittha ‘lunā’ti lāvitā, luyamānā yavā yasminti luyamānayavaṃ ic‍cādi|

tathā pātau nahānaṃ yasmiṃ kālaiti pātanahānaṃ| aivaṃ sāyanahānaṃ, pātau kammakaraṇakālau yasminti  pātakālaṃ| aivaṃ sāyakālaṃ, pātau vassati maighau yasminti pātamaighaṃ| aivaṃ sāyamaighaṃ, pātau gantabbau maggau yasminti pātamaggaṃ| aivaṃ sāyamaggaṃ ic‍cādi| mahāvuttinā pātausaddassa pātattaṃ| aittha ca ‘tiṭṭhagu’ ic‍cādīni viggahatthavasaina añ‍ñapadatthai siddhāni viya dissanti, añ‍ñapadassa pana liṅgādīnaṃ vasaina taisaṃ rūpavikārau nāma natthi, tasmā abyayarūpattā idha gahitāni, sabbañ‍caitaṃ asaṅkhyasamāsapadaṃ nāma napuṃsakaṃ aiva hauti, rassantamaiva hauti| sabbavibhattīnañ‍ca akārantamhā bahulaṃ aṃ hauti, ikārukārantaihi laupau hauti|

abyayībhāvasamāsau niṭṭhitau|

tappurisasamāsa

dutiyātappurisa

atha amādisamāsau vuc‍catai, sau tappurisauti ca vuc‍cati| tassa purisau tappurisau, tappurisasaddaina sadisattā ayaṃ samāsau tappurisauti vuc‍cati| yathā hi tappurisasaddau visaisanapadatthaṃ jahitvā visaisyapadatthai tiṭṭhati, aivaṃ ayaṃ samāsaupīti|

345. amādi ka॰ 327; rū॰ 351; nī॰ 704; caṃ॰ 2.2.16|

amādivibhattiyuttaṃ syādyantapadaṃ paṭhamantaina syādyantapadaina saha aikatthaṃ bhavati, ayañ‍ca samāsau anvatthavasaina ‘‘amādisamāsau’’ti ca ‘‘tappurisasamāsau’’ti ca vuc‍cati| iminā amādisahitassa vākyassa amādaikatthasañ‍ñaṃ katvā vuttatthānaṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupau, tatau aikatthapadantai syādyuppatti hauti|

sau  pana samāsau dutiyātappurisau, tatiyātappurisau, catutthītappurisau, pañ‍camītappurisau, chaṭṭhītappurisau, sattamītappurisauti chabbidhau| aikamaikasmiñ‍caittha ‘‘nic‍casamāsau, anic‍casamāsau’’ti ca ‘‘luttasamāsau, aluttasamāsau’’ti ca duvidhau|

tattha dutiyātappurisau kattuvācakaisu gata, nissita,-atīta, atik‍kanta, patta, āpan‍naic‍cādīsu paraisu hauti|

gāmaṃ gatauti gāmagatau gāmaṃ gatau vā| aivaṃ arañ‍ñagatau, bhūmigatau, rājānaṃ nissitauti rājanissitau| aivaṃ atthanissitau, dhammanissitau| bhavaṃ atītau bhavātītau| aivaṃ bhayātītau, kālātītau, khaṇātītau, pamāṇaṃ atik‍kantauti pamāṇātik‍kantau, sukhaṃ pattauti sukhappattau| aivaṃ dukkhappattau, sautaṃ āpan‍nauti sautāpan‍nau| aivaṃ niraudhasamāpan‍nau, addhānamaggaṃ paṭipan‍nauti addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau, rukkhaṃ ārūḷhauti rukkhārūḷhau, rathārūḷhau, aughaṃ tiṇṇauti aughatiṇṇau aughaṃ tiṇṇau vā ic‍cādi|

kammupapadavihitaihi kitantapadaihi pana nic‍casamāsauyaiva, kumbhaṃ karautīti kumbhakārau, rathakārau, pattaṃ gaṇhātīti pattaggāhau, atthaṃ kāmaitīti atthakāmau, dhammakāmau, vinayaṃ dhāraitīti vinayadharau, dhammadharau, brahmaṃ carati sīlaināti brahmacārī, bhavapāraṃ gacchati sīlaināti bhavapāragū, sabbaṃ jānātīti sabbañ‍ñū, atthañ‍ñū, dhammañ‍ñūic‍cādi|

bahulādhikārattā ta, tavantu, tāvī, anta, māna, tuna, tvāna, tvā, tuṃ, tavaipac‍cayantaisu paraisu samāsau na hauti, vassaṃ vutthau, audanaṃ bhuttau, audanaṃ bhuttavā, audanaṃ bhuttāvī, dhammaṃ suṇantau, dhammaṃ suṇamānau  dhammaṃ sautuna, dhammaṃ sutvāna, dhammaṃ sutvā, dhammaṃ sautuṃ, dhammaṃ sautavai|

iti dutiyātappurisau|

tatiyātappurisa

tatiyātappurisau kammavācakaisu kitantaisu ca sampan‍na, sahagatādīsu ca pubba, sadisa, sama, ūnattha, kalaha, nipuṇa,-missaka, sakhilādīsu ca paraisu hauti|

buddhaina bhāsitau buddhabhāsitau| aivaṃ buddhadaisitau, buddhapañ‍ñattau, buddharakkhitau, satthārā vaṇṇitau satthuvaṇṇitau, viñ‍ñūhi garahitau viñ‍ñugarahitau, viñ‍ñupasatthau, issaraina kataṃ issarakataṃ, attanā kataṃ sayaṃkataṃ, paraihi kataṃ paraṃkataṃ, bindāgamau| sukaihi āhaṭaṃ sukāhaṭaṃ, rañ‍ñā hatau rājahatau, raugaina pīḷitau raugapīḷitau, agginā daḍḍhau aggidaḍḍhau, sappaina daṭṭhau sappadaṭṭhau, sal‍laina viddhau sal‍laviddhau, icchāya apakatau abhibhūtau icchāpakatau|

sīlaina sampan‍nau sīlasampan‍nau| aivaṃ sukhasahagataṃ, ñāṇasampayuttaṃ, mittasaṃsaggau, piyasampayaugau, piyavippayaugau, jātiyā andhau jac‍candhau, guṇahīnau, guṇavuḍḍhau, catuvaggaina saṅghaina karaṇīyaṃ kammaṃ catuvaggakaraṇīyaṃ| aivaṃ pañ‍cavaggakaraṇīyaṃ, kākaihi paiyyā kākapaiyyā, nadī|

aikakkharaisu parapadaisu nic‍casamāsau, uraina gacchatīti uragau, pādaina pivatīti pādapau ic‍cādi|

k‍vaci majjhaipadalaupau, guḷaina saṃsaṭṭhau audanau guḷaudanau| aivaṃ khīraudanau, assaina yuttau rathau assarathau| aivaṃ ājañ‍ñarathau, maggaina sampayuttaṃ cittaṃ maggacittaṃ, jambuyā pañ‍ñātau dīpau jambudīpau, aikaina adhikā dasa aikādasa ic‍cādi|

pubbādīsu – māsaina pubbau māsapubbau, mātarā sadisau mātusadisau| aivaṃ mātusamau, pitusamau, aikaina ūnā vīsati aikūnavīsati, sīlaina vikalau sīlavikalau, asinā kalahau asikalahau, vācāya nipuṇau vācānipuṇau| aivaṃ yāvakālikasammissaṃ, vācāsakhilau, satthārā sadisau satthukappau, puñ‍ñaina atthau puñ‍ñatthau, puñ‍ñaina atthikau puñ‍ñatthikau| aivaṃ saiyyatthikau, mahagghatthikau, guṇaina adhikau guṇādhikau ic‍cādi|

bahulādhikārā k‍vaci samāsau na hauti, pharasunā chin‍naṃ, kākaihi pātabbā, dassanaina pahātabbā dhammā, bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā ic‍cādi|

iti tatiyātappurisau|

catutthītappurisa

catutthītappurisau tadatthai vā attha, hita, daiyyādīsu vā paraisu hauti|

kathinassa dussaṃ kathinadussaṃ, kathinatthāya ābhaṭaṃ dussanti atthau| aivaṃ kathinacīvaraṃ, kathināya dussaṃ, kathināya cīvarantipi yuj‍jati, kathinatthārāyāti atthau| cīvarāya dussaṃ cīvaradussaṃ| aivaṃ cīvaramūlaṃ cīvaramūlyaṃ (rū॰ nī॰), saṅghassa ābhaṭaṃ bhattaṃ saṅghabhattaṃ, saṅghatthāya vā paṭiyattaṃ bhattaṃ saṅghabhattaṃ| aivaṃ āgantukabhattaṃ, gamikabhattaṃ, gilānabhattaṃ|

saṅghassa  atthau saṅghatthau, laukassa hitau laukahitau, buddhassa daiyyaṃ buddhadaiyyaṃ, buddhassa paṇāmau buddhappaṇāmau| aivaṃ buddhathaumanā, buddhupaṭṭhānaṃ, suttassa anulaumaṃ suttānulaumaṃ| aivaṃ suttānurūpaṃ, suttānukūlaṃ, suttānuguṇaṃ, ṭhānassa arahaṃ ṭhānārahaṃ, rañ‍ñau arahaṃ rājārahaṃ| aivaṃ rājagghaṃ, rājadaiyyaṃ, kattuṃ kāmaitīti kattukāmau| aivaṃ gantukāmau, kathaitukāmau, daṭṭhukāmau, sautukāmau| aittha ca tumantassa asaṅkhyattā ‘asaṅkhyaihi sabbāsa’nti tatau nic‍caṃ catutthīlaupau hauti, samāsai katai nic‍caṃ niggahītalaupau ca| ‘‘saṅghassa dātabbaṃ, saṅghassa dātuṃ’’ ic‍cādīsu samāsau na hauti|

iti catutthītappurisau|

pañ‍camītappurisa

pañ‍camītappurisau apagamana, bhaya, virati, maucanādiatthaisu paraisu hauti|

maithunā apaitauti maithunāpaitau, palāsatau apagatauti palāsāpagatau, nagaramhā niggatauti nagaraniggatau, piṇḍapātatau paṭik‍kantauti piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau| aivaṃ gāmanikkhantau, rukkhapatitau, sāsanamhā cutauti sāsanacutau, āpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ āpattivuṭṭhānaṃ, udakatau uggatau udakuggatau, bhavatau nissaṭau bhavanissaṭau, khandhasaṅgahatau nissaṭanti khandhasaṅgahanissaṭaṃ, cauramhā bhītauti caurabhītau, pāpatau bhāyati sīlaināti pāpabhīrukau, pāpatau jigucchati sīlaināti pāpajigucchī, vaṭṭatau nibbindatīti vaṭṭanibbin‍nau, kāyaduc‍caritatau virati kāyaduc‍caritavirati| aivaṃ vacīduc‍caritavirati, bandhanā muttau bandhanamuttau| aivaṃ bandhanamaukkhau, kammatau samuṭṭhitaṃ kammasamuṭṭhitaṃ| aivaṃ kammajātaṃ, kammasambhūtaṃ, kammanibbattaṃ, laukatau aggau laukaggau| aivaṃ laukajaiṭṭhau, laukuttamau, sabbaihi jaiṭṭhau sabbajaiṭṭhau, sabbaihi kaniṭṭhau sabbakaniṭṭhau| aivaṃ sabbahīnau, sabbapacchimau, uk‍kaṭṭhatau uk‍kaṭṭhauti uk‍kaṭṭhuk‍kaṭṭhau, aumakatau aumakauti aumakaumakau|

k‍vaci nic‍casamāsau hauti, mātitau jātau mātujau| aivaṃ pitujau, kammajaṃ, cittajaṃ, utujaṃ ic‍cādi|

iti pañ‍camītappurisau|

chaṭṭhītappurisa

rañ‍ñau puttau rājaputtau| aivaṃ rājapurisau, buddhasāvakau, samuddaghausau, dhañ‍ñānaṃ rāsi dhañ‍ñarāsi, mattikāya pattauti mattikāpattau, vikārasambandhai chaṭṭhī, mattikāmayapattauti atthau| aivaṃ suvaṇṇakaṭāhaṃ, suvaṇṇabhājanaṃ, pānīyassa thālakaṃ pānīyathālakaṃ|

samāsamajjhai ī, ūnaṃ bahulaṃ rassattaṃ, daṇḍinau kulaṃ daṇḍikulaṃ, hatthipadaṃ, itthirūpaṃ, nadikūlaṃ, naditīraṃ, bhikkhunīnaṃ saṅghau bhikkhunisaṅghau, jambuyā sākhā jambusākhā ic‍cādi|

anta, māna, niddhāraṇiya, pūraṇa, bhāva, suhitatthaihi samāsau na hauti, mamaṃ anukubbantau, mamaṃ anukurumānau, gun‍naṃ kaṇhā sampan‍nakhīratamā| vibhattāvadhichaṭṭhiyā pana hautiyaiva, narānaṃ uttamau naruttamau, narasaiṭṭhau, naravarau, gaṇānaṃ uttamau gaṇuttamau, dvipadānaṃ uttamau dvipaduttamau ic‍cādi| sissānaṃ pañ‍camau sissau, kappassa tatiyau bhāgau, pakkhassa aṭṭhamī, paṭassa suk‍katā, rūpassa lahutā, rūpassa  mudutā, rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā| k‍vaci hauti, kāyalahutā, cittalahutā, buddhasubuddhatā| dhammasudhammatā, phalānaṃ suhitau, phalānaṃ tittau, phalānaṃ asitau, karaṇatthai chaṭṭhī|

‘‘bhaṭau rañ‍ñau puttau daivadattassā’’ti aittha ‘rājaputtau’ti na hauti añ‍ñamañ‍ñānapaikkhattā| ‘‘daivadattassa kaṇhā dantā’’ti aittha ‘daivadattakaṇhadantā’ti na hauti añ‍ñasāpaikkhattā nī॰ 690| añ‍ñasāpaikkhattaipi nic‍caṃ sambandhīpaikkhasaddānaṃ samāsau hauti vākyai viya samāsaipi sambandhassa viditattā| vuttañ‍ca ‘‘satipi sāpaikkhattai gamakattā samāsau hautī’’ti ka॰ 328; rū॰ 352; nī॰ 691, daivadattagurukulaṃ, rājadāsīputtau, daivadāsīputtau ic‍cādi| tattha daivadattassa guru daivadattaguru, tassa kulaṃ daivadattagurukulanti viggahau| gurunau kulaṃ gurukulaṃ, daivadattassa gurukulaṃ daivadattagurukulantipi vadanti| ‘‘rañ‍ñau māgadhassa bimbisārassa puttau’’ti aitthapi añ‍ñasāpaikkhattā ‘bimbisāraputtau’ti na hauti, rañ‍ñau gau ca assau ca purisau cāti atthai ‘rājagavassapurisā’ti hauti dvandatau pubbapadassa dvandapadaihipi pac‍caikaṃ sambandhassa viditattā| tathā dvandatau parapadassapi, narānañ‍ca daivānañ‍ca sārathi naradaivasārathi|

iti chaṭṭhītappurisau|

sattamītappurisa

sattamītappurisai rūpai sañ‍ñā rūpasañ‍ñā| aittha ca kārakānaṃ kriyāsādhanalakkhaṇattā kriyāpadaihaiva sambandhau hauti, tasmā akriyavācakaina parapadaina saddhiṃ samāsai jātai majjhai anurūpaṃ kriyāpadaṃ viñ‍ñāyati, yathā  ‘assaina yuttau rathau assarathau’ iti ‘rūpai sañ‍ñā’ti rūpai uppan‍nā sañ‍ñāti atthau| cakkhusmiṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, dhammai ratau dhammaratau| aivaṃ dhammaruci, dhammagāravau, dānai ajjhāsayau dānajjhāsayau| aivaṃ dānādhimutti, vaṭṭai bhayaṃ vaṭṭabhayaṃ, vaṭṭadukkhaṃ, gāmai sūkarau gāmasūkarau, vanamahiṃsau, samuddamacchau, itthīsu dhuttau itthidhuttau, itthisauṇḍau|

upapadakitantaisu nic‍casamāsau nī॰ 682, vanai caratīti vanacarau, kāmāvacarau, kucchimhi sayatīti kucchissayau, gabbhai saitīti gabbhasaiyyau, thalai tiṭṭhatīti thalaṭṭhau, jalaṭṭhau, pabbataṭṭhau, paṅkai jātaṃ paṅkajaṃ| aivaṃ atrajau, khaitrajau ic‍cādi| idha na hauti nī॰ 681, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā, indriyaisu guttadvārau, āsanai nisin‍nau, āsanai nisīditabbaṃ|

iti sattamītappurisau|

luttatappurisa

tappurisapadānaṃ mahāvuttisuttaina k‍vaci vipal‍lāsau|

uparigaṅgā, haiṭṭhānadī, antauvihārau, antausamāpatti, haṃsānaṃ rājā rājahaṃsau haṃsarājā vā, māsassa aḍḍhaṃ aḍḍhamāsaṃ, kahāpaṇassa aḍḍhaṃ aḍḍhakahāpaṇaṃ, aḍḍhamāsaṃ, rattiyā aḍḍhaṃ aḍḍharattaṃ| aivaṃ pubbarattaṃ, pararattaṃ, issa attaṃ| kāyassa pubbabhāgau pubbakāyau, parakāyau, ahassa pubbau pubbaṇhau, majjhaṇhau, sāyanhau, pubbaidiṭṭhau diṭṭhapubbau, tathāgataṃ diṭṭhapubbau thairau, tathāgatau diṭṭhapubbau thairaina ic‍cādi|

iti luttatappurisau|

aluttatappurisa

idāni  aluttatappurisā vuc‍cantai|

dīpaṅkarau, pabhaṅkarau, amatandadau, purindadau, vaissantarau, attantapau, parantapau, raṇañ‍jahau, jutindharau, vij‍jandharau, dassanainapahātabbadhammau, kutaujau, tataujau, itaujau, bhayatau upaṭṭhānaṃ bhayatupaṭṭhānaṃ, kaṭattākammaṃ, kaṭattārūpaṃ, parassapadaṃ, attanaupadaṃ, daivānamindau, daivānaṃpiyatissau, gavampatitthairau, pubbainivāsau, majjhaikalyāṇaṃ, dūrairūpaṃ, santikairūpaṃ, dūrainidānaṃ, santikainidānaṃ, antaivāsikau, janaisutau, kāmaisumicchācārau ic‍cādi ka॰ 327; rū॰ 351; nī॰ 686|

iti aluttatappurisau|

sabbau cāyaṃ amāditappurisau abhidhaiyyavacanau parapadaliṅgau ca|

amāditappurisau niṭṭhitau|

kammadhārayasamāsa

atha kammadhārayasañ‍ñitau paṭhamātappurisau vuc‍catai|

kammamiva dvayaṃ dhāraitīti kammadhārayau| yathā kammaṃ kriyañ‍ca payaujanañ‍ca dvayaṃ dhāraiti kammai sati tassa dvayassa sambhavatau, tathā ayaṃ samāsau aikassa atthassa dvai nāmāni dhāraiti imasmiṃ samāsai sati aikatthajautakassa nāmadvayassa sambhavatauti ka॰ 324; rū॰ 339; nī॰ 702|

api ca kattabbanti kammaṃ, dhāraitabbanti dhāriyaṃ, kammañ‍ca taṃ dhāriyañ‍cāti kammadhāriyaṃ, yaṃkiñ‍ci hitakammaṃ, kammadhāriyasaddasadisattā  sabbau cāyaṃ samāsau kammadhārayauti vuc‍cati issa attaṃ katvā| yathā hi kammadhāriyasaddau aikassa atthassa dvai nāmāni dhāraiti, tathā ayaṃ samāsaupīti| sau aiva uttarapadatthapadhānatāsaṅkhātaina tappurisalakkhaṇaina yuttattā ‘tappurisau’ti ca vuc‍cati| bhin‍napavattinimittānaṃ dvin‍naṃ padānaṃ visaisanavisaisitabbabhāvaina aikasmiṃ atthai pavatti tulyādhikaraṇatā nāma, taina tulyādhikaraṇalakkhaṇaina yuttattā ‘tulyādhikaraṇasamāsau’ti ca vuc‍cati| sau aiva ca visaisanapadavasaina guṇavisaisadīpanattā ‘visaisanasamāsau’ti ca vuc‍cati| tasmiṃ visaisanasamāsai –

346. visaisanamaikatthaina ka॰ 324; rū॰ 339; nī॰ 702; caṃ॰ 2.2.18; pā॰ 2.1.57|

visaisanabhūtaṃ syādyantapadaṃ aikatthaina visaisyabhūtaina syādyantapadaina saddhiṃ aikatthaṃ hauti|

aittha ca visaisīyati dabbaṃ visiṭṭhaṃ karīyati aitaināti visaisanaṃ| aikau atthau yassāti aikatthaṃ, ‘aikau’ti samānau, ‘atthau’ti abhidhaiyyatthau, naimittakatthau, sauyaiva dvin‍naṃ pavattinimittānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṭṭhaina ‘adhikaraṇa’nti ca vuc‍cati| pavattinimittānañ‍ca adhiṭṭhānattai sati padānampi adhiṭṭhānatā siddhā hauti| iti aikatthanti tulyādhikaraṇaṃ, samānādhikaraṇanti vuttaṃ hauti, taina aikatthaina| ‘aikatthaṃ hautī’ti aikatthībhūtaṃ hautīti atthau|

sau ca samāsau navavidhau visaisanapubbapadau, visaisanuttarapadau, visaisanaubhayapadau, upamānuttarapadau, sambhāvanāpubbapadau, avadhāraṇapubbapadau, nanipātapubbapadau, kunipātapubbapadau, pādipubbapadau cāti|

tattha  visaisanapubbapadau yathā? mahāpurisau, mahānadī, mahabbhayaṃ| aittha ca ‘‘sā sainā dissatai mahā jā॰ 2.22.771, bārāṇasiraj‍jaṃ nāma mahā’’ti jā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1.mahāsīlavajātakavaṇṇanā pāḷi dissati| tasmā samāsaipi tiliṅgai nipātarūpau mahāsaddau yuj‍jati| mahā ca sau purisau cāti mahāpurisau, mahā ca sā nadī cāti mahānadī, mahā ca taṃ bhayañ‍cāti mahabbhayaṃ, dvittaṃ saṃyaugai ca rassattaṃ| mahāsaddavaivacanaina mahantasaddainapi vākyaṃ dassaituṃ yuj‍jati, mahantau purisau mahāpurisau, mahantī nadī mahānadī, mahantaṃ bhayaṃ mahabbhayanti| ca, tasaddaihi ca saddhiṃ paripuṇṇaṃ katvā dassaituṃ yuj‍jati, mahantau ca sau purisau cāti mahāpurisau, mahantī ca sā nadī cāti mahānadī, mahantañ‍ca taṃ bhayañ‍cāti mahabbhayanti| mahantasaddau vā mahā hauti, ‘ṭa ntantūna’nti suttaina uttarapadai parai ntassa sabbassa attaṃ, mahāvuttinā dīghau ca|

aittha ca dvīhi casaddaihi dvin‍naṃ padānaṃ sakatthanānāttaṃ dīpaiti| taṃsaddaina sakatthanānāttaipi sakatthānaṃ adhikaraṇabhūtassa dabbatthassa aikattaṃ dīpaiti| imasmiṃ byākaraṇai visuṃ rūpavidhānakic‍caṃ nāma natthi, taṃtaṃsuttavidhānañ‍ca tadanurūpaṃ dassitaviggahavākyañ‍ca disvā tassa tassa siddhapadassa atthabyañ‍janavinicchayai ñātai rūpavidhānakic‍caṃ siddhaṃ hauti, santau ca sau purisau cāti sappurisau, saitahatthī, nīluppalaṃ, lauhitacandanaṃ|

visadisaliṅga, vacanāpi saddā aikatthā haunti, vinayau ca sau pariyatti cāti vinayapariyatti, vinayau ca sau piṭakañ‍cāti vinayapiṭakaṃ, avij‍jā ca sā pac‍cayau cāti avij‍jāpac‍cayau, avij‍jā ca sā nīvaraṇañ‍cāti avij‍jānīvaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ itthiratanaṃ, sīlañ‍ca taṃ guṇau cāti sīlaguṇau, sīlañ‍ca taṃ patiṭṭhā cāti sīlapatiṭṭhā ic‍cādi|

tathā  vīsati ca sā purisā cāti vīsatipurisā, satañ‍ca taṃ purisā cāti satapurisā, saṅkhārā ca tai pac‍cayau cāti saṅkhārapac‍cayau, aṅgā ca tai janapadañ‍cāti aṅgajanapadaṃ, magadhā ca tai raṭṭhañ‍cāti magadharaṭṭhaṃ| aivaṃ kāsiraṭṭhaṃ ic‍cādi|

idha na hauti rū॰ 341; nī॰ 681, puṇṇau mantānīputtau, cittau gahapati, sak‍kau daivarājā, brahmā sahampati ic‍cādi|

k‍vaci nic‍casamāsau, kaṇhasappau, lauhitamālaṃ ic‍cādi|

visaisanuttarapadau yathā? sāriputtatthairau, buddhaghausācariyau, ācariyaguttilau vā, mahausadhapaṇḍitau, purisuttamau, purisavarau, purisavisaisau ic‍cādi|

visaisanaubhayapadau yathā? chin‍nañ‍ca taṃ parūḷhañ‍cāti chin‍naparūḷhaṃ, sītañ‍ca taṃ uṇhañ‍cāti sītuṇhaṃ, khañ‍jau ca sau khuj‍jau cāti khañ‍jakhuj‍jau| aivaṃ andhabadhirau, katañ‍ca taṃ akatañ‍cāti katākataṃ, chiddāvachiddaṃ, chin‍nabhin‍naṃ, sittañ‍ca taṃ sammaṭṭhañ‍cāti sittasammaṭṭhaṃ, santassa bhāvau sac‍caṃ, akhaimaṭṭhaina dukkhañ‍ca taṃ aviparītaṭṭhaina sac‍cañ‍cāti dukkhasac‍caṃ ic‍cādi|

upamānuttarapadau yathā? sīhau viyāti sīhau, muni ca sau sīhau cāti munisīhau| aivaṃ munipuṅgavau, buddhanāgau, buddhādic‍cau, raṃsi viyāti raṃsi, saddhammau ca sau raṃsi cāti saddhammaraṃsi| aivaṃ vinayasāgarau, samaṇapadumau, samaṇapuṇḍarīkau ic‍cādi|

sambhāvanāpubbapadau yathā? haitu hutvā pac‍cayau haitupac‍cayau| aivaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayau, manussabhūtau, daivabhūtau, dhammau iti saṅkhātau dhammasaṅkhātau, dhammasammatau, dhammasañ‍ñitau, dhammalakkhitau, aiva iti saṅkhātau saddau aivasaddau| aivaṃ casaddau, vāsaddau, ariyabhūtau saṅghau ariyasaṅghau| aivaṃ buddhamuni, pac‍caikamuni ic‍cādi| aittha ca sambhāvanā nāma sāmañ‍ñabhūtassa uttarapadatthassa daḷhaṃ katvā thaumanā sarūpavisaisadīpanā, na guṇamattadīpanāti adhippāyau| garū pana ‘‘dhammau iti buddhi dhammabuddhi| aivaṃ dhammasañ‍ñā, anic‍casañ‍ñā, dhātusañ‍ñā, mātusañ‍ñā, pāṇasañ‍ñitā, attadiṭṭhi’’ ic‍cādīnipi aittha āharanti, imāni pana ‘‘saraṇaṃ iti gatau upagatau saraṇaṅgatau’’ti padaṃ viya itiluttāni paṭhamātappurisapadāni nāma yuj‍jantīti rū॰ 343; nī॰ 702|

avadhāraṇapubbapadau yathā? guṇau aiva dhanaṃ na maṇisuvaṇṇādīti guṇadhanaṃ| aivaṃ saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, cakkhu aiva dvāraṃ na gāmadvārādīti cakkhudvāraṃ| aivaṃ cakkhuvatthu, cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhāyatanaṃ, cakkhudhātu, khandhā aiva bhārā khandhabhārā| aittha ca yadi bharitabbaṭṭhaina bhārā nāma siyuṃ, pañ‍cakkhandhā aiva bhārā nāma siyuṃ, na sīsabhāra, aṃsabhārādayau| khandhā hi nic‍cabhārā haunti, itarai tāvakālikā, khandhamūlikā cāti| aivaṃ atisayatthasambhāvanatthaṃ khandhā aiva bhārāti avadhāraṇavākyaṃ payuj‍jati, na sīsabhārādīnaṃ sabbasau bhārabhāvapaṭikkhipanatthanti| aivaṃ sabbattha, avij‍jā aiva malaṃ na kaṃsamalādikanti avij‍jāmalaṃ, avij‍jā aiva āsavau na madhvāsavādikauti avij‍jāsavau| aivaṃ taṇhāsal‍laṃ, pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ, pañ‍ñālaukau, pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau, rāgaggi, dausaggi, mauhaggi ic‍cādi| garū pana ‘‘dhanaṃ viyāti dhanaṃ, saddhā aiva ariyānaṃ dhanaṃ saddhādhanaṃ, satthaṃ viyāti satthaṃ, pañ‍ñā aiva satthaṃ pañ‍ñāsattha’’nti yaujainti, aivaṃ sati atisayasambhāvanatthau na sijjhati rū॰ 343; nī॰ 702|

347. nanipātapubbapadai nau ka॰ 326; rū॰ 341; nī॰ 707|

ñānubandhau  paṭisaidhamhā añ‍ñanakāra nivattanatthau, nauic‍caitaṃ syādyantaṃ añ‍ñaina syādyantaina saha aikatthaṃ hauti| iminā nañai katthasañ‍ñaṃ katvā –

348. ṭa naussa ka॰ 333; rū॰ 344; nī॰ 717; caṃ॰ 2.2.20; pā॰ 2.2.6; ‘‘nau’’ (bahūsu)|

uttarapadai parai nauic‍caitassa ṭānubandhau a hautīti nassa attaṃ|

na brāhmaṇau abrāhmaṇau| aittha siyā – kiṃ vij‍jamānassa vāyaṃ nisaidhau, udāhu avij‍jamānassa vāti, kiñ‍caittha – yadi vij‍jamānassa nisaidhau, aivaṃ sati laukai vij‍jamānā sabbai brāhmaṇā abrāhmaṇā nāma bhavaiyyuṃ| tasmā ‘‘idha janau na brāhmaṇau, tattha janau na brāhmaṇau’’tiādinā daisādiniyamaṃ vinā laukai vij‍jamānassa brāhmaṇassa nisaidhau na yuj‍jati, atha laukai avij‍jamānassa nisaidhau, aivañ‍ca sati kiṃ avij‍jamānassa nisaidhaina nisaidhanīyassaiva avij‍jamānattāti? vuc‍catai – taṃsadisādiatthaisu tabbauhārassaivāyaṃ nisaidhau| tathā hi brāhmaṇasadisai abrāhmaṇai kaisañ‍ci brāhmaṇasañ‍ñā saṇṭhāti, sañ‍ñānurūpañ‍ca brāhmaṇavauhārau tasmiṃ pavattati, aivaṃ pavattassa abrāhmaṇai brāhmaṇavauhārassa ayaṃ paṭisaidhau hauti| yathā taṃ? laukasmiṃ bālajanānaṃ micchāsañ‍ñāvasaina micchāvauhārau pavattatiyaiva ‘‘rūpaṃ attā, vaidanā attā’’ ic‍cādi, taisaṃ tassa micchābhāvakhyāpanatthaṃ paṭisaidhau yaujiyati ‘‘rūpaṃ anattā, vaidanā anattā’’ mahāva॰ 20 ic‍cādi| aittāvatā suddhabrāhmaṇasaddassapi micchāvasaina taṃsadisai atthai pavattisambhavau siddhau hauti, nakārassa  ca tadatthajautakamattatā siddhā hauti, aivañ‍ca sati uttarapadatthapadhānatāsaṅkhātaṃ tappurisalakkhaṇampi idha na virujjhati, tasmā abrāhmaṇauti brāhmaṇasadisauti vuttaṃ hauti| aisa nayau tadañ‍ña, tabbiruddha, tadabhāvatthādīsu|

tattha tadañ‍ñatthai –

saṅkhatā dhammā asaṅkhatā dhammā dha॰ sa॰ dukamātikā 8| aittha ca na saṅkhatā asaṅkhatā, saṅkhatadhammaihi añ‍ñai dhammāti atthau|

tabbiruddhai –

akusalau, kusalapaṭipakkhau dhammauti atthau|

tadabhāvai –

na katvā akatvā, karaṇaina sabbasau vināti atthau|

duvidhau paṭisaidhau pasaj‍japaṭisaidhau, payirudāsapaṭisaidhau cāti|

tattha attanā yuttapadatthaṃ pasaj‍ja laggaitvā paṭisaidhaitīti pasaj‍japaṭisaidhau, tadabhāvamattajautakau nakārau, kriyāmattanisaidhauti vuttaṃ hauti| akatvā, akātuṃ, akarauntau, na karauti, na kātabbaṃ ic‍cādi|

pasaj‍jamattai aṭṭhatvā taṃsadisādikai paritaubhāgai uggayha nisaidhaitabbaṃ atthaṃ asati khipati chaḍḍaitīti payirudāsau, taṃsadisādijautakau, dabbanisaidhauti vuttaṃ hauti| abrāhmaṇau ic‍cādi| aivaṃ asamaṇau, asakyaputtiyau, amittau, mittadhammavidhurauti atthau|

349. ana sarai ka॰ 334; rū॰ 345; nī॰ 718; caṃ॰ 5.2.119; pā॰ 6.3.105|

sarai parai nauic‍caitassa ana hauti|

na  ariyau anariyau, ariyadhammavimukhauti atthau| na āvāsau anāvāsau, na issarau anissarau| na īti anīti, ‘ītī’ti upaddavau, na yuttau upāyau anupāyau, na ūmi anūmi, na yuttā aisanā anaisanā, na yuttau aukāsau anaukāsau, na atik‍kamma anatik‍kamma, anādāya, anaulaukaitvā ic‍cādi|

bahulādhikārā ayuttatthānampi samāsau hauti ka॰ 336; rū॰ 347; nī॰ 689, puna na gāyitabbāti apunagaiyyā, gāthā, candaṃ na ul‍laukaintīti acandamul‍laukikāni, mukhāni, sūriyaṃ na passantīti asūriyapassā, rājakañ‍ñā, saddhaṃ na bhuñ‍jati sīlaināti asaddhabhaujī| aivaṃ alavaṇabhaujī, atthaṃ na kāmaintīti anatthakāmā| aivaṃ ahitakāmā, aukāsaṃ na kāraisīti anaukāsaṃkāraitvā| aivaṃ animittaṃkatvā, mūlamūlaṃ na gacchatīti amūlamūlaṃgantvā ic‍cādi|

‘‘puna gāyitabbāti punagaiyyā, na punagaiyyā apunagaiyyā| atthaṃ kāmaintīti atthakāmā, na atthakāmā anatthakāmā| atha vā na atthau anatthau, anatthaṃ kāmaintīti anatthakāmā’’ ic‍cādinā vākyai yaujitai pana yuttasamāsā haunti| garū pana ‘‘atthaṃ na kāmainti anatthamaiva kāmaintīti anatthakāmā, hitaṃ na kāmainti ahitamaiva kāmaintīti ahitakāmā, phāsuṃ na kāmainti aphāsumaiva kāmaintīti aphāsukāmā’’ti yaujaisuṃ, dvādhippāyapadaṃ nāmaitaṃ|

kunipātapubbapadai nic‍casamāsattā añ‍ñapadaina viggahau, khuddakā nadī kun‍nadī, khuddakau saumbhau kusaumbhau, khuddakaṃ vanaṃ kubbanaṃ|

350. sarai kada kussuttaratthai ka॰ 335; rū॰ 346; nī॰ 719|

sarādikai  uttarapadai parai uttarapadatthai vattamānassa kunipātassa kadi hauti|

kucchitaṃ an‍naṃ kadan‍naṃ, kucchitaṃ asanaṃ kadasanaṃ, kucchitau ariyau kadariyau, maccharī|

saraiti kiṃ? kuputtā, kudārā, kudāsā|

uttaratthaiti kiṃ? kucchitau auṭṭhau yassāti kuauṭṭhau|

351. kāppatthai ka॰ 336; rū॰ 347; nī॰ 720|

uttarapadai parai uttarapadatthai ṭhitassa appatthai vattamānassa kunipātassa kā hauti vā|

appakaṃ lavaṇaṃ kālavaṇaṃ| aivaṃ kāpupphaṃ|

pādipubbapadau ca nic‍casamāsauva, pakaṭṭhaṃ vacanaṃ pāvacanaṃ, dīghattaṃ, pakaṭṭhaṃ hutvā nītaṃ paṇītaṃ, pamukhaṃ hutvā dhānaṃ padhānaṃ| aivaṃ paṭṭhānaṃ, vividhā mati vimati, adhikau daivau adhidaivau, atiraikau visaisau vā dhammau abhidhammau, sundarau gandhau sugandhau, kucchitau gandhau duggandhau| saubhaṇaṃ kataṃ sukataṃ, kucchitaṃ kataṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ, viparītau pathau uppathau| aivaṃ ummaggau, uddhammau, ubbinayauic‍cādi|

ayampi kammadhārayasamāsau abhidhaiyyavacanau parapadaliṅgau ca|

kammadhārayasamāsau niṭṭhitau|

digusamāsa

atha  digusaṅkhātau paṭhamātappurisau vuc‍catai|

dvai gāvau digu, saṅkhyāpubbattaina napuṃsakaikattaina ca digusaddasadisattā sabbau cāyaṃ samāsau digūti vuc‍cati|

352. saṅkhyādi ka॰ 321; rū॰ 349; nī॰ 699|

samāhāraikatthai saṅkhyāpubbakaṃ aikatthaṃ napuṃsakaṃ hauti, samāhāravacanainaiva aikattañ‍ca siddhaṃ|

dvai gāvau digu, ‘gaussū’ti suttaina aussa uttaṃ, tayau laukā tilaukaṃ, tayau laukā ñāṇasmiṃ samāhaṭā sampiṇḍitāti tilaukaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ laukānaṃ samāhārauti tilaukaṃ, tayau ca tai laukā cāti tilaukaṃ| aivaṃ tibhavaṃ, tipurisaṃ, tīṇi malāni timalaṃ, tiratanaṃ, tissau sañ‍ñāyautisañ‍ñaṃ, ‘syādīsu rassau’ti rassattaṃ| cattārau pathā catuppathaṃ, cattāri sac‍cāni catusac‍caṃ, catassau disā catuddisaṃ| aivaṃ pañ‍casikkhāpadaṃ, saḷāyatanaṃ, sattāhaṃ, aṭṭhapadaṃ, navalaukuttaraṃ, dasasikkhāpadaṃ, satayaujanaṃ, sahassayaujanaṃ ic‍cādi|

imasmiṃ samāhāradigumhi sabbaṃ napuṃsakamaiva rassantamaiva aikavacanantamaiva cāti|

asamāhāradigu rū॰ 350 nī॰ 703 yathā? aikau puggalau aikapuggalau, tayau bhavā tibhavā, catassau disā catuddisā ic‍cādi|

saṅkhyāṭhānai pana ka॰ 392; rū॰ 418; nī॰ 831 dvai satāni dvisataṃ| aivaṃ tisataṃ, catusataṃ, pañ‍casataṃ, chasataṃ, sattasataṃ, aṭṭhasataṃ, navasataṃ, dasasataṃ, dvisahassaṃ, tisahassaṃ, catusahassaṃ, pañ‍casahassaṃ, dasasahassaṃ|

dvai  satasahassāni dvisatasahassaṃ| aivaṃ ‘‘tisatasahassaṃ, catusatasahassaṃ, pañ‍casatasahassa’’nti vā ‘‘dvisatāni, dvai satāni, tisatāni, tīṇi satāni, catusatāni, cattāri satāni, dvisahassāni, dvai sahassāni, tisahassāni, tīṇi sahassāni, dvisatasahassāni, dvai satasahassānī’’ti vā aivaṃ vacanadvayañ‍ca vākyañ‍ca vaiditabbaṃ| aivaṃ satasahassaipīti|

aittha siyā – digu nāma saṅkhyāpubbamaiva siyā, imaisu ca sabbaṃ saṅkhyāpadamaiva hautīti? digumhi pubbaṃ saṅkhyāpadamaiva siyā, parapadaṃ pana saṅkhyāpadampi añ‍ñampi yuj‍jatīti|

digusamāsau niṭṭhitau|

bahubbīhisamāsa

atha bahubbīhisamāsau vuc‍catai|

bahavau vīhayau yasmiṃ daisai sauyaṃ bahubbīhi, tādisau gāmau vā daisau vā janapadau vā, bahubbīhisaddasadisattā sabbau cāyaṃ samāsau bahubbīhīti vuc‍cati| yathā hi bahubbīhisaddau samāsapadatthai atik‍kamma gāma, daisa, janapadaic‍cādīnaṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ padānaṃ atthaisu tiṭṭhati, tathā ayaṃ samāsaupi| añ‍ñapadatthapadhānau hi bahubbīhisamāsau|

sau saṅkhaipaina duvidhau tagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau, atagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau cāti|

tattha ‘guṇau’ti appadhānabhūtau samāsapadānaṃ atthau, sau añ‍ñapadatthassa visaisanabhūtattā tassa añ‍ñapadatthassa guṇauti  atthaina tagguṇauti vuc‍cati, viñ‍ñātabbauti viñ‍ñāṇau, añ‍ñapadatthau, tagguṇaṃ amuñ‍citvā tagguṇaina sahaiva viñ‍ñāṇau añ‍ñapadatthau yasminti tagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau, na tagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau atagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau, yattha samāsapadatthau avayavabhāvaina vā sahavidhaiyyabhāvaina vā añ‍ñapadatthai antaugadhau hauti, sau tagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau| yathā? chin‍nahatthau purisau, buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa bhattaṃ daiti, saputtadārau āgatau, pādayau upasaggā nāmāti|

aittha ca ‘buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa bhattaṃ daitī’ti bhikkhusaṅghassa ca bhattaṃ daiti, pamukhabhūtassa buddhassa ca bhattaṃ daitīti atthau| ‘saputtadārau āgatau’ti puttadārā ca āgatā, purisau ca āgatauti atthau| ‘pādayau upasaggā nāmā’ti pa-kārau ca upasaggau nāma, parādayau ca upasaggā nāmāti atthau| aivaṃ yaujanārahatā añ‍ñapadatthaina saha samāsapadatthassa vidhaiyyatā nāmāti|

atagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau yathā? din‍nasuṅkau rājā dānaṃ daiti, pabbatādīni khaittāni kassati ic‍cādi| imaisu pana samāsapadatthau avidhaiyyau, añ‍ñapadatthau aiva vidhaiyyau|

paṭhamābahubbīhi

paṭhamābahubbīhi, dutiyābahubbīhi, tatiyābahubbīhi, catutthībahubbīhi, pañ‍camībahubbīhi, chaṭṭhībahubbīhi, sattamībahubbīhi cāti sattavidhau|

tattha paṭhamābahubbīhi sahapubbapada, upamānapubbapada, saṅkhyaubhayapada, disantarāḷattha, byatihāralakkhaṇavasaina pañ‍cavidhau|

tattha –

353. vānaikamañ‍ñatthai ka॰ 328; rū॰ 352; nī॰ 708|

anaikaṃ  syādyantapadaṃ añ‍ñapadassa atthai vikappaina aikatthaṃ hauti|

saha vitak‍kaināti savitak‍kau, vitak‍kaina saha yau vattatīti vā savitak‍kau, samādhi|

aittha ca ‘saha vitak‍kainā’ti aittha paṭhamāvibhattiyā atthabhūtau añ‍ñapadatthau vākyasāmatthiyaina sijjhati| na hi kriyākārakarahitaṃ vākyaṃ nāma sambhavati, iminā suttaina sahapada, vitak‍kapadānaṃ samādhisaṅkhātaina añ‍ñapadatthaina aikatthībhāvau hauti, aikatthībhāvai ca hauntai vākyai ṭhitānaṃ añ‍ñapadānaṃ vibhattīnañ‍ca sabbai atthā aikatthabhūtaina samāsaina vuttā nāma haunti, añ‍ñapadāni ca vibhattiyau ca vuttatthā nāma, vuttatthānañ‍ca attharahitattā payaugakic‍caṃ natthi, tasmā ‘aikatthatāya’nti suttaina vibhattīnaṃ laupau, aivaṃ sabbasamāsaisu vākyaidissamānānaṃ ya, ta, aita, ima, iti, aiva, iva, viya, ca, vāic‍cādīnaṃ añ‍ñapadānaṃ mahāvuttisuttaina laupau, vibhattīnañ‍ca laupai sati sarantānaṃ byañ‍janantānañ‍ca samāsapadānaṃ sayamaiva pakatibhāvau, idha pana ‘sahassa sauñ‍ñatthai’ti suttaina sahasaddassa sattaṃ, tatau syādyuppatti, savitak‍kau samādhi, savitak‍kā samādhayau, savitak‍kā pañ‍ñā, savitak‍kā pañ‍ñāyau, savitak‍kaṃ jhānaṃ, savitak‍kāni jhānāni ic‍cādinā sabbaliṅga, vibhatti, vacanaihi yaujaitvā payaugasiddhi vaiditabbā|

upamānapubbapadau yathā? kāyabyāmānaṃ samapamāṇattā nigraudhau iva parimaṇḍalau nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau, nigraudhau iva vā parimaṇḍalau yau hautīti sau nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau, rājakumārau, saṅkhau iva paṇḍarau saṅkhapaṇḍarau, kākau  iva sūrau kākasūrau, sattānaṃ pañ‍ñācakkhupaṭilābhakaraṇaina taisaṃ cakkhu viya bhūtauti cakkhubhūtau, laukuttaradhammapaṭilābhakaraṇaina taisaṃ dhammau viya bhūtauti dhammabhūtau, nic‍casaummahadayatāya brahmā viya bhūtauti brahmabhūtau, andhau viya bhūtau ayanti andhabhūtau ic‍cādi|

saṅkhyaubhayapadau yathā? dvai vā tayau vā pattā dvittippattā, idha vāsaddāyaiva añ‍ñapadāni nāma, aniyamabhūtau taisaṃ atthau añ‍ñapadatthau nāma| dvīhaṃ vā tīhaṃ vā dvīhatīhaṃ, cha vā pañ‍ca vā vācā chappañ‍cavācā| aivaṃ sattaṭṭhamāsā, aikayaujanadviyaujanāni ic‍cādi|

disantarāḷatthau yathā? dakkhiṇassā ca pubbassā ca yadantarāḷaṃ hauti sā dakkhiṇapubbā| aivaṃ pubbuttarā, pacchimuttarā, aparadakkhiṇā, mahāvuttinā pubbapadai rassattaṃ| dakkhiṇā ca sā pubbā cāti dakkhiṇapubbā ic‍cādinā kammadhārayaupi yuj‍jati|

byatihāralakkhaṇai ka॰ 328; rū॰ 352; nī॰ 708 –

354. tattha gahaitvā taina paharitvā yuddhai sarūpaṃ|

sattamyantaṃ tatiyantañ‍ca samānarūpaṃ syādyantapadaṃ tattha gahaitvā taina paharitvā yuddhai añ‍ñapadatthai aikatthaṃ hauti vā|

355. ṅi vītihārai ka॰ 404; rū॰ 370|

añ‍ñapadatthavisayai kriyābyatihārai gamyamānai padantai ṅānubandhau ipac‍cayau hauti, aittha ikārau rassau aiva|

356. ṅi smiṃca ka॰ 403; rū॰ 354|

vipac‍cayantai  uttarapadai parai pubbapadantassa āttaṃ hauti|

kaisaisu ca kaisaisu ca gahaitvā idaṃ yuddhaṃ pavattatīti kaisākaisi, daṇḍaihi ca daṇḍaihi ca paharitvā idaṃ yuddhaṃ pavattatīti daṇḍādaṇḍi| aivaṃ muṭṭhāmuṭṭhi, musalāmusali|

iti paṭhamābahubbīhi|

dutiyābahubbīhi

āgatā samaṇā imaṃ saṅghārāmaṃ sauyaṃ āgatasamaṇau, saṅghārāmau| aittha ca samāsapadassa atthau duvidhau vāc‍catthau, abhidhaiyyatthau cāti|

tattha saṅghārāmassa samaṇaihi pattabbabhāvasaṅkhātā kammasatti vāc‍catthau nāma, sattimantabhūtau saṅghārāmau abhidhaiyyatthau nāma|

tattha āgatasamaṇasaddau vāc‍catthamaiva ujuṃ vadati, na abhidhaiyyatthaṃ, āgatasamaṇauti sutvā samaṇaihi pattabbabhāvamattaṃ jānāti, saṅghārāmadabbaṃ na jānātīti vuttaṃ hauti, tasmā tassaṃ abhidhaiyyatthau añ‍ñaina saṅghārāmasaddaina ācikkhiyati, vāc‍catthassa pana taina ujuṃ vuttattā puna vattabbābhāvatau dutiyāvibhattiyā ācikkhanakic‍caṃ natthi, tasmā saṅghārāmapadai dutiyāvibhattisambhavau natthi, liṅgatthamattavisayā paṭhamāvibhatti aiva pavattati, puna padantarasambandhai sati ‘‘saṅghārāmaṃ passati āgatasamaṇaṃ, saṅghārāmaina gāmau saubhati āgatasamaṇaina, saṅghārāmassa pūjaiti āgatasamaṇassā’’tiādinā tatau sabbā vibhattiyau  pavattanti| aisa nayau sabbaisu vācakapadaisu naitabbauti|

āgatasamaṇā sāvatthi, āgatasamaṇaṃ jaitavanaṃ, āgacchanti samaṇā imanti vā āgatasamaṇau, vihārau| ārūḷhā vānarā imaṃ rukkhanti ārūḷhavānarau, rukkhau| sampattā gāmikā yaṃ gāmanti sampattagāmikau| aivaṃ paviṭṭhagāmikau ic‍cādi|

iti dutiyābahubbīhi|

tatiyābahubbīhi

jitāni indriyāni yaināti jitindriyau, samaṇau| diṭṭhau catusac‍cadhammau yaināti diṭṭhadhammau| aivaṃ pattadhammau, viditadhammau, pariyaugāḷhadhammau, katāni catumaggakic‍cāni yaināti katakic‍cau, bahuvacanai sati katāni kic‍cāni yaihi tai katakic‍cā, arahantau| dhammaina adhigatā bhaugā yaināti dhammādhigatabhaugau, purisau| aivaṃ adhammādhigatabhaugau| aivaṃ kattari| karaṇai pana chin‍nau rukkhau yaināti chin‍narukkhau, pharasu ic‍cādi|

iti tatiyābahubbīhi|

catutthībahubbīhi

din‍nau suṅkau yassa rañ‍ñau sauyaṃ din‍nasuṅkau, upanītaṃ bhaujanaṃ yassāti upanītabhaujanau, natthi tulau aitassāti atulau, ‘ṭa nañssā’ti na-kārassa ṭattaṃ, natthi paṭipuggalau yassāti appaṭipuggalau, natthi sīlaṃ assāti dussīlau, natthi paṭisandhipañ‍ñā assāti duppañ‍ñau, ‘ghapassantassāppadhānassā’ti suttaina ghasañ‍ñassa āssa  rassattaṃ| natthi sīlaṃ assāti nissīlau, nippañ‍ñau, apañ‍ñau, virūpaṃ mukhaṃ assāti dummukhau| aivaṃ dummanau, dubbaṇṇau, natthi attanau uttarau adhikau yassāti anuttarau, ‘ana sarai’ti suttaina nassa ana|

idha bāhiratthabahubbīhi nāma vuc‍cati, sattāhaṃ parinibbutassa assāti sattāhaparinibbutau, aciraṃ parinibbutassa assāti aciraparinibbutau, māsau jātassa assāti māsajātau, dvaimāsajātau, aikau māsau abhisittassa assa rañ‍ñauti aikamāsābhisittau, aikāhaṃ matassa assāti aikāhamataṃ| aivaṃ dvīhamataṃ, tīhamataṃ, aikāhaṃ paṭicchan‍nāya assāti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā| aivaṃ dvīhappaṭicchan‍nā, āpatti| yaujanaṃ gatassa assāti yaujanagatau, dviyaujanagatau ic‍cādi|

iti catutthībahubbīhi|

pañ‍camībahubbīhi

niggatā janā asmā gāmāti niggatajanau, apagataṃ kāḷakaṃ itauti apagatakāḷakau, paṭau| apagatakāḷakaṃ, vatthaṃ| apaitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ yamhāti apaitaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, matasarīraṃ ic‍cādi|

iti pañ‍camībahubbīhi|

chaṭṭhībahubbīhi

chin‍nau hatthau yassa sauti chin‍nahatthau, hatthacchin‍nau, jātau chandau yassāti jātachandau, chandajātau, sañ‍jātaṃ pītisaumanassaṃ yassāti sañ‍jātapītisaumanassau, pītisaumanassasañ‍jātau, visuddhaṃ sīlaṃ yassāti visuddhasīlau, sīlavisuddhau, mahantau kāyau yassāti mahākāyau|

idha upamānapubbapadau nāma vuc‍cati, suvaṇṇassa viya vaṇṇau yassāti suvaṇṇavaṇṇau, brahmunau viya sarau yassāti brahmassarau, nāgassa viya gati assāti nāgagati| aivaṃ sīhagati, nāgavik‍kamau, sīhavik‍kamau, sīhassa viya hanu assāti sīhahanu, aiṇissa viya jaṅghā yassāti aiṇijaṅghau, usabhassa viya assa khandhauti usabhakkhandhau ic‍cādi|

rūpaṃ vuc‍cati sabhāvau, yādisaṃ rūpaṃ assāti yathārūpaṃ| aivaṃ tathārūpaṃ, aivaṃ rūpaṃ assāti aivarūpaṃ, bindulaupau| aivaṃ ādi assāti aivamādi| tathā ic‍cādi, ic‍caivamādi, īdisaṃ nāmaṃ yassāti itthan‍nāmau, aivaṃnāmau, kīdisaṃ nāmaṃ yassāti kin‍nāmau, ‘kaunāmau’ti aittha mahāvuttinā kiṃsaddassa kauttaṃ|

kau samudayau yassa dhammassāti kiṃsamudayau, kā jāti yassāti kiṃjātikau, kiṃnidānaṃ yassāti kiṃnidānau, kati vassāni yassāti kativassau, kau atthau assāti kimatthaṃ, vacanaṃ| ‘k‍vatthau’ti mahāvuttinā kiṃsaddassa kauttaṃ, yādisau atthau assāti yadatthau, tādisau atthau assāti tadatthau, aidisau atthau yassa vinayassāti aitadatthau, vinayau| aitadatthā, vinayakathā| aitadatthaṃ, sautāvadhānaṃ ic‍cādi|

iti chaṭṭhībahubbīhi|

sattamībahubbīhi

sampan‍nāni sassāni yasmiṃ janapadai sauyaṃ sampan‍nasassau, sulabhā bhikkhā yasmiṃ janapadai sauyaṃ subhikkhau, dul‍labhā bhikkhā yasminti dubbhikkhau, bahavau gāmā asmiṃ janapadaiti bahugāmau| aivaṃ bahujanau, gāmau| natthi gāmakhaittaṃ yasmiṃ arañ‍ñai tayidaṃ agāmakaṃ, samāsantai kau| saṃvij‍janti manussā yasmiṃ gāmai samanussau, na vij‍janti manussā yasmiṃ gāmai amanussau ic‍cādi|

iti sattamībahubbīhi|

bhin‍nādhikaraṇabahubbīhi

bhin‍nādhikaraṇabahubbīhi nāma vuc‍cati, aikarattiṃ vāsau assāti aikarattivāsau, samānaina janaina saddhiṃ vāsau assāti samānavāsau, ubhatau kammatau uppan‍naṃ byañ‍janadvayaṃ assāti ubhataubyañ‍janau, aluttasamāsau| aivaṃ kaṇṭhasmiṃ kāḷau assāti kaṇṭhaikāḷau, urasmiṃ laumāni assāti urasilaumau, yassa hatthai pattau atthīti pattahatthau| aivaṃ asihatthau, daṇḍahatthau, chattaṃ pāṇimhi assāti chattapāṇi| aivaṃ satthapāṇi, daṇḍapāṇi, vajirapāṇi, dānai ajjhāsayau assāti dānajjhāsayau, dānādhimuttikau, buddhaisu bhatti assāti buddhabhattikau, buddhai gāravau assāti buddhagāravau, dhammagāravau ic‍cādi|

tipadabahubbīhi

tipadabahubbīhi nāma vuc‍cati, parak‍kamaina adhigatā sampadā yaihi tai parak‍kamādhigatasampadā, dhammaina adhigatā bhaugā yaihi tai dhammādhigatabhaugā, auṇītau pattamhā pāṇi yaina sau auṇītapattapāṇi, sīhassa pubbaddhaṃ viya kāyau assāti sīhapubbaddhakāyau, mattā bahavau mātaṅgā yasmiṃ vanaiti mattabahumātaṅgaṃ ic‍cādi|

bahubbīhisamāsau niṭṭhitau|

dvandasamāsa

atha  dvandasamāsau dīpiyatai|

dvai ca dvai ca padāni dvandā, dvai ca dvai ca atthā vā dvandā, mahāvuttinā dvin‍naṃ dvisaddānaṃ dvandādaisau| dvandasaddasadisattā sabbau cāyaṃ samāsau dvandauti vuc‍cati|

atha vā dvai avayavā andiyanti bandhiyanti aitthāti dvandau, yugaḷassaitaṃ nāmaṃ ‘‘pādadvandaṃ munindassa, vandāmi sirasāmaha’’nti aittha viya, idha pana padayugaḷaṃ atthayugaḷañ‍ca gayhati| ubhayapadatthapadhānau hi dvandau|

aittha siyā – yadi ubhayapadatthappadhānau dvandau, aivañ‍ca sati dvandai kathaṃ aikatthībhāvalakkhaṇaṃ siyāti? vuc‍catai – abhin‍navidhaiyyatthattā| vacanapathañhi patvā kattubhāvakammabhāvādikau vidhaiyyatthau aiva padānaṃ ac‍cantappadhānatthau hauti vacanavākyasampattiyā padhānaṅgattā, sau ca vidhaiyyatthau dvandaipi abhin‍nau aiva hauti| tathā hi ‘‘sāriputtamauggal‍lānā gacchanti, sāriputtamauggal‍lānai passati’’ ic‍cādīsu dvai atthā aikavibhattiyā visayā hutvā aikakattu, aikakammādibhāvaina aikattaṃ gacchanti, aivaṃ dvandaipi dvin‍naṃ tiṇṇaṃ bahun‍naṃ vā padānaṃ aikatthībhāvalakkhaṇaṃ labbhatiyaivāti|

357. catthai ka॰ 329; rū॰ 357; nī॰ 709|

anaikaṃ syādyantapadaṃ casaddassa atthai aikatthaṃ hauti vā|

aittha ca samuc‍cayau, anvācayau, itarītarayaugau, samāhārauti cattārau casaddatthā haunti|

tattha samuc‍cayau yathā? cīvarañ‍ca piṇḍapātañ‍ca saināsanañ‍ca daitīti| anvācayau yathā? dānañ‍ca daiti, sīlañ‍ca rakkhatīti| imai  dvai casaddatthā vākyadvandai aiva labbhanti, na samāsadvandai padānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nirapaikkhattāti vadanti| taṃ anvācayai yuj‍jati nānākriyāpaikkhattā, samuc‍cayai pana ‘‘cīvarañ‍ca piṇḍapātañ‍ca saināsanañ‍ca daitī’’ti vā ‘‘cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanāni daitī’’ti vā aivaṃ dvidhāpi yaujaituṃ yuj‍jatiyaiva ‘‘lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’’nti ma॰ ni॰ 1.65 pāḷidassanatau| anvācayaupi vā samāsadvandai nau na labbhati ‘‘mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā’’ti dī॰ ni॰ 1.10 pāḷidassanatau| aivaṃ pana yuj‍jaiyya – casaddatthau aikakriya, nānākriyāpaikkhanabhaidaina duvidhau hauti samuc‍cayau, anvācayau cāti, taisu ca aikaikau avayavappadhāna, samudāyappadhānabhaidaina duvidhau hauti itarītarayaugau, samāhārau cāti| tattha itarītarayaugai avayavappadhānattā sabbavibhattīsu bahuvacanamaiva yuj‍jati|

dvandai paṇītataraṃ pubbai nipatati| sāriputtau ca mauggal‍lānau ca sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, sāriputtamauggal‍lānai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaihi ic‍cādi| aivaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇā, brāhmaṇagahapatikā, khattiyabrāhmaṇā, daivamanussā, candimasūriyā|

appakkhara, bahvakkharaisu appakkharaṃ k‍vaci pubbaṃ hauti, gāmanigamā, gāmajanapadā ic‍cādi|

k‍vaci ivaṇṇu’vaṇṇantā pubbai haunti, aggi ca dhūmau ca aggidhūmā, rattidivā, dhātuliṅgāni|

avaṇṇantaisu sarādipadaṃ pubbaṃ hauti, atthau ca dhammau ca atthadhammā, dhammatthā vā ic‍cādi|

ayañ‍ca  niyamau dvipadadvandaisu yaibhuyyaina labbhati, bahupadadvandaisu na labbhati|

samāhāradvandai –

358. samāhārai napuṃsakaṃ ka॰ 322; rū॰ 359; nī॰ 700|

359. k‍vacaikattañ‍ca chaṭṭhiyā ka॰ 327; rū॰ 351; nī॰ 704; caṃ॰ 2.2.69-73; pā॰ 2.4.22-25|

pumbhāvātidaisarāsi

362. itthiyaṃ bhāsitapumitthī pumāvaikatthai ka॰ 331; rū॰ 353; nī॰ 714; caṃ॰ 5.2.29; pā॰ 6.3.34|

‘aikatthai’ti tulyādhikaraṇai, itthiyaṃ vattamānai aikatthai uttarapadai parai kadāci bhāsitapumau itthiliṅgasaddau pumā iva hauti| caturaṅgamidaṃ vidhānaṃ, pubbapadaṃ itthiliṅgañ‍ca bhāsitapumañ‍ca siyā, parapadaṃ niyatitthiliṅgañ‍ca pubbapadaina aikatthañ‍ca siyāti|

dīghā jaṅghā yassa sau dīghajaṅghau, purisau, dīghajaṅghā, itthī, dīghajaṅghaṃ, kulaṃ|

aittha ca yai saddā katthaci pul‍liṅgarūpā haunti, katthaci itthipac‍cayayuttā itthiliṅgarūpā, tai bhāsitapumā nāma| dīghau maggau, dīghā ratti, gatau purisau, gatā itthī, kumārau, kumārī, brāhmaṇau, brāhmaṇī ic‍cādi|

yai pana itthipac‍cayayuttā nic‍caṃ itthiliṅgarūpā haunti, tai bhāsitapumā nāma na haunti, kañ‍ñā, pañ‍ñā, saddhā, nadī, itthī, pathavī ic‍cādi| tathā sabhāvaitthiliṅgāpi niyatapun‍napuṃsakaliṅgāpi bhāsitapumā na haunti, daivatā, ratti, dhainu, vadhū, sak‍kau, daivau, brahmā, ratanaṃ, saraṇaṃ ic‍cādi|

idha pana dīghasaddau ‘‘dīghau bālāna saṃsārau’’ti dha॰ pa॰ 60 ādīsu bhāsitapumau, sau visaisyaliṅgānugatavasaina idha itthipac‍cayayuttau  itthiliṅgasaddau nāma| iminā suttaina pumbhāvātidaisai katai tattha āpac‍cayau antaradhāyati, ‘ghapassantassāppadhānassā’ti suttaina samāsantassa ākārassa rassattaṃ, kumārī bhariyā yassa sau kumārabhariyau, īpac‍cayanivatti| yuvati jāyā yassa sau yuvajāyau, tipac‍cayanivatti| brahmabandhū bhariyā yassa sau brahmabandhubhariyau, ūpac‍cayanivatti|

itthiyanti kiṃ? kumārī ratanaṃ yassa sau kumārīratanau, purisau, idha parapadaṃ itthiliṅgaṃ na hauti, tasmā pumbhāvātidaisau na kātabbau, yadi karaiyya, kumārau ratanaṃ yassa kumāraratanauti aivaṃ aniṭṭhatthau bhavaiyya|

aikatthaiti kiṃ? kumārīsu bhatti yassa sau kumārībhattikau| aivaṃ samaṇībhattikau, brāhmaṇībhattikau, samāsantai kau, idha parapadaṃ pubbapadaina aikatthaṃ na hauti, tasmā pumbhāvātidaisau na kātabbau, yadi karaiyya, kumāraisu bhatti yassa sau kumārabhattikauti aivaṃ aniṭṭhatthau bhavaiyya|

itthīti kiṃ? daṭṭhabbaṭṭhaina diṭṭhi, gāmaṇikulaṃ diṭṭhi yaina sau gāmaṇidiṭṭhi, idha gāmaṇisaddau bhāsitapumau hauti, idha pana kulavācakattā napuṃsakaliṅgai tiṭṭhati, itthipac‍cayau natthi, tasmā pumbhāvātidaisakic‍caṃ natthi|

bhāsitapumauti kiṃ? saddhā pakati yassa sau saddhāpakatikau| aivaṃ pañ‍ñāpakatikau, idha pubbapadaṃ niyatitthiliṅgattā bhāsitapumaṃ na hautīti| saddhādhanau, pañ‍ñādhanau, saddhādhurau pañ‍ñādhurau ic‍catra duvaṅgavaikal‍laṃ hauti|

kammadhārayamhi ka॰ 332; rū॰ 343; nī॰ 716 pana ‘aikatthai’ti padaṃ visuṃ aikaṃ aṅgaṃ na hauti anaikatthassa idha asambhavatau| dīghā ca sā jaṅghā cāti  dīghajaṅghā, kumārī ca sā bhariyā cāti kumārabhariyā| aivaṃ khattiyakañ‍ñā, brāhmaṇakañ‍ñā, yuvati ca sā bhariyā cāti yuvabhariyā, brahmabandhū ca sā bhariyā cāti brahmabandhubhariyā|

itthiyanti kiṃ? kumārī ca sā ratanañ‍cāti kumārīratanaṃ| aivaṃ samaṇīpadumaṃ|

itthīti kiṃ? gāmaṇikulañ‍ca taṃ diṭṭhi cāti gāmaṇidiṭṭhi|

bhāsitapumauti kiṃ? saddhāpakati, gaṅgānadī, taṇhānadī, pathavīdhātu|

sañ‍ñāsaddaisu pana caturaṅgayuttaipi vidhānaṃ na hauti, nandādaivī, nandāpaukkharaṇī, kāyagatāsati, paṭhamāvibhatti, dutiyāvibhatti, pañ‍camīvibhatti, chaṭṭhīvibhatti ic‍cādi|

363. k‍vaci pac‍cayai ka॰ 332; rū॰ 343; nī॰ 716; caṃ॰ 5.2.31; pā॰ 6.3.35|

pac‍cayai parai kadāci bhāsitapumau itthiliṅgasaddau k‍vaci pumāva hauti|

byattatarā, byattatamā, aittha ca byattānaṃ itthīnaṃ atisayaina byattāti byattatarā, byattatamāti atthau| aivaṃ paṇḍitatarā, paṇḍitatamā ic‍cādi|

364. sabbādayau vuttimattai ka॰ 331; rū॰ 353; nī॰ 714; caṃ॰ 5.2.41; pā॰ 6.3.35|

vuttimattai ṭhānai sabbādināmakā sabbanāmasaddā pumāva haunti|

sā pamukhā yassa sau tappamukhau| aivaṃ tappadhānau, tāya tāhi vā sampayuttau taṃsampayuttau| sā aiva pamukhā tappamukhā| aivaṃ tappadhānā, tassā mukhaṃ tammukhaṃ, tassaṃ gāthāyaṃ  tāsu gāthāsu vā tatra, tāya gāthatau tāhi vā gāthāhi tatau, tassaṃ vailāyaṃ tadā ic‍cādi|

aittha ca vutti nāma samāsa, taddhitā’ yādidhātupac‍cayanta, vibhattipac‍cayantānaṃ nāmaṃ|

iti pumbhāvātidaisarāsi|

365. ltitthiyūhi kau ka॰ 338; rū॰ 356; nī॰ 725; caṃ॰ 4.4.140; pā॰ 5.4.152|

añ‍ñapadatthavisayai kattuic‍cādīhi ltupac‍cayantaihi itthiyaṃ ī, ūkārantaihi ca bahulaṃ kapac‍cayau hauti|

bahavau kattārau yasmiṃ daisai sau bahukattukau| aivaṃ bahuvattukau, bahukā nadiyau yasmiṃ daisai sau bahunadikau| aivaṃ bahuitthikau, gāmau, bahuitthikā, sabhā, bahuitthikaṃ, kulaṃ| aivaṃ bahukumārikaṃ, bahubrahmabandhukau|

366. vāñ‍ñatau ka॰ 338; rū॰ 356; nī॰ 725; caṃ॰ 6.2.72; pā॰ 5.4.152|

añ‍ñapadatthavisayai ltitthiyūhi añ‍ñatau avaṇṇivaṇṇuvaṇṇantaihi bahulaṃ kapac‍cayau hauti vā|

368. pāpādīhi bhūmiyā ka॰ 337; rū॰ 350; nī॰ 722; caṃ॰ 4.4.72; …pai॰… 5.4.75; ‘gaudāvarīnaṃ’ (bahūsu)|

pāpānaṃ bhūmi pāpabhūmaṃ, pāpānaṃ uppattibhūmityatthau, jātiyā bhūmi jātibhūmaṃ, satthujātaraṭṭhaṃ| aivaṃ pacchābhūmaṃ, majjhimadaisai pacchābhāgaraṭṭhaṃ|

369. saṅkhyāhi ka॰ 337; rū॰ 350; nī॰ 722; caṃ॰ 4.4.73; pā॰ 5.4.75|

saṅkhyāhi parāya bhūmiyā samāsantau a hauti|

dvai bhūmiyau aitthāti dvibhūmau, dvibhūmakau, pāsādau, dvibhūmikau vā, tissau bhūmiyau aitaisanti vā tīsu bhūmisu pariyāpan‍nāti vā taibhūmakā, dhammā, catubhūmakā, dhammā, taibhūmikā, catubhūmikā vā| digumhi-dvai bhūmiyau dvibhūmaṃ, tissau bhūmiyau tibhūmaṃ, catassau bhūmiyau catubhūmaṃ ic‍cādi|

370. nadīgaudhāvarīnaṃ ka॰ 337; rū॰ 350; nī॰ 722; caṃ॰ 4.4.73; pā॰ 5.4.75|

saṅkhyāhi parāsaṃ nadī, gaudhāvarīnaṃ samāsantau a hauti|

pañ‍ca nadiyau pañ‍canadaṃ, pañ‍ca vā nadiyau yasmiṃ padaisai sau pañ‍canadau, satta gaudhāvariyau sattagaudhāvaraṃ|

371. asaṅkhyaihi caṅgulyānañ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthaisu ka॰ 337; rū॰ 350; nī॰ 722; caṃ॰ 4.4.74; pā॰ 5.4.86|

añ‍ñatthau ca asaṅkhyatthau ca añ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthā| tattha ‘añ‍ñatthau’ti añ‍ñapadatthau bahubbīhisamāsau, ‘asaṅkhyatthau’ti asaṅkhyatthasamāsau  abyayībhāvasamāsauti vuttaṃ hauti, na añ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthā anañ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthā, añ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthavaj‍jitaisu samāsaisu asaṅkhyaihi upasaggaihi ca saṅkhyāhi ca parāya aṅguliyā samāsantau a hauti| casaddaina ‘‘sugataṅgulaina, pamāṇaṅgulaina’’ ic‍cādīni sijjhanti|

aṅgulīhi niggataṃ niraṅgulaṃ, aṅguliyau atik‍kantaṃ ac‍caṅgulaṃ, imai dvai amādisamāsā, dvai aṅguliyau samāhaṭāti dvaṅgulaṃ|

anañ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthaisūti kiṃ? pañ‍ca aṅguliyau asminti pañ‍caṅguli, hatthau| aṅguliyā samīpaṃ upaṅguli| ‘‘caturaṅgulaṃ kaṇṇaṃ ausāraitvā mahāva॰ 66, aṭṭhaṅgulaṃ dantakaṭṭhaṃ, dvaṅgulaparamaṃ, caturaṅgulaparamaṃ, aṭṭhaṅgulaparama’’ntiādīsu ‘aṅgula’nti akārantaṃ pamāṇavācīsaddantaraṃ|

372. dārumhaṅgulyā ka॰ 337; rū॰ 350; nī॰ 722; caṃ॰ 4.4.97; pā॰ 5.4.114; ‘dārumyaṅgulyā’ (bahūsu)|

dārusaṅkhātai añ‍ñapadatthai pavattāya aṅguliyā samāsantau ahauti|

pañ‍ca aṅguliyau assāti pañ‍caṅgulaṃ, dāru| aittha ca aṅgulipamāṇāvayavau dhañ‍ñādīnaṃ mānavisaisau ‘dārū’ti vuc‍cati|

373. dīghāhauvassaikadaisaihi ca ratyā ka॰ 337; rū॰ 350; nī॰ 722; caṃ॰ 4.4.75; pā॰ 5.4.87|

dīghau ca ahau ca vassau ca aikadaisau cāti dvandau, aikadaisau nāma pubba, parādi, anañ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthaisu dīghādīhi ca asaṅkhyaihi ca saṅkhyāhi ca parāya rattiyā samāsantau a hauti| casaddaina ‘‘ciraratta’’nti sijjhati|

dīghā  rattiyau dīgharattaṃ, dīghā rattidivaparaṃparātyatthau| ahau ca ratti ca ahaurattaṃ, vassaina taimitā ratti vassarattaṃ, rattiyā pubbaṃ pubbarattaṃ, rattiyā paraṃ pararattaṃ, rattiyā aḍḍhaṃ aḍḍharattaṃ, rattiṃ atik‍kantau atirattau, dvai rattiyau dvirattaṃ| aivaṃ tirattaṃ, caturattaṃ, pañ‍carattaṃ, chārattaṃ, vādhikārattā ‘‘aikarattaṃ, aikarattī’’ti sijjhati|

anañ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthaisūti kiṃ? dīghā ratti aitthāti dīgharatti, haimantau| rattiyā samīpaṃ uparatti|

374. gau tvacatthai cālaupai ka॰ 337; rū॰ 350; nī॰ 722; caṃ॰ 4.4.77; pā॰ 5.4.92|

acatthai ca anañ‍ñā’saṅkhyatthaisu ca pavattā gausaddamhā alaupaṭṭhānai samāsantau a hauti|

rañ‍ñau gau rājagavau, attanau gau sagavau, paraisaṃ gau paragavau, pañ‍cagavau, pañ‍cagavaṃ| aivaṃ dasagavaṃ|

alaupaiti kiṃ? pañ‍cahi gauhi kītau pañ‍cagu| aittha ca taina kītauti aitasmiṃ atthai taddhitapac‍cayassa laupau, taina ayaṃ apac‍cayau na hauti, ‘gaussū’ti aussuttaṃ|

acatthaiti kiṃ? gavajā ca gāvau ca gavajagavau, yaumhi gaussa gavattaṃ|

anañ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthaisūti kiṃ? cittagu, upagu|

375. rattidiva dāragava caturassā ka॰ 337; rū॰ 350; nī॰ 722; caṃ॰ 4.4.62; pā॰ 5.4.77|

aitai tayau saddā aantā nipac‍cantai|

ratti ca divā ca rattidivaṃ, dārā ca gāvau ca dāragavaṃ, catassau assiyau yassa sau caturassau, apac‍cayau, assissa issa attaṃ|

376. āyāmainugavaṃ ka॰ 337; rū॰ 350; nī॰ 722; caṃ॰ 4.4.69; pā॰ 5.4.83|

gauhi anuṭṭhitaṃ sakaṭaṃ anugavaṃ|

āyāmaiti kiṃ? gun‍naṃ pacchā anugu|

377. akkhismāñ‍ñatthai ka॰ 337; rū॰ 350; nī॰ 722; caṃ॰ 4.4.96; pā॰ 5.4.113|

añ‍ñapadatthai pavattā akkhimhā samāsantau a hauti|

visālāni akkhīni yassa sau visālakkhau, virūpāni akkhīni yassa sau virūpakkhau, anaikasahassāni akkhīni yassa sau sahassakkhau, lauhitāni akkhīni yassa sau lauhitakkhau| aivaṃ nīlakkhau, nīlakkhi vā| bahulādhikārā anañ‍ñatthaipi, akkhīnaṃ paṭimukhaṃ pac‍cakkhaṃ, akkhīnaṃ parabhāgau paraukkhaṃ, akkhīnaṃ tiraubhāgau tiraukkhaṃ| akkhisaddaina caittha pañ‍cindriyāni gayhanti|

mahāvuttinā k‍vaci samāsantau a, ā, ipac‍cayā haunti|

tattha apac‍cayai –

vāyunau sakhā vāyusakhau, vāyusaṅkhātau sakhā assāti vā vāyusakhau, aggi, sabbaisaṃ piyā’piyamajjhattānaṃ sakhāti sabbasakhau, sabbai vā sakhāyau assāti sabbasakhau, maittāvihārī| ‘‘sabbamittau sabbasakhau’’ti thairagā॰ 648 pāḷi| pāpānaṃ sakhāti pāpasakhau, pāpā sakhārau yassāti vā pāpasakhau| ‘‘pāpamittau pāpasakhau’’ti dī॰ ni॰ 3.253 pāḷi| pahitau paisitau attā yaināti pahitattau, majjhimau attā sabhāvau yassāti majjhattau, chātaṃ ajjhattasantānaṃ saṇṭhānaṃ (mūlapāṭhai) yassāti chātajjhattau, suhitau attā yassāti suhitattau, yatau saṃyatau attā yassāti yatattau, ṭhitau attā assāti ṭhitattauic‍cādi|

āpac‍cayai –

pac‍cakkhau dhammau yassāti pac‍cakkhadhammā, chādaitīti chadau, mauhau, vivaṭau chadau yasminti vivaṭacchadā, sammāsambuddhau|

ipac‍cayai –

sundarau gandhau yassāti sugandhi, kucchitau gandhau yassāti duggandhi, pūtinau gandhau yassāti pūtigandhi, surabhinau gandhau surabhigandhi| ‘‘sarīrassa sugandhinau, guṇagandhiyuttau aha’’nti pāḷi|

iti samāsantaapac‍cayarāsi|

nānādaisarāsi

378. uttarapadai ka॰ 333-334; rū॰ 344-345; nī॰ 717-718|

uttarapadai parai pubbapadai vidhi hautīti atthau| adhikārasuttamidaṃ|

379. nakhādayau ka॰ 328; rū॰ 352; nī॰ 708; caṃ॰ 5.2.95; pā॰ 6.3.75|

nakhādayau saddā napakatikā sijjhanti|

nā’ssa khamatthīti nakhau, ‘kha’nti sukhaṃ, dukkhañ‍ca, nā’ssa kulamatthīti nakulau, aivaṃnāmakau brāhmaṇau, pumassa sakaṃ puṃsakaṃ  natthi puṃsakaṃ aitasminti napuṃsakau, khañ‍janaṃ vaikal‍lagamanaṃ khattaṃ, natthi khattaṃ aitassāti nakkhattaṃ, kaṃ vuc‍cati sukhaṃ, tabbiruddhattā akaṃ vuc‍cati dukkhaṃ, natthi akaṃ aitasminti nākau, saggau, na muñ‍catīti namuci, mārau| na galati na cavatīti nagaraṃ, gaihai vattabbai ‘agāra’nti sijjhati|

380. nagau vā pāṇini ka॰ 333-334; rū॰ 344-345; nī॰ 717-718; caṃ॰ 5.2.96; pā॰ 6.3.77; ‘nagau vāppāṇini’ (bahūsu)|

apāṇimhi nagauti sijjhati vā|

na gacchantīti nagā, rukkhā| nagā, pabbatā| agā, rukkhā, agā, pabbatā|

apāṇinīti kiṃ? agau vasalau kiṃ taina| aittha ‘agau’ti duggatajanau, ‘vasalau’ti lāmakau, ‘kiṃ tainā’ti nindāvacanaṃ, ‘‘sītainā’’tipi pāṭhau| aivaṃ naikai, anaikai, naikāni, anaikāni ic‍cādi|

iti na-rāsi|

381. sahassa sauñ‍ñatthai ka॰ 404; rū॰ 370; nī॰ 859; caṃ॰ 5.2.97; pā॰ 6.3.82|

puttaina saha yau vattatīti saputtau, sahaputtau|

382. sañ‍ñāyaṃ ka॰ 404; rū॰ 370; nī॰ 859; caṃ॰ 5.2.98; pā॰ 6.3.78|

383. apac‍cakkhai ka॰ 404; rū॰ 307; nī॰ 859; caṃ॰ 5.2.99; pā॰ 6.3.80|

apac‍cakkhai gamyamānai uttarapadai parai sahassa sau hauti|

auḍḍiyati aitāyāti auḍḍi, pāsau| auḍḍiyā saha yau vattatīti sauḍḍi, kapautau| idha ‘auḍḍi’ apac‍cakkhā| ‘‘sāggi kapautau’’tipi pāṭhau, picunā saha vattatīti sapicukā, vātamaṇḍalikā, sā ca apac‍cakkhā, uggantvā ākāsai paribbhamantaṃ picusaṅghāṭaṃ disvā ñātabbā| ‘‘sapisācā vātamaṇḍalikā’’tipi pāṭhau| aivaṃ sarajā, vātā, sarakkhasī, ratti|

384. akālai sakatthassa ka॰ 404; rū॰ 370; nī॰ 859; caṃ॰ 5.2.110; pā॰ 6.3.81|

385. ganthantādhikyai ka॰ 404; rū॰ 370; nī॰ 859; caṃ॰ 5.2.101; pā॰ 6.3.79|

ganthassa antau ganthantau| yathā taṃ kac‍cāyanaganthassa antau uṇādikappau, adhikabhāvau ādhikyaṃ, ganthantai ca ādhikyai ca vattamānassa sahasaddassa sau hauti uttarapadai parai|

saha  uṇādinā’ yaṃ adhiyataiti taṃ sauṇādi, sakalaṃ kac‍cāyanaṃ adhītaityatthau| saha muhuttaina sakalaṃ jautiṃ adhītai samuhuttaṃ, jautīti nakkhattasatthaṃ|

ādhikyai – sadauṇā, khārī, sakahāpaṇaṃ, nikkhaṃ, samāsakaṃ, kahāpaṇaṃ| nic‍catthamidaṃ suttaṃ|

386. samānassa pakkhādīsu vā ka॰ 404; rū॰ 370; nī॰ 859; caṃ॰ 5.2.103-4; pā॰ 6.3.84-86|

pakkhādīsu uttarapadaisu samānassa sau hauti vā|

samānau pakkhau sahāyau sapakkhau, samānau pakkhau yassāti vā sapakkhau, samānapakkhau vā| aivaṃ sajāti, samānajāti, sajanapadau, saratti|

samānau pati yassā sā sapati| aivaṃ sanābhi, sabandhu, sabrahmacārī, sanāmau| avhayaṃ vuc‍cati nāmaṃ, candaina samānaṃ avhayaṃ yassa sau candasavhayau, sagauttau, indaina samānaṃ gauttaṃ yassa sau indasagauttau, sarūpaṃ, saṭṭhānaṃ| hari vuc‍cati suvaṇṇaṃ, harinā samānau vaṇṇau yassa sau harissavaṇṇau, sassa dvittaṃ| aivaṃ siṅgīnikkhasavaṇṇau, savayau, sadhanau, sadhammau, sajātiyau|

pakkhādīsūti kiṃ? samānasīlau|

387. udarai iyai ka॰ 404; rū॰ 370; nī॰ 859; caṃ॰ 5.2.105; pā॰ 6.3.88|

iyayuttai udarai parai samānassa sau hauti vā|

samānai udarai jātau saudariyau, samānaudariyau|

iyaiti kiṃ? samānaudaratā|

añ‍ñaisupi  samānassa sau hauti, candaina samānā sirī yassa taṃ candassasirīkaṃ, mukhaṃ| aivaṃ padumassasirīkaṃ, vadanaṃ|

mahāvuttinā santādīnañ‍ca sau hauti, saṃvij‍jati laumaṃ assāti salaumakau| aivaṃ sapakkhakau, saṃvij‍janti āsavā aitaisanti sāsavā, saṃvij‍janti pac‍cayā aitaisanti sappac‍cayā, saṃvij‍janti attanau uttaritarā dhammā aitaisanti sauttarā, santau purisau sappurisau| tathā saj‍janau, saddhammau, santassa bhāvau sabbhāvau ic‍cādi|

iti sa-rāsi|

388. imassidaṃ ka॰ 129; rū॰ 222; nī॰ 305|

uttarapadai parai imassa idaṃ hauti|

ayaṃ atthau aitassāti idamatthī, samāsantai ī, idamatthinau bhāvau idamatthitā| ayaṃ pac‍cayau aitaisanti idappac‍cayā, idappac‍cayānaṃ bhāvau idappac‍cayatā| ‘‘imaisaṃ pac‍cayā idappac‍cayā, idappac‍cayā aiva idappac‍cayatā’’tipi yaujainti| ‘ida’nti nipātapadampi atthi, ‘‘rūpañ‍ca hidaṃ bhikkhavai attā abhavissā, vaidanā ca hidaṃ| sañ‍ñā ca hidaṃ| saṅkhārā ca hidaṃ bhikkhavai attā abhavissaṃsu’’ ic‍cādi mahāva॰ 20|

389. puṃ pumassa vā ka॰ 82; rū॰ 149|

390. ṭa ntantūnaṃ ka॰ 126; rū॰ 101; nī॰ 301|

uttarapadai  parai nta, ntūnaṃ ṭa hauti vā k‍vaci|

bhavaṃ patiṭṭhau yaisaṃ tai bhavaṃpatiṭṭhā, bindāgamau| bhagavā mūlaṃ yaisaṃ tai bhagavaṃmūlakā, dhammā| aivaṃ bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā, dhammā|

bahulādhikārā tarādīsu ca paraisu, mahantīnaṃ atisayaina mahāti mahattarī, rattañ‍ñūnaṃ mahantassa bhāvau rattañ‍ñumahattaṃ| aivaṃ jātimahattaṃ, guṇamahattaṃ, puñ‍ñamahattaṃ, arahantassa bhāvau arahattaṃ|

392. rīrikkhakaisu ka॰ 642; rū॰ 589; nī॰ 1269; caṃ॰ 5.2.107; pā॰ 6.3.89-90|

nic‍casamāsattā añ‍ñapadaina viggahau, saṃvij‍jatīti samānau, pac‍cakkhai viya cittai upalabbhatīti atthau| samānau viya sau dissatīti sadī, sadikkhau, sadisau, samānā viya tai dissantīti sadisā|

393. ntakimimānaṃ ṭākīṭī ka॰ 126; rū॰ 101; nī॰ 301|

396. tumhamhānaṃ tāmaikasmiṃ ka॰ 642; rū॰ 589; nī॰ 1269|

taisu paraisu aikavacanai pavattānaṃ tumha’ mhasaddānaṃ tā, mā haunti vā|

ahaṃ viya sau dissatīti mādī, mādikkhau, mādisau, tvaṃ viya sau dissatīti tādī, tādikkhau, tādisau|

aikasminti kiṃ? amhai viya tai dissantīti amhādinau, amhādikkhā, amhādisā, tumhai viya tai dissantīti tumhādinau, tumhādikkhā, tumhādisā| aittha ca upamānatthassaiva aikattaṃ icchīyati, tasmā ahaṃ viya tai dissantīti mādisā  tvaṃ viya tai dissantīti tādinau, tādisātipi yuj‍janti| ‘‘mādisā vai jinā haunti, yai pattā āsavakkhaya’’nti mahāva॰ 11 pāḷi, imāni padāni upari kitakaṇḍaipi āgamissanti|

397. taṃmamañ‍ñatra ka॰ 143; rū॰ 235; nī॰ 322|

rī, rikkha, kapac‍cayaihi añ‍ñasmiṃ uttarapadai parai tumha’mhānaṃ taṃ, maṃādaisā haunti k‍vaci|

tvaṃ laiṇaṃ yaisaṃ tai taṃlaiṇā, ahaṃ laiṇaṃ yaisaṃ tai maṃlaiṇā saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.359| aivaṃ taṃdīpā, maṃdīpā saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.359, taṃpaṭisaraṇā, maṃpaṭisaraṇā|

398. manādyāpādīnamau mayai ca ka॰ 183; rū॰ 386; nī॰ 375|

manausaiṭṭhā, manaumayā, rajaujal‍laṃ, rajaumayaṃ, sabbau manaugaṇau idha vattabbau, āpaudhātu, āpaumayaṃ| anuyanti disaudisaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 3.281, jīva tvaṃ saradausataṃ jā॰ 1.2.9|

400. janai puthassu ka॰ 49; rū॰ 44; nī॰ 129|

ariyaihi puthagaivāyaṃ janauti puthuj‍janau| api ca pāḷinayai puthusaddauyaiva bahulaṃ dissati, puthu kilaisai janaintīti puthuj‍janā, puthu nānāsatthārānaṃ mukhaṃ ul‍laukaintīti puthuj‍janā, puthu nānāaughaihi vuyhantīti puthuj‍janā mahāni॰ 51, 94, sañ‍ñānānāttaputhuttapabhaidaṃ paṭic‍ca taṇhānānāttaputhuttapabhaidau hauti dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ 1, iṅgha añ‍ñaipi pucchassu, puthū samaṇabrāhmaṇai saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.246| gāmā gāmaṃ vicarissaṃ, sāvakai vinayaṃ puthū, āyatāni puthūni ca, puthunā vij‍juvaṇṇinā jā॰ 2.22.968, puthukāyā puthubhūtā ic‍cādi| tasmā ‘‘puthagaiva, puthak‍karaṇai’’ ic‍cādīsu thussa ukārassa akārau akārauti? yuj‍jati|

405. jāyāya jāyaṃ patimhi ka॰ 339; rū॰ 358; nī॰ 731; ‘…jayaṃ patimhi’ (bahūsu)|

puttaṃ janaitīti jāyā, jāyā ca pati ca jāyampatī jayampatī (bahūsu)| atha vā ‘‘jāyampatī’’ti idaṃ sandhividhināva siddhaṃ, tasmā ‘‘jampatī’’tipāṭhau siyā yathā ‘‘daivarājā sujampatī’ saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.264’ti, yathā ca sak‍kataganthaisu ‘‘dārau ca pati ca dampatī’’ti| idha pana mahāvuttinā patimhi sujātāya sujaṃ hauti, dārassa ca daṃ hauti, tathā jāyā ca pati ca jampatīti niṭṭhaṃ gantabbaṃ|

yañ‍ca vuttiyaṃ ‘‘jānipatīti pakatyantaraina siddhaṃ, tathā dampatī’’ti vuttaṃ| tattha puttaṃ janaitīti jānī| jānī ca pati ca jānīpatīti yuj‍jati| ‘‘tudampatī’’ti pāṭhau| kac‍cāyanai ca ‘jāyāya tu daṃjāni patimhī’ti| tattha tusaddau padapūraṇamattai yuj‍jati|

413. añ‍ñai ca ka॰ 324, 327; rū॰ 339, 351; nī॰ 682-688, caṃ॰ 2.2.30, 33, 34, 37, 44; pā 1.4.67, 71, 72, 75, 76; 3.4.63|

kaici pana ‘‘sacchikarauti, manasikarauti, pākaṭīkarauti, āvīkarauti, pātukarauti, alaṅkarauti, sak‍karauti, pabhavati, parābhavati’’ ic‍cādīnampi aikatthībhāvaṃ vadanti| tumantatvantādikāpi aittha saṅgayhanti, gantuṃ kāmaitīti gantukāmau, kattukāmau, daṭṭhukāmau, gantuṃ manau aitassāti gantumanau, saṃvidhāya avahārau saṃvidhāvahārau, yalaupau| aivaṃ upādāya uppan‍naṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ, anupādāya vimuttau anupādāvimuttau, paṭic‍casamuppādau, āhac‍cabhāsitau, upahac‍caparinibbāyī pu॰ pa॰ 37, avaic‍cappasādau, chakkhattuparamaṃ, sattakkhattuparamau pu॰ pa॰ 31 ic‍cādi|
